# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [37] [Source-to-Source Transformations for GPU Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13864)
*Julien de Castelnau,Thomas Koehler,Arthur Charguéraud,Clément Pit-Claudel*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出OptiGPU，一个通过证明保留编译技术实现GPU安全编程（数据竞争自由、死锁自由、功能正确性）的系统，允许用户通过复现简单CPU程序的证明来验证优化后的复杂GPU代码，无需直接证明复杂GPU程序的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU编程中的并发bug难以检测，现有安全GPU编程方法在保证强度、底层控制程度和额外工作开销三个维度上存在权衡，缺乏能同时兼顾三者优势的方案。

Method: OptiGPU采用证明保留编译（proof-preserving compilation），通过源到源转换（source-to-source transformations）将已验证的简单CPU程序精化（refinement）为优化的GPU程序，自动保留证明。基于OptiTrust框架扩展，支持内核启动、共享内存、同步屏障等GPU特性，生成设备端和主机端代码。

Result: 在矩阵转置和树形并行归约两个案例中，OptiGPU能够导出与手写参考实现相匹配的CUDA代码。

Conclusion: OptiGPU在提供强安全保证（数据竞争自由、死锁自由、全功能正确性）的同时，相比现有方法最小化了底层控制能力和额外证明工作方面的权衡，验证了证明保留编译在GPU编程中的可行性。

Abstract: GPUs have become essential in modern high performance computing, but programming them correctly remains a significant challenge. This difficulty arises from subtle concurrency bugs that result from the explicit management of synchronization primitives and data movement across intricate hierarchies of memory and parallel threads. At the same time, the ability to control these aspects explicitly is at the core of the performance gains granted by GPUs. These challenges have motivated interest in safe GPU programming: tools and languages that can prevent or detect such bugs statically. However, existing approaches make tradeoffs in three dimensions: the strength of their guarantees, the degree of low-level control they allow, and the amount of additional effort required to achieve these safety guarantees. This thesis presents OptiGPU, a system for GPU programming with strong safety guarantees-data race freedom, deadlock freedom, and full functional correctness-that minimizes tradeoffs in the other two dimensions compared to previous approaches. OptiGPU applies proof-preserving compilation to GPU programming, allowing verification of low-level, optimized GPU programs through refinement of simple, verified CPU programs. An OptiGPU user thus avoids the substantial proof burden of directly verifying complex optimized GPU programs, instead directing this refinement with source-to-source transformations that automatically preserve proofs. OptiGPU is implemented as a set of extensions to OptiTrust, an existing framework for proof-preserving compilation on CPUs. OptiGPU models essential GPU programming features, including kernel launches, shared memory, and synchronous barriers, and produces both device and host-side code. We evaluate OptiGPU on two case studies, matrix transpose and tree-based parallel reduction, showing it can derive CUDA code matching techniques found in handwritten references.

</details>


### [38] [Quantitative Symbolic Patch Impact Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13885)
*Laboni Sarker,Abdus Satter,Tevfik Bultan*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出**定量部分等价分析**方法，通过符号分析量化补丁前后程序在输入域上的行为差异，使用基于范围的搜索启发式算法高效计算等价下界。在90个CVE补丁和Juliet测试集上验证了该方法能有效刻画和量化补丁影响，并在EqBench基准中发现了5个误标为等价的程序对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统等价性检查仅输出"等价/不等价"的二值结果，无法满足补丁影响分析等任务的需求——在这些任务中，补丁版本预期与原版本不等价，但需要进一步了解它们**在什么条件下表现不同**以及**受影响的输入占比**。这是一个尚未解决的公开挑战。

Method: 1）提出**定量部分等价分析**框架，通过符号分析识别使补丁前后程序行为相同或不同的输入条件；2）将不等价概念细化为**输入域上行为差异的程度度量**；3）针对数值域的高效量化分析，提出**基于范围的搜索启发式算法**，提供等价的可靠下界。

Result: 在90个来自Linux、Qemu、FFmpeg等知名开源项目的CVE补丁以及基于Juliet Test Suite的CWE数据集上验证，该方法能有效刻画和量化补丁影响。此外，在EqBench基准测试中发现**5个C程序对被误标为等价**，并识别出它们行为分歧的具体输入条件。

Conclusion: 定量部分等价分析能够超越传统的二值等价性判断，为软件补丁评估提供更细粒度的行为差异量化信息，是补丁影响分析的有效工具。

Abstract: Traditional equivalence checking classifies programs as equivalent or non-equivalent, providing insufficient information for tasks like patch impact analysis where it is expected the patched version of the program to be non-equivalent to the original program. When two program versions are non-equivalent, determining under what conditions they differ and what percentage of inputs are affected remains an open challenge. In this work, we introduce quantitative partial equivalence analysis, an approach for assessing software patches by quantifying behavioral differences between the original (vulnerable) code and the patched code. Using symbolic analysis, we identify input conditions under which patched and original programs exhibit identical or divergent behaviors. Our approach refines non-equivalence by measuring the extent of behavioral divergence across the input domain. For efficient quantitative analysis of numerical domains, we propose a range-based search heuristic that provides a sound lower bound on equivalence. We demonstrate our approach on 90 CVE patches from widely used open-source projects (Linux, Qemu, FFmpeg), as well as on a Juliet Test Suite-based dataset containing programs with CWEs. Our results show that quantitative partial equivalence analysis effectively characterizes and quantifies patch impact. Additionally, experiments on the EqBench benchmark reveal five C program pairs that are mislabeled as equivalent, and we identify the input conditions under which their behaviors diverge.

</details>


### [39] [Linear-Time T-Gate Optimization via Random Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13929)
*Aws Albarghouthi*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种线性时间的随机化相位折叠算法(TZAP)，用于大规模量子电路中的T门优化。该算法通过新颖的随机化静态分析（传播定宽比特串而非符号表达式）来近似可达量子态集合，在数秒内即可优化包含数百万门的电路，速度比现有工具快数个数量级，同时保持相当的T门减少效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子优势需要容错计算，而T门在大多数容错方案中成本极高（需通过魔态蒸馏实现），T门数量成为大规模量子计算资源的瓶颈。现有T门优化工具无法扩展到量子优势所需的电路规模。

Method: 提出一种线性时间随机化相位折叠算法。核心创新在于随机化静态分析：不追踪符号表达式，而是在电路中传播恒定宽度的比特串，以任意高概率可靠地近似可达量子态集合。

Result: 实现工具TZAP比现有最先进工具（PyZX、VOQC、Feynman）快数个数量级，在标准基准测试上接近这些工具的T门缩减效果，能在笔记本电脑上数秒内优化包含数百万门的电路。

Conclusion: 提出的随机化静态分析方法有效解决了T门优化在大规模电路上的可扩展性问题，为通往量子优势提供了实用的编译优化工具。

Abstract: Quantum computers promise exponential speedups for problems in cryptography, chemistry, and optimization. Realizing this promise requires fault tolerance: physical qubits are noisy, so logical qubits must be encoded redundantly across many physical ones using quantum error-correcting codes. In most practical fault-tolerance schemes, T gates cannot be implemented transversally and instead require costly magic-state distillation protocols involving a complex set of operations. As a result, T-gate count can dominate the resource budget of large-scale quantum computations, making T-count minimization a central bottleneck on the path to quantum advantage. Existing T-count optimization tools, however, do not scale to the circuits that quantum advantage demands.
  We present theoretical and practical results on T-gate optimization. On the theoretical side, we give a linear-time randomized algorithm for phase folding, based on a novel randomized static analysis. Our static analysis soundly approximates the set of reachable quantum states with an arbitrarily high probability. Our key insight is a static analysis that does not track symbolic expressions, but propagates constant-width bitstrings down the circuit. On the practical side, our implementation, TZAP, is multiple orders of magnitude faster than state-of-the-art tools -- such as PyZX, VOQC, and Feynman -- closely matches their T-count reductions on standard benchmarks, and within seconds on a laptop computer can optimize circuits with millions of gates.

</details>


### [40] [Mat2Boundary: Treating User-Defined Boundary Condition as SpMV for Distributed PDE Solvers on Block-Structured Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14780)
*Yanzheng Cai,Mingzhe Zhang,Shengqi Chen,Haoyuan Song,Wenguang Chen*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: Mat2Boundary是一个用于边界条件计算的DSL和编译器，将边界条件建模为仿射稀疏线性算子，通过多阶段编程和多面体分析生成高效内核，在浅水方程求解器和HPCG上实现最高7.6倍加速、减少70%以上代码量，并扩展到1344个CPU核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构化/块结构化网格的PDE求解器中，边界条件处理是主要复杂度来源，尤其对于高阶方法和分布式内存执行而言。

Method: 将多种边界条件建模为仿射稀疏线性算子，暴露模块化的基本子矩阵接口用于声明式组合；结合多阶段编程和多面体分析，为结构化情况生成无矩阵内核，支持用户自定义稀疏矩阵处理不规则情况，消除冗余边界工作，合成分布式执行的可复用通信调度。

Result: 在立方球网格上的两个浅水方程求解器和HPCG上，实现最高7.6倍边界条件内核加速，减少70%以上边界代码，扩展到1344个CPU核心并达到72%-88%的效率。

Conclusion: Mat2Boundary通过抽象边界条件为稀疏线性算子并配合编译优化，显著降低了边界条件处理的复杂性和代码量，同时提升了性能和可扩展性。

Abstract: Boundary-condition (BC) handling is a major source of complexity in PDE solvers on structured and block-structured grids, especially for high-order methods and distributed-memory execution. We present Mat2Boundary, a DSL and compiler for boundary computations that models a broad class of boundary-conditions as affine sparse linear operators. This abstraction unifies halo copying, circular and symmetric mappings, zero padding, block-edge synchronization, and user-defined interpolation, while exposing a modular basic sub-matrix interface for declarative composition. To make this representation efficient, Mat2Boundary combines multi-stage programming and polyhedral analysis to generate matrix-free kernels for structured cases, support user-defined sparse matrices for irregular cases, eliminate redundant boundary work, and synthesize reusable communication schedules for distributed execution. Evaluated on two shallow-water equation solvers on cubed-sphere grids and HPCG, Mat2Boundary achieves up to 7.6$\times$ BC-kernel speedup, reduces BC code by over 70%, and scales to 1,344 CPU cores with 72%-88% efficiency.

</details>

### [41] [Real-Time Group Dynamics with LLM Facilitation: Evidence from a Charity Allocation Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14097)
*Aaron Parisi,Nithum Thain,Alden Hallak,Vivian Tsai,Crystal Qian*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 两项实证研究（N=879）表明，LLM作为群体 deliberation 的促进者并未显著提升共识或参与公平性，但参与者更偏好有AI协助的讨论；同时发现LLM存在算法导向（最高可改变5.5个百分点的慈善分配）和包容性幻觉两大治理风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM从单用户助手演变为参与公民和职场 deliberation 的积极角色，评估其对集体决策的影响成为一个治理挑战。

Method: 两项真实经济激励（7,200美元）的慈善分配任务实验，采用实时文本群体讨论。研究1（N=204）比较三种前沿模型；研究2（N=675）比较不同的促进策略与无促进基线。

Result: LLM促进未显著提升群体共识；参与者更偏好有促进的讨论；LLM促进者改变了特定慈善分配（最高5.5个百分点）；参与公平性未提升；参与者在AI施加方向性影响的条件下反而对过程更信任。

Conclusion: AI中介的群体 deliberation 中，感知的程序改善可能与可测量的导向和未改变的参与不平等共存，需将集体结果、互动动态和参与者感知视为独立的治理目标进行评估。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) evolve from single-user assistants to active participants in civic and workplace deliberation, evaluating their effects on collective decision making becomes a governance challenge. We present two empirical studies (N=879) of real-time, text-based group deliberation in an incentive-compatible charity allocation task with real financial stakes ($7,200 USD). Groups of three allocate a donation budget under varying LLM facilitation conditions: Study 1 (N=204) compares three frontier models; Study 2 (N=675) compares facilitator strategies against a no-facilitation baseline. Across both studies, LLM facilitation did not significantly improve group consensus in either study, yet participants consistently preferred facilitated discussion. We additionally identify two governance-relevant risks. First, algorithmic steering: facilitators shifted select charity-level allocations by up to 5.5 percentage points -- directly affecting the final charitable payout -- even when aggregate agreement metrics remained unchanged. Second, an illusion of inclusion: participants cited inclusivity as their primary reason for preferring LLM facilitators, yet neither survey nor transcript-based measures of participation equity improved. Notably, participants reported greater trust in the process under the same conditions where facilitators exerted directional influence on outcomes. Together, these findings show that in AI-mediated group deliberation, perceived procedural improvement can coexist with measurable steering and unchanged participation inequality, motivating evaluation practices that treat collective outcomes, interaction dynamics, and participant perceptions as distinct governance targets.

</details>


### [42] [Pluot: Towards 'write once, run everywhere' visualization software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14118)
*Mark S. Keller,Nils Gehlenborg*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Pluot是一种基于Rust的可视化架构，允许开发者用单一代码实现即可在静态/交互、桌面/Web等不同环境和格式下运行可视化渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可视化工具通常被划分为静态vs交互、桌面vs Web等不同阵营，导致开发者需要为不同目标和环境分别实现可视化代码，缺乏统一的跨环境解决方案。

Method: 使用Rust语言实现可视化渲染函数一次，然后通过生成绑定（bindings）使其他编程语言（如Python或JavaScript）可调用该Rust程序，支持渲染为位图或矢量图形格式。

Result: 实现了一个能够桥接静态/交互、桌面/Web等不同环境划分的单一可视化软件架构，兼顾交互性能与出版级图片质量。

Conclusion: Pluot架构证明了一种可视化实现即可跨多种交互级别和计算环境使用的可行性，缩小了不同可视化工具阵营之间的差距。

Abstract: Tools used for implementing visualization software systems can generally be divided into camps such as static versus interactive and desktop versus web-based. We contribute Pluot, an architecture that bridges these divides, enabling a single software implementation of a visualization to be used regardless of the target level of interactivity or computing environment. With Pluot, a visualization developer implements a given visualization rendering function once, using the Rust programming language. Then, bindings to the Rust program can be generated to enable reproducible execution of the rendering function from other languages, such as Python or JavaScript. Pluot can render visualizations to bitmap or vector graphics format, bridging gaps between interactive performance and publication-quality figure creation. The software is available at https://pluot.dev.

</details>


### [43] [Self-Regulated Learning in Essay Writing: Consistency of Strategies and Impact on Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14228)
*Gloria Fernández-Nieto,Kiyoshige Garcés,Mladen Raković,Tongguang Li,Xinyu Li,Linxuan Zhao,Dragan Gašević*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析哥伦比亚93-95名中学生在两周内完成在线议论文写作时的元认知轨迹数据，结合过程挖掘与无监督机器学习，识别出三种主要的自我调节学习（SRL）策略。研究发现许多学生倾向于采用“先读后写”策略，而“精读选写”策略虽较少使用，但与学习成果正相关。研究揭示了SRL策略的动态变化及其与学习成效的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 青春期是自我调节学习能力巩固的关键期，在线与混合教育日益普及，但对中学生在在线环境中如何实施SRL、策略如何随时间演化及其对学习结果的影响尚缺乏了解，尤其缺少重复测量设计的研究证据。

Method: 采用重复测量设计，通过数字学习平台收集哥伦比亚两所学校中学生在两周内完成的在线议论文写作任务中的元认知相关轨迹数据（第一轮N=93，第二轮N=95）。结合过程挖掘与无监督机器学习技术，识别基于既定SRL理论的主导策略，并分析其稳定性与学习成果的关联。

Result: 识别出三种主导SRL策略。“先读后写”策略为多数学生采用或转向的策略；“精读选写”策略在第二轮中无人使用，但该策略与学习成果呈正相关。结果显示SRL策略存在明显的变异性。

Conclusion: 中学生在在线议论文写作中的SRL策略呈现动态变化，不同策略对学习效果的影响存在差异。研究为设计在线学习支持机制提供了依据，并强调了纵向追踪SRL策略的必要性。

Abstract: Background: Abilities for effective self-regulated learning (SRL) are critical for lifelong learning, particularly during adolescence when these skills consolidate and strongly influence future learning. Their importance has grown with the rise of online and blended education. Yet, little is known about how secondary school students self-regulate in online environments, how their SRL processes and strategies evolve, or how they affect outcomes. In secondary education, understanding these processes can reveal patterns and indicators of learning success, informing the design of online support mechanisms. Evidence from repeated-measures designs remains scarce. Objectives: This study aims to examine how secondary school students enact SRL strategies during online essay writing, how these strategies change over time, and how they relate to learning outcomes. Methods: We analysed metacognition-related trace data collected from secondary students during a two-wave online essay-writing task conducted one week apart in two Colombian schools (N = 93 for session 1, N = 95 for session 2) via a digital learning platform. Using a combination of process mining and unsupervised machine learning techniques, we identified dominant SRL strategies grounded in established SRL processes and examined their stability and association with learning outcomes. Results and conclusions: Three dominant SRL strategies were identified. Results showed variability: many students remained in or shifted to Read first, write next, while none used Write intensively, read selectively in session 2. Although less common, latter strategy was positively associated with learning outcomes.

</details>


### [44] [A Formative Study of Brief Affective Text as a Complement to Wearable Sensing for Longitudinal Student Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14360)
*Tamunotonye Harry,Johanna Hidalgo,Matthew Price,Yuanyuan Feng,Kathryn Stanton,Connie Tompkins,Peter Sheridan Dodds,Mikaela Irene Fudolig,Laura Bloomfield,Christopher Danforth*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究探讨超简短自然语言关切文本（中位数3词）能否作为被动传感的补充。基于458名大学生历时一年（3,610人波次）的Oura环数据，发现学术关切周与低体力活动相关，情绪疲惫语言与睡眠质量差及心率变异性低相关。通用预训练嵌入优于领域适应模型，情感维度而非主题内容承载主要信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备虽能高保真捕捉生理行为数据，但塑造这些结果的心理情境难以仅从传感器数据中恢复，限制了被动感应在数字健康中的效用。本研究旨在探索超简短自然语言文本能否作为被动传感的可扩展补充，以增强数据的心理可解释性。

Method: 458名大学生佩戴Oura环历时一年，每两月回应一次开放式提示（最担心什么），回答中位数3词。采用基于词典、通用预训练和领域自适应三种NLP方法，通过个体内混合效应模型分析九个睡眠和体力活动结果。

Result: 学术关切占主导的周与较低体力活动相关；情绪疲惫语言与较差睡眠质量和较低心率变异性相关。通用预训练嵌入在多数结果上优于领域自适应模型；关切主题的零样本分类无显著关联，而所有方法的情感维度均与结果显著相关。

Conclusion: 超简短情感提示能以最小负担丰富被动生理数据的心理可解释性。情感基调而非主题内容承载了信号，为数字健康中被动传感与语言数据的结合提供了设计指导。

Abstract: Wearable devices capture physiological and behavioral data with increasing fidelity, but the psychological context shaping these outcomes is difficult to recover from sensor data alone, limiting passive sensing utility for digital health. We examined whether ultra-brief naturalistic concern text could serve as a scalable complement to passive sensing. In a year-long study of 458 university students (3,610 person-waves) tracked with Oura rings, participants responded bimonthly to an open-ended prompt about what concerned them most; responses had a median length of three words. We compared dictionary-based, general pretrained, and domain-adapted NLP approaches using within-person mixed-effects models across nine sleep and physical activity outcomes. Weeks dominated by academic concern framing were associated with lower physical activity; weeks characterized by emotional exhaustion language were associated with poorer sleep quality and lower heart rate variability. General pretrained embeddings outperformed domain-adapted models for most outcomes, with domain adaptation showing relative advantage for autonomic outcomes. Zero-shot classification of concern topics produced no significant associations, while affective dimensions across all three methods were consistently associated with outcomes, indicating emotional register rather than topical content carries the signal. These findings offer design guidance: ultra-brief affective prompts enrich the psychological interpretability of passive physiological data at minimal burden.

</details>


### [45] [SmartWalkCoach: An AI Companion for End-to-End Walking Guidance, Motivation, and Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14628)
*Xianzhe Zhang,Mingxuan Hu,Bufan Xue,Erick Purwanto,Thomas J Selig,Daniel Yonto*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: SmartWalkCoach是一个移动AI伴侣系统，通过三个轻量级智能体（地理、陪伴、总结）为步行全流程（规划→途中→反思）提供支持。12人交叉实验表明，在信息提示基础上增加动机性对话显著提升了积极情绪和用户体验，并总结出移动AI伴侣的两大设计要义：支持性关系表达与上下文感知时机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究存在地图导航与动机性教练之间的空白：导航工具仅提供路径指引，缺乏对步行动机和陪伴的支持；而健康教练类应用又缺少实时路线规划和上下文感知能力。作者希望构建一个端到端、工具使用的智能体系统，降低步行规划认知负荷，并在步行过程中通过动态、步频感知的干预持续维持用户参与和动机。

Method: 系统由三个智能体构成：(1) GeographyAgent——基于附近POI和用户偏好进行对话式路线策划，路径规划委托给地图API；(2) AccompanyAgent——提供上下文感知、即时触发的提示，融合信息线索与关系性鼓励；(3) SummaryAgent——生成简洁的步行回顾和下一步规划。采用两周期AB/BA交叉实验设计（N=12），每位参与者在平衡条件下完成两次可比较的步行：纯信息提示 vs 信息+动机提示。使用线性混合模型分析和主题分析。

Result: 线性混合模型显示，增加动机性、伴侣式对话显著改善了结果：参与者报告了更高的积极情绪和更好的用户体验，且无携带效应。主题分析揭示了移动伴侣的两个设计要义：支持性关系表达和上下文感知时机（如避开高负荷时刻，在疲劳或里程碑时介入）。

Conclusion: 研究贡献包括：(i) 一个端到端、工具使用的智能体架构，降低日常步行的规划和陪伴认知负荷；(ii) 一项将上下文感知动机与情感和用户体验增益联系起来的受控现场评估；(iii) 针对mHealth伴侣的表达、时机和频率提供了可操作的设计指导。未来方向为多模态、语音优先的伴侣系统，配备自适应个性化机制。

Abstract: We present SmartWalkCoach, a mobile AI companion that supports the full walking journey: from pre-walk planning to in-walk guidance through to post-walk reflection. Addressing a gap between map navigation and motivational coaching, SmartWalkCoach orchestrates three lightweight agents: (1) GeographyAgent for conversational route curation from nearby points of interest and user preferences while delegating pathfinding to map APIs; (2) AccompanyAgent for context-aware, just-in-time prompts that blend informational cues with relational encouragement; and (3) SummaryAgent for concise reflection and next-step planning. This end-to-end, tool-using design aims to lower cognitive load in planning and sustain engagement and motivation during walking through delivering dynamic, cadence-aware interventions. We conducted an in-the-wild, two-period AB/BA crossover study (N=12), where each participant completed two comparable walks with counterbalanced conditions: Information-only versus Information+Motivation. Linear mixed models show that adding motivational, companion-like dialogue significantly improved outcomes: participants reported higher positive feelings and better user experience, with no evidence of carryover. Thematic analysis surfaced two design imperatives for mobile companions: supportive, relational expression and context-aware timing (e.g., avoiding high-load moments, intervening at fatigue/milestones). Our contributions are: (i) an end-to-end, tool-using agent architecture for everyday walking that reduces cognitive load during planning and accompaniment; (ii) a controlled field evaluation linking context-aware motivation to affect and UX gains; and (iii) actionable design guidance on expression, timing, and frequency for mHealth companions.We outline limitations and paths toward multimodal, voice-first companions, with adaptive personalization mechanisms.

</details>


### [46] [Beliefs and Misconceptions around Integrated Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14849)
*William Seymour,Adam Jenkins,Mark Cote,Jose Such*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 探讨用户如何理解和使用内置于浏览器中的对话式AI（以Microsoft Edge的Copilot为例），发现用户依赖对LLM和互联网搜索的既有认知，并通过引用可信度来建立信任，且较少主动核查引用来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM驱动的对话式AI逐渐融入日常工具（如浏览器），用户对其产出机制的理解和信任建立方式尚不明确，研究旨在探索用户如何形成对AI的理解并决定是否信任其输出。

Method: 进行了用户研究，20名参与者在Microsoft Edge中使用Copilot AI功能完成信息检索和规划任务，分析其理解方式和信任决策过程。

Result: 参与者依赖对LLM和互联网搜索的既有认知；引用的存在增强了答案的可信度，但用户很少实际核查引用；用户进行事实核查时倾向于访问与AI相同的信源。

Conclusion: 用户对集成式AI的信任建立存在认知依赖和引用盲区，需关注AI引用机制对用户判断的影响以及用户核查行为的局限性。

Abstract: LLM-driven conversational AI is beginning to disappear into the background, shifting from something used directly towards something increasingly integrated into existing workflows. In the process, markers of origin and training are smoothed away as LLMs become commodified in the eyes of users. We explore how people approach using a web browser with conversational AI built in, focusing on how they develop their understanding and determine whether to trust its outputs. We conducted a study where 20 participants used the Copilot AI features in Microsoft Edge to conduct information retrieval and planning tasks. Participants relied on a combination of existing perceptions of LLMs and internet search, tracing the effect of beliefs about how Copilot generated answers on prompting strategies. The inclusion of citations increased the trustworthiness of answers without participants feeling the need to be check them, with participants often reaching for the same information sources as the CAI when fact-checking.

</details>


### [47] [Towards Gaze-Informed AI Disclosure Interfaces: Eye-Tracking Attentional and Cognitive Load While Reading AI-Assisted News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14999)
*Pooja Prajod,Hannes Cools,Thomas Röggla,Pablo Cesar,Abdallah El Ali*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过眼动追踪和NASA-TLX测量发现，AI使用披露会带来注意力成本（单行披露导致更高的注视时长和眼跳次数），但不会增加认知负荷；受访者更偏好详细或"按需提供细节"的披露设计，为自适应披露界面设计提供了依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在新闻业中的普及使得设计有效的AI使用披露成为关键挑战，但现有研究主要关注信任和可信度，对披露对读者注意力和认知负荷的影响关注不足。

Method: 采用3×2×2混合因子实验设计，操纵AI使用披露详细程度（无、单行、详细）、新闻类型（政治、生活方式）和AI角色（编辑、部分内容生成），通过NASA-TLX和眼动追踪测量负荷。

Result: 单行披露导致显著更高的注视时长和眼跳次数（尤其在AI编辑内容中），详细披露未增加额外负担；NASA-TLX和瞳孔直径在各条件下无显著差异；受访者偏好详细或"按需提供细节"的设计。

Conclusion: 基于信息缺口理论，简短标签可能触发读者更多的视觉审视；AI使用披露不增加认知负荷；研究结果支持设计基于注视的自适应披露界面，根据读者注意力模式和新闻语境动态调整透明度。

Abstract: As generative AI becomes increasingly integrated into journalism, designing effective AI-use disclosures that inform readers without imposing unnecessary burden is a key challenge. While prior research has primarily focused on trust and credibility, the impact of disclosures on readers' attentional and cognitive load remains underexplored. To address this gap, we conducted a $3\times2\times2$ mixed factorial study manipulating the level of AI-use disclosure detail (none, one-line, detailed), news type (politics, lifestyle), and role of AI (editing, partial content generation), measuring load via NASA-TLX and eye-tracking. Our results reveal a significant attentional cost: one-line disclosures resulted in significantly higher fixation durations and saccade counts, particularly for AI-edited content. Detailed disclosures did not impose additional burden. Drawing on Information-Gap Theory, we argue that brief labels may trigger increased visual scrutiny by alerting readers to AI use without providing enough information. NASA-TLX scores and pupil diameter showed no significant differences across conditions, suggesting that AI-use disclosures do not impose cognitive burden regardless of the detail level. Interview insights contextualize these findings and reveal a strong preference for detailed or ``detail-on-demand'' designs. Our findings inform the design of gaze-informed adaptive disclosure interfaces that dynamically adjust transparency levels based on readers' attentional patterns and news context.

</details>


### [48] [Deceptive Cookies: Consent by Design -- A Mixed Methods Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15056)
*Liv Hilde Sjøflot,Tobias A. Opsahl*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过可用性测试和问卷（N=20）发现，用户虽倾向于拒绝Cookie收集，但因Cookie同意横幅中的欺骗性设计模式而往往接受；撤回同意的平均耗时是给予同意的20倍以上，表明当前Cookie同意横幅在用户自主性方面存在严重问题，导致“设计性同意”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解影响用户管理Cookie同意时的体验和行为的因素，探究用户声明的隐私偏好与实际行为之间的差距。

Method: 采用混合方法，通过可用性测试和问卷调查（N=20）收集数据。

Result: 参与者普遍希望拒绝Cookie收集，但因横幅中的欺骗性设计模式而接受；用户更愿意向信任的网站以及预期能改善用户体验的网站给予同意；撤回同意的平均耗时是给予同意的20倍以上。

Conclusion: 当前形式的Cookie同意横幅在用户自主性方面不理想，常导致用户“设计性同意”，即系统设计引导用户做出并非真实意愿的同意决策。

Abstract: While companies increasingly rely on data, especially when it comes to targeted advertising, adapting content to users, selling data and training machine learning models, the collection of data raises privacy concerns. One way of collecting data is by using HTTP cookies when interacting with a website. Legal regulations require service providers to collect consent for some forms of cookie collection, which is often acquired through \emph{cookie consent banners}, but their effectiveness has been debated. This study aims to understand what influences users' experience and behaviour when managing their cookie consent, by investigating the gap between their stated privacy preferences and their actual actions. A mixed methods approach was used, collecting data from a usability test and a survey (N=20). The results showed that although participants generally want to reject cookie collection, they often end up accepting because of deceptive patterns in the cookie consent banner design. It also showed that they were more willing to consent to websites they trusted and if they expected it would improve their user experience. Although the current EU legislation states that withdrawing consent must be as easy as giving it, withdrawing consent took on average more than 20 times longer than giving it. This suggests that cookie consent banners in their current form are not ideal with respect to user autonomy, often leading users to \emph{consent by design}.

</details>


### [49] [Usable but Conventional: An Empirical Study on the UX of AI-Generated Interface Prototypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15124)
*Karoline Romero,Igor Wiese,Renato Balancieiri,Gislaine Camila Leal,Guilherme Guerino*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过92名参与者的实证调查，评估了GenAI生成的原型与人类创建的原型在用户体验上的差异。结果显示GenAI原型在实用性和效率等实用维度获得积极评价，但在原创性和创新性等享乐维度评价中性或消极，表明GenAI能够生成功能性界面但强化了视觉和结构模式，影响了对原创性的感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究生成式人工智能工具生成的原型在用户体验方面的表现，特别是与人类创建的原型相比，GenAI原型在实用和享乐维度上的差异。

Method: 采用实证调查研究方法，招募92名参与者，在不知道作者身份的情况下分别评估AI生成和人类创建的原型，使用UEQ-S量表测量用户体验的实用和享乐维度。

Result: GenAI原型在实用方面（可用性、效率）获得积极评价，但在享乐方面（原创性、创新性）评价为中性或消极。

Conclusion: GenAI能够生成功能性界面，但倾向于强化视觉和结构模式，从而影响用户对原创性的感知。

Abstract: This paper investigates User Experience (UX) with prototypes generated by Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) tools. An empirical survey with 92 participants evaluated AI-generated and human-created prototypes without prior identification of authorship. We measured UX using the UEQ-S, covering pragmatic and hedonic dimensions. Results indicate positive evaluations in pragmatic aspects, such as usability and efficiency, and neutral or negative evaluations in hedonic aspects, including originality and innovation. We concluded that GenAI can produce functional interfaces but tends to reinforce visual and structural patterns that affect perceptions of originality.

</details>

### [50] [Ready from Day 1: Population-Aware Coordination for Large-Scale Constrained Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13900)
*Angel Wang,Dominique Perrault-Joncas,Alvaro Maggiar,Carson Eisenach,Dean Foster*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出人口感知协调接口（population-aware coordination interfaces）：通过学习基于人口摘要的条件化原始映射和对偶映射，使上游规划器在共享资源约束的大规模多智能体系统中，能够在人口组成变化时无需重新训练即可有效评估资源计划，实现跨周期的可靠协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模多智能体系统中，上游规划器需迭代评估候选资源计划（可行性、聚合响应、边际成本），但成本-利用率响应映射依赖于随规划周期变化的人口组成，导致传统方法需频繁重训练，效率低下。

Method: 提出人口感知协调接口：学习两个条件化映射——原始映射（预测给定成本轨迹下的聚合利用率）和对偶映射（预测目标计划对应的成本轨迹），二者以紧凑人口摘要为条件；支持从子样本到全人口的协调；将Sim2Real迁移转化为可回溯测试流程。

Result: 在供应链容量控制案例中：人口感知接口相比非感知基线，预测误差降低16-19%，容量违反降低20-51%；20K智能体子样本可准确协调500K智能体种群；模拟训练的原始映射在真实数据上MAPE为11.1%，基线为13-24%。

Conclusion: 通过学习人口感知的协调接口，可在人口组成动态变化的大规模系统中实现高效、鲁棒的资源计划协调，显著减少重训练需求并支持Sim2Real迁移。

Abstract: In large-scale multi-agent systems with shared resource constraints, an upstream planner must iteratively evaluate candidate resource plans -- assessing feasibility, aggregate response, and marginal cost -- before committing to one. Lagrangian relaxation separates local decisions through a broadcast cost signal, but the planner still needs the cost-to-utilization response map to explore plan space, and this map depends on population composition that changes across planning cycles. We propose \emph{population-aware coordination interfaces}: learned primal and dual maps, conditioned on compact population summaries, that the planner queries inside its iterative loop. The primal map predicts aggregate utilization under a proposed cost trajectory; the dual map predicts the cost trajectory for a target plan. By encoding response-relevant population structure, these maps remain reliable across evolving populations without per-cycle retraining, and support coordination of large populations from compact subsamples. We additionally cast Sim2Real transfer as a backtestable procedure, enabling evaluation before deployment. In a supply-chain capacity-control case study, population-aware interfaces reduce forecast error by 16--19\% and capacity violations by 20--51\% relative to population-unaware baselines under composition shift; 20K-agent cohorts support accurate coordination of 500K-agent populations; and simulator-trained primal maps achieve 11.1\% MAPE on real observations versus 13--24\% for baselines.

</details>


### [51] [IFPV: An Integrated Multi-Agent Framework for Generative Operational Planning and High-Fidelity Plan Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14851)
*Zhigao Huang,Zhengqing Hu,Dong Chen,Shaohan Zhang,Zhao Jin,Bo Zhang,Han Wu,Mingliang Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成多智能体框架IFPV，用于生成性作战规划和高保真计划验证。该框架包含多视角分层智能体（MPHA）生成可执行战术行动序列，以及对抗性认知仿真引擎（ACSE）通过配备定制世界模型的对手进行动态对抗验证。实验表明，IFPV在非对称作战战术模拟器中相比单步大语言模型基线提升了19.4%的任务成功率，降低了41.7%的作战成本，且ACSE的验证环境比传统规则验证器更严格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统作战计划生成与验证方法面临生成不可行和验证不充分的双重挑战，难以适应现代复杂多变的战场环境。

Method: 提出IFPV框架，包含两个紧密耦合的模块：MPHA（由Pathfinder、Analyst和Planner三种智能体协作，将指挥官意图分解为可执行的多平台战术行动序列）和ACSE（引入配备定制世界模型的对手，预测关键平台未来演化并对候选计划进行动态对抗）。

Result: 在ACTS仿真环境中，IFPV相比单步LLM规划基线，任务成功率提升19.4%，作战成本降低41.7%。相比传统规则验证器，ACSE的平均压制率提升31.8%，验证环境更具严格性和判别力。

Conclusion: 所提出的IFPV框架能够有效提升作战计划生成的可行性与验证的充分性，为复杂战场环境下的自动化作战规划提供了新范式。

Abstract: Operational plan generation and verification are critical for modern complex and rapidly changing battlefield environments, yet traditional generation and verification methods still respectively face the challenges of generation infeasibility and verification insufficiency. To alleviate these limitations, we propose an Integrated Multi-Agent Framework for Generative Operational Planning and High-Fidelity Plan Verification (IFPV). IFPV consists of two tightly coupled modules: Multi-Perspective Hierarchical Agents (MPHA) for generative operational planning and an Adversarial Cognitive Simulation Engine (ACSE) for high-fidelity adversarial plan verification. MPHA decomposes commander intent into executable multi-platform tactical action sequences through the collaboration of Pathfinder, Analyst, and Planner agents. ACSE introduces an opponent equipped with a customized world model, which predicts the future evolution of mission-critical platforms and conducts dynamic counteractions against candidate plans. Simulation experiments in the Asymmetric Combat Tactic Simulator (ACTS) show that IFPV improves mission success by 19.4% and reduces operational cost by 41.7% compared with a single-step large language model (LLM) planning baseline. Compared with a traditional rule-based validator, ACSE increases the average suppression rate by 31.8%, indicating that the proposed verification environment is stricter and more discriminative in revealing the latent vulnerabilities of candidate plans. The code for IFPV can be found at https://github.com/zhigao3ks/IFPV.

</details>


### [52] [Decision-Level Fusion for Robust Wearable Affect Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14878)
*Lokesh Singh,Athina Georgara,Jayati Deshmukh,Tan Viet Tuyen Nguyen,Sarvapali D. Ramchurn*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合FBSE和EWT的非平稳情感识别方法，并通过决策级聚合（按不确定性和可靠性加权）处理多模态生理信号，在WESAD数据集上展现出优于特征级聚合的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴生理信号的情感识别在真实部署中面临非平稳动态、伪影和传感器缺失等挑战，而传统的固定基谱特征（如FFT频带功率、Welch PSD）会过度平滑短时判别模式，亟需更鲁棒的方法。

Method: 提出非平稳处理流程：使用Fourier-Bessel级数展开（FBSE）结合EWT数据驱动谱分割提取模态级瞬态描述子；采用决策级聚合融合多模态信号，按预测不确定性和模态可靠性加权各模态预测器。

Result: 在WESAD数据集（15名受试者，ECG/EDA/BVP/EMG/ACC信号，三类情感）上，决策级聚合约84%时间不劣于特征级聚合，约48%时间严格优于特征级聚合。

Conclusion: 所提方法在异质、部分不可靠传感条件下具有更好的鲁棒性，验证了非平稳特征提取和不确定性加权决策融合的有效性。

Abstract: Automatic recognition of affective state from wearable physiology has clear societal impact for public health, preventive care, and stress-aware interventions, but real deployments require robustness to non-stationary dynamics, artefacts, and missing sensors. We study this problem on WESAD, using baseline, stress, and amusement conditions, where common fixed-basis spectral features such as FFT bandpower and Welch PSD can oversmooth short-lived discriminative patterns. We propose a non-stationary pipeline that combines Fourier-Bessel Series Expansion (FBSE) with EWT data-driven spectral segmentation to extract mode-wise transient descriptors. For multimodal integration, we adopt decision-level aggregation over per-modality predictors and weight each modality by predictive uncertainty and modality reliability. Results on WESAD, using 15 subjects and ECG, EDA, BVP, EMG, and ACC signals across three classes, indicate that decision-level aggregation is approximately 84 percent of the time at least as good as feature-level aggregation, and approximately 48 percent of the time strictly better, suggesting improved robustness under heterogeneous and partially reliable sensing.

</details>


### [53] [Temporal Fair Division in Multi-Agent Systems: From Precise Alternation Metrics to Scalable Coordination Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14879)
*Nikolaos Al. Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出旋转周期性（RP）和滑动窗口（ALT）两类轻量级时序公平性度量指标，用于重复多智能体资源竞争场景。通过定义多智能体轮流博弈（MBoE）形式化框架，证明完美交替（PA）是时序公平的规范解。实验表明，RP复杂度为O(νn+n)显著优于ALT的O(νn·n)，且两者互补：ALT在小规模群体中区分度更高，RP在大规模场景中可扩展性更强。Q-learning智能体在时序公平性上甚至劣于随机策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实环境中智能体需要反复竞争有限资源，传统公平性指标仅关注单轮分配结果，无法捕捉跨交互历史的时序公平性。现有时序公平度量缺乏轻量级、可计算的形式化方法，亟需建立统一的时序公平划分理论框架。

Method: 1) 提出旋转周期性（RP）族度量，包含旋转得分（RS）和等待周期评估（WPE）两个互补子度量；2) 提出滑动窗口（ALT）族度量作为对比基线；3) 形式化多智能体轮流博弈（MBoE）框架；4) 建立完美交替（PA）作为时序公平规范解，连接比例性、无嫉妒性和n周期循环分配；5) 在n∈{2,3,5,8,10}场景下进行Q-learning与随机策略的对比实验。

Result: 1) RP复杂度O(νn+n) vs ALT的O(νn·n)；2) Q-learning智能体在RP上比随机策略差10-73%，在CALT上差7-35%，而Reward Fairness错误地保持>0.92；3) RP实现12-25倍计算加速；4) 两类度量互补：ALT小规模区分度优，RP大规模可扩展性强。

Conclusion: RP和ALT共同构成时序公平划分的诊断工具包，揭示了传统奖励公平性指标无法检测的协调失败问题。RP在大规模场景下的计算优势使其成为分析重复多智能体资源竞争时序公平性的实用方案。

Abstract: A plethora real-world environments require agents to compete repeatedly for the same limited resource, calling for a temporal notion of fairness judged across entire interaction histories. This paper advances the theory of temporal fair division by introducing Rotational Periodicity (RP), a family of lightweight metrics, alongside the ALT family of sliding-window measures, within a unified framework for repeated multi-agent resource competition.
  We formalise the Multi-Agent Battle of the Exes (MBoE) as a repeated fair division instance and establish Perfect Alternation (PA) as its canonical temporally fair solution, drawing connections to proportionality, envy-freeness, and n-periodic round-robin allocation. RP decomposes temporal fairness into two complementary sub-measures: Rotational Score (RS) and Waiting Periods Evaluation (WPE), achieving O(nu+n) time complexity versus the O(nu*n) of ALT, where nu is the episode count and n the agent count.
  Empirical evaluation across n in {2,3,5,8,10} reveals three findings. First, both RP and ALT expose a coordination failure invisible to traditional metrics: Q-learning agents perform worse than random policies by 10-73% on RP and 7-35% on CALT, while Reward Fairness remains misleadingly high (above 0.92 for n>=3). Second, RP achieves 12-25x computational speedup over ALT, growing with n. Third, the two families are complementary: ALT provides richer discrimination for small populations; RP scales reliably where ALT becomes intractable. Together they form a diagnostic toolkit for temporal fair division.

</details>


### [54] [Multi-Agentic Approach for History Matching of Oil Reservoirs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15028)
*Linar Samigullin,Sergei Shumilin,Evgeny Burnaev*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: PetroGraph是一个多智能体框架，将油藏历史匹配工作流分解为模型审查、实验规划、参数化、优化、模拟和总结等 specialized agents，结合LLM、领域工具、检索增强文档、人工干预检查点和OPM Flow模拟后端，支持用户通过自然语言驱动历史匹配。在SPE1、SPE9和Norne三个模型上分别降低95%、69%和13%的加权归一化均方根误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 油藏历史匹配作为反问题核心，自动化虽能减少人工，但实际部署中工程师仍需手动配置异构工作流（参数选择、物理边界、优化器选择、超参数调优、模拟执行、诊断报告等），缺乏统一智能框架来降低操作复杂性和专业门槛。

Method: 提出PetroGraph多智能体框架，包含六个专业智能体（模型审查、实验规划、参数化、优化、模拟、总结），集成大语言模型、领域专用工具、基于检索增强的模拟器文档访问、ECLIPSE输入文件验证、人工在环检查点以及基于OPM Flow的模拟后端，支持自然语言驱动的交互式历史匹配。

Result: 在三个油藏模型上以加权归一化均方根误差为目标进行评估：SPE1（合成简单模型）降低95%误差，SPE9（含断层基准模型）降低69%，Norne（真实油田模型）降低13%。

Conclusion: 多智能体编排能够自动化历史匹配中的关键决策，降低复杂模拟工作流的专业知识门槛，为可扩展、领域感知的油藏模型自适应提供灵活基础。

Abstract: History matching is a central inverse problem in reservoir engineering, where uncertain reservoir parameters must be calibrated against observations. Although automated history matching can reduce manual effort, practical deployment remains difficult because engineers must still configure heterogeneous workflows involving parameter selection, physically admissible bounds, optimizer choice, hyperparameter tuning, simulator execution, and diagnostic reporting. We propose PetroGraph, a multi-agent framework for intelligent reservoir history matching that decomposes this workflow into specialized agents for model review, experimental planning, parameterization, optimization, simulation, and summarization. The system combines large language model agents with domain-specific tools, retrieval-augmented access to simulator documentation, validation of modified ECLIPSE input decks, human-in-the-loop checkpoints, and an OPM Flow-based simulation backend. This design enables users to initiate and steer history matching through natural language while preserving explicit control over selected parameters and optimization settings. We evaluate PetroGraph on three reservoir models of increasing complexity: the synthetic SPE1 model, the faulted SPE9 benchmark, and the real-field Norne model. Using weighted normalized root mean square error as the objective, PetroGraph reduces the mismatch by 95% on SPE1, 69% on SPE9, and 13% on Norne. These results demonstrate that multi-agent orchestration can automate key decisions in history matching, lower the expertise barrier for operating complex simulation workflows, and provide a flexible foundation for extensible, domain-aware reservoir model adaptation.

</details>

### [55] [BiSpikCLM: A Spiking Language Model integrating Softmax-Free Spiking Attention and Spike-Aware Alignment Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13859)
*Sihang Guo,Chenlin Zhou,Jiaqi Wang,Kehai Chen,Qingyan Meng,Zhengyu Ma*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: BiSpikCLM是首个全二值化的脉冲因果语言模型，通过Softmax-Free脉冲注意力机制消除浮点运算，并采用Spike-Aware Alignment Distillation训练策略，在仅使用5.6%训练tokens的情况下达到与ANN模型相当的性能，计算成本仅为后者的4.16%-5.87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脉冲大语言模型为保持容量仍需大量浮点矩阵乘法和非线性操作，且复杂的时空动态特性导致训练困难，亟需一种既能充分利用SNN事件驱动低功耗优势、又能解决训练难题的高效方案。

Method: 提出Softmax-Free Spiking Attention (SFSA)消除自回归语言建模中的softmax和浮点运算；设计Spike-Aware Alignment Distillation (SpAD)框架，在嵌入、注意力图、中间特征和输出logits四个层次对齐ANN教师与SNN学生，实现高效知识蒸馏。

Result: BiSpikCLM在自然语言生成任务上以仅4.16%-5.87%的计算成本达到竞争性性能；1.3B模型仅需5.6%的训练tokens即可匹配ANN对应模型的性能。

Conclusion: 全二值化脉冲驱动大语言模型在保持性能的同时显著降低计算成本是可行且有效的，所提出的蒸馏框架为类脑脉冲自然语言处理提供了有前景的路径。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer promising energy-efficient alternatives to large language models (LLMs) due to their event-driven nature and ultra-low power consumption. However, to preserve capacity, most existing spiking LLMs still incur intensive floating-point matrix multiplication (MatMul) and nonlinearities, or training difficulties arising from the complex spatiotemporal dynamics. To address these challenges, we propose BiSpikCLM, the first fully binary spiking MatMul-free causal language model. BiSpikCLM introduces Softmax-Free Spiking Attention (SFSA), eliminating softmax and floating-point operations in autoregressive language modeling. For efficient training, we introduce Spike-Aware Alignment Distillation (SpAD), which aligns ANN teacher and SNN student across embeddings, attention maps, intermediate features, and output logits. SpAD framework allows BiSpikCLM to reach comparable performance to ANN counterparts using substantially fewer training tokens (e.g., only 5.6% of the tokens for the 1.3B model). As a result, BiSpikCLM achieves competitive performance at only 4.16% - 5.87% of the computational cost on natural language generation tasks. Our results highlight the feasibility and effectiveness of fully binary spike-driven LLMs and establish the distillation as a promising pathway for brain-inspired spiking NLP.

</details>


### [56] [Indian Wedding System Optimization (IWSO): A Novel Socially Inspired Metaheuristic with Operational Design and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13871)
*Deepika Saxena,Kishu Gupta,Jitendra Kumar,Jatinder Kumar,Sakshi Patni,Vinaytosh Mishra,Niharika Singh,Ashutosh Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受印度传统婚礼社会文化动态启发的新型群体元启发式算法——印度婚礼系统优化（IWSO），通过模拟家庭、候选人和媒人之间的协作匹配过程，实现复杂优化问题的求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受传统印度婚礼中家族、候选人与媒人协作匹配的社会文化动态启发，旨在设计一种高效的群体元启发式优化算法。

Method: IWSO算法包含两个关键创新：（i）媒人引导的影响策略，由精英解引导弱候选解的进化，无需外部参数即可增强收敛性；（ii）自适应淘汰与重新初始化机制，通过替换表现不佳的个体来维持种群多样性并防止早熟收敛。算法采用加权多目标适应度函数，并推导了时间和空间复杂度。

Result: 在高维和多模态基准测试函数上的大量实验表明，IWSO在收敛速度、解质量和鲁棒性方面优于遗传算法（GA）、粒子群优化（PSO）、差分进化（DE）、布谷鸟搜索（CS）等现有优化方法。

Conclusion: IWSO作为一种新颖的群体元启发式算法，在复杂优化问题上展现出优越性能，验证了其社会文化启发机制的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel population-based metaheuristic, Indian Wedding System Optimization (IWSO), inspired by the socio-cultural dynamics of traditional Indian weddings. IWSO models the matchmaking process driven by collaboration among families, candidates, and matchmakers as a guided, selective search framework for solving complex optimization problems. The algorithm introduces two key innovations: (i) a matchmaker-guided influence strategy, where elite solutions direct the evolution of weaker candidates, enhancing convergence without external parameters; and (ii) an adaptive elimination and reinitialization mechanism that maintains diversity and prevents premature convergence by replacing underperforming individuals. IWSO employs a weighted multi-objective fitness function and analytically derived time and space complexity, benchmarked against existing optimization approaches such as Genetic Algorithm (GA), Partical Swarm Optimization (PSO), Differential Evolution (DE), Cuckoo Search (CS), etc. Extensive experiments on benchmark high-dimensional and multimodal test functions demonstrate superior performance of IWSO in terms of convergence speed, solution quality, and robustness.

</details>


### [57] [ARES-LSHADE: Autoresearch-Enhanced LSHADE with Memetic Polish for the GNBG Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13877)
*Abdullah Naeem,Md Wasi Ul kabir,Manish Bhatt,Ayon Dey,Anav Katwal,Md Tamjidul Hoque*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: ARES-LSHADE 是一种基于 LLM 驱动的记忆差分进化变体，结合自适应 CMA-ES 的侦察增强变异算子和多起点 L-BFGS-B 精化阶段，在 GECCO 2026 GNBG 竞赛中获得 510/744 胜场，达到 18/24 函数的机器精度。论文还报告了 LLM 研究循环的收敛行为，以及扩展观测空间时触及黑箱规则边界的发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 GECCO 2026 LLM 设计进化算法竞赛的 GNBG 基准上，进一步提升算法优化性能，同时探索 LLM 驱动的自动化算法设计方法的边界与局限。

Method: 基于 LLM-LSHADE 2025 冠军算法，引入两个新组件：(a) 侦察增强变异算子，经约三十次 LLM 驱动实验自适应集成 CMA-ES；(b) 多起点 L-BFGS-B 精化阶段，严格遵循黑箱设定。采用操作符编辑曲面和适应度观测空间，通过自主循环迭代设计。

Result: 在 31 次运行/函数的官方评估中，ARES-LSHADE 获得 510/744 胜场（函数间差距 <1e-8），18/24 函数达到机器精度。剩余 6 个函数呈现典型的 GNBG 组成结构平台特征，被自主研究循环识别为最困难函数。

Conclusion: LLM 驱动的研究循环在仅操作符编辑和适应度观测下会收敛到特征平台；当观测空间扩展到包含基准组成元数据时算法可轻松解决所有函数但违反了竞赛的黑箱规则。揭示了 LLM 能力与基准完整性之间的张力，为未来 LLM 驱动的优化算法设计提供参考。

Abstract: We present ARES-LSHADE, a memetic differential-evolution variant submitted to the GECCO 2026 competition on LLM-designed evolutionary algorithms for the Generalized Numerical Benchmark Generator (GNBG). The algorithm builds on the LLM-LSHADE 2025 winner, contributing two new components: (a) a scout-augmented mutation operator with adaptive CMA-ES integration, produced by an autonomous research loop across approximately thirty LLM-driven design experiments, and (b) a multi-start L-BFGS-B polish phase that respects strict blackbox treatment of the benchmark. On the official 31-run-per-function evaluation with the competition-specified function-evaluation budgets, ARES-LSHADE obtains 510 of 744 wins (per-function gap below 1e-8), reaching machine precision on 18 of 24 functions. The remaining six functions exhibit characteristic plateau signatures consistent with GNBG's compositional structure, and were independently identified by the autoresearch loop as the hardest of the suite. Beyond the result itself, this report documents two methodological observations: (i) an LLM-driven research loop with operator-only edit surface and fitness-only observation space converges to a characteristic plateau on this benchmark; (ii) when we initially widened the observation space to include the benchmark's compositional metadata, the resulting algorithm trivially solved all 24 functions but violated the competition's blackbox rule, which we identified before submission. We discuss this tension between LLM capability and benchmark integrity as a design consideration for future LLM-driven optimization-algorithm research. Code and reproducibility artifacts are available at https://github.com/anaeem1/ARES-LSHADE.

</details>


### [58] [Darwin Family: MRI-Trust-Weighted Evolutionary Merging for Training-Free Scaling of Language-Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14386)
*Taebong Kim,Youngsik Hong,Minsik Kim,Sunyoung Choi,Jaewon Jang,Junghoon Shin,Minseo Kim*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出Darwin Family框架，通过无需训练的进化式合并方法，在权重空间中进行梯度无关的重组，显著提升大语言模型的推理性能。旗舰模型Darwin-27B-Opus在GPQA Diamond上达到86.9%，排名第6，超越其全训练的基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否可以在不进行额外训练的情况下，通过重组已有检查点中编码的潜在能力来提升前沿水平的推理性能，从而避免昂贵且资源密集的后训练流程。

Method: 提出三项核心技术：(i) 14维自适应合并基因组，实现细粒度的组件级和块级重组；(ii) MRI-Trust Fusion，通过可学习的信任参数自适应平衡诊断性层重要性信号与进化搜索；(iii) 架构映射器（Architecture Mapper），实现异构模型族之间的跨架构繁殖。

Result: Darwin-27B-Opus在GPQA Diamond上达到86.9%，在1252个评估模型中排名第6，超越其完全训练的基础模型。在4B到35B参数规模上，Darwin模型持续优于其父代模型，支持递归多代进化，并能结合Transformer和Mamba组件。

Conclusion: 诊断引导的进化式合并是面向推理中心语言模型的一种实用且可复现的替代方案，可替代成本高昂的后训练流程。

Abstract: We present Darwin Family, a framework for training-free evolutionary merging of large language models via gradient-free weight-space recombination. We ask whether frontier-level reasoning performance can be improved without additional training, by reorganizing latent capabilities already encoded in existing checkpoints. Darwin introduces three key ideas: (i) a 14-dimensional adaptive merge genome enabling fine-grained component- and block-level recombination; (ii) MRI-Trust Fusion, which adaptively balances diagnostic layer-importance signals with evolutionary search through a learnable trust parameter; and (iii) an Architecture Mapper that enables cross-architecture breeding between heterogeneous model families. Empirically, the flagship Darwin-27B-Opus achieves 86.9% on GPQA Diamond, ranking #6 among 1,252 evaluated models, and outperforming its fully trained foundation model without any gradient-based training. Across scales from 4B to 35B parameters, Darwin models consistently improve over their parents, support recursive multi-generation evolution, and enable a training-free evolutionary merge that combines Transformer- and Mamba-based components. Together, the Darwin Family demonstrates that diagnostic-guided evolutionary merging is a practical and reproducible alternative to costly post-training pipelines for reasoning-centric language models.

</details>


### [59] [First Mathematical Runtime Analyses of Multi-Objective Evolutionary Algorithms for Multi-Valued Decision Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14836)
*Mingfeng Li,Zheng Cheng,Weijie Zheng,Benjamin Doerr*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文首次对多值决策空间（r>2）下的多目标进化算法（MOEA）进行了严格的运行时分析。针对经典SEMO算法在r值OneMinMax基准问题上的表现，证明了期望运行时间上界O(n²r²log n)和下界Ω(n²r(r+log n))，并通过只接受严格改进解的变体算法将上界改进至O(n²r(r+log n))，与下界匹配。结果表明经典MOEA处理多值变量时无显著额外困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多值决策变量问题在实际中普遍存在，但此前多目标进化算法的数学运行时分析仅局限于二进制决策空间（r=2），缺乏对r>2情况的理论研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究MOEA在多值决策空间中的表现。

Method: 分析经典SEMO算法（使用单位强度局部变异）在多值OneMinMax基准问题上的运行时行为。首先推导期望运行时间的上界O(n²r²log n)和下界Ω(n²r(r+log n))；随后考虑只接受严格改进解的算法变体，推导其上界为O(n²r(r+log n))，与下界匹配。

Result: 对于标准SEMO算法，得到上界O(n²r²log n)和下界Ω(n²r(r+log n))，两者间存在小间隙；对于只接受严格改进解的变体，得到上界O(n²r(r+log n))，与下界完全匹配。

Conclusion: 经典MOEA在处理多值决策变量时不会遇到显著的额外困难；但对于具有更复杂种群动态的算法，可能需要更高级的分析工具才能得到紧界。

Abstract: Problems defined on binary decision spaces have been intensively studied in the theory of multi-objective evolutionary algorithms (MOEAs). In contrast, no mathematical runtime analyses exist so far for MOEAs dealing with decision variables that take a finite number $r > 2$ of values, despite the prevalence of such problems in practice. In this work, we begin to fill this research gap. We analyze how the classic SEMO algorithm with unit-strength local mutation computes the Pareto front of an $r$-valued counterpart of the classic \oneminmax benchmark. For the expected number of function evaluations until the Pareto front is covered by the population of this MOEA, we prove an upper bound of $O(n^2 r^2 \log n)$ and a near-tight lower bound of $Ω(n^2 r (r + \log n))$. We can close the small remaining gap between these two bounds by considering a variant of the algorithm that accepts only strictly better solutions; for this variant, we show an upper bound of $O(n^2 r (r + \log n))$, matching our lower bound (which also holds for this variant). Our results suggest that classic MOEAs encounter no significant additional difficulties when dealing with multi-valued decision variables. However, significantly more advanced tools may be required to obtain tight bounds for algorithms with more complex population dynamics.

</details>
