### [60] [Neural Code Translation of Legacy Code: APL to C#](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13896)
*Abdulrahman Ramadan,Hanen Borchani,Iben Lilholm,Mikkel Almind,Allan Peter Engsig-Karup*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用大语言模型将高度简洁的APL语言自动翻译为C#语言，通过对比自然语言描述辅助、检索增强和迭代优化三种引导策略，证明了神经代码翻译能够有效弥合APL与C#之间的差距，且引入额外上下文和引导能显著提升翻译质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APL语言语法稀疏、缺乏大规模并行语料库且需要专业知识才能理解，导致其与C#之间的自动翻译极具挑战性，现有方法难以有效处理该问题。

Method: 提出了一个包含三种引导策略（自然语言描述辅助、检索增强、迭代优化）的APL-to-C#翻译框架，与基线直接翻译模型进行对比；构建了涵盖不同复杂度的功能等价代码对数据集，并开发了自动评估流水线来验证生成代码的语法编译和功能执行。

Result: 神经代码翻译能够成功地将APL程序转换为C#代码，且引入额外上下文和引导（如自然语言描述、检索增强、迭代优化）显著提升了模型性能。

Conclusion: 基于大语言模型的引导式翻译框架可以有效解决APL到C#的翻译难题，为处理高度简洁和特殊化编程语言之间的自动翻译提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Automatic translation between programming languages remains a challenging problem, particularly when the source language is highly concise and specialized. This paper investigates the translation of APL into C# using large language models. The task is difficult due to APL's sparse syntax, the scarcity of large-scale parallel corpora, and the requirement for specialized knowledge to interpret APL programs. To address these challenges, we introduce a novel framework for APL-to-C# translation by comparing three guided strategies, namely natural language description-mediated, retrieval-augmented, and iterative refinement, against a baseline direct translation model. We constructed multiple datasets of functionally equivalent code pairs spanning various levels of complexity, and to rigorously assess translation quality, we developed an automated evaluation pipeline that verifies both syntactic compilation and functional execution of the generated C# code. Our results demonstrate that neural code translation can successfully bridge the gap between APL and C# for a wide range of programs, and that incorporating additional context and guidance significantly improves model performance.

</details>


### [61] [Bidirectional Empowerment of Metamorphic Testing and Large Language Models: A Systematic Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13898)
*Zheng Zheng,Zenghui Zhou,Yinwang Xu,Daixu Ren,Tsong Yueh Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了93篇研究，提出 "双向赋能" 框架：一方面变体测试用于验证大模型的幻觉、公平性、鲁棒性等；另一方面大模型自动化解变体测试中关系发现、输入合成、测试实现等劳动密集型环节，形成良性循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的生成性、概率性和开放性给软件质量保证带来巨大挑战，传统测试方法因预言问题（oracle problem）难以适用。变体测试（MT）通过检查多次执行间的必要关系而非依赖精确预期输出，成为有前景的替代方案；同时LLM的语义理解与生成能力又可反向自动化MT中传统上劳动密集的环节，这种双向关系亟需系统性梳理。

Method: 系统综述方法，筛选93篇主要研究，提出"MT与LLM双向赋能"分类体系：方向一为MT for LLMs（验证、评估LLM的幻觉、公平性、鲁棒性等），方向二为LLMs for MT（利用LLM自动化变体关系发现、输入变换与合成、可执行测试实现、智能体闭环测试）。

Result: 构建了涵盖两个互补方向的系统化分类框架，总结了MT在LLM幻觉、公平性、鲁棒性、代码可靠性、检索增强生成、对话系统、自主智能体等场景的应用，以及LLM对MT各阶段的自动化支持。

Conclusion: MT与LLM之间形成双向赋能的协同关系，为构建更严谨、可扩展、可信赖的AI质量保证方法提供了结构化基础，并指出了未来研究方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have introduced substantial challenges to software quality assurance due to their generative, probabilistic, and open-ended nature, which intensifies the oracle problem and limits the applicability of traditional testing methods. Metamorphic testing (MT), which checks necessary relations among multiple related executions rather than relying on exact expected outputs, has emerged as a promising approach for testing LLMs and other oracle-deficient systems. At the same time, the strong semantic understanding, reasoning, and code generation capabilities of LLMs create new opportunities to automate the traditionally labor-intensive phases of MT. This survey systematically reviews 93 primary studies and characterizes this reciprocal relationship as the bidirectional empowerment of MT and LLMs. We propose a taxonomy spanning two complementary directions: MT for LLMs, which uses MT to verify, validate, assess, and understand LLMs and LLM-based systems across issues such as hallucination, fairness, robustness, code reliability, retrieval-augmented generation, dialogue, and autonomous agents; and LLMs for MT, which leverages LLMs to support metamorphic relation discovery, input transformation and synthesis, executable test implementation, and agentic closed-loop testing. By synthesizing these developments, this survey provides a structured foundation for understanding the evolving synergy between MT and LLMs and highlights future directions for building more rigorous, scalable, and trustworthy AI quality assurance methodologies.

</details>


### [62] [A Non-Destructive Methodological Framework for Modernizing Legacy Clinical Reporting Systems for AI-Driven Pharmacoinformatics: A SAS Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13905)
*Jaime Yan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非破坏性方法框架，通过元数据层（桥接映射、类型化中间表示IR、编排器）包装遗留临床报告系统，在不改动源码前提下实现AI就绪。在558个组件、37.3万行代码的SAS库上验证，共存模式下立即产出机器可读输出；核心模块重构后专有代码减少92%，Phase III研究14类报告平均单元格级一致率82.7%（最佳99.2%），CDISC基准测试达100%一致性。LLM实验证实IR支持自动化药物警戒、表格总结和试验配置生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统临床报告系统虽符合监管逻辑，但输出不透明、缺乏机器可读中间层，阻碍AI集成。现有方案要么完全重写（风险高、成本大），要么渐进重构（保留结构性障碍），无法兼顾合规性与AI就绪。

Method: 构建非破坏性元数据层，包含：(1) 桥接映射（bridge map），建立遗留组件与现代核心例程的对应关系；(2) 类型化中间表示（IR），将系统输出转为LLM可消费的结构化数据；(3) 编排器（orchestrator），协调新旧组件协同工作。支持可选的增量式核心替换。

Result: 在558个组件、37.3万行代码的SAS库上验证：共存模式下立即实现AI就绪；重构核心减少92%专有代码；14类Phase III报告中11类实现≥80%单元格级一致（均值82.7%，最高99.2%）；CDISC基准测试5类报告达100%一致；LLM实验确认IR支持药物警戒、表格总结与试验配置生成。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一条合规感知、非破坏性的临床报告系统AI集成路径，既保留现有系统的监管合规性，又解锁AI驱动的药物信息学能力，可加速药物开发且不中断监管提交流程。

Abstract: Drug development and pharmacovigilance are frequently bottlenecked by legacy clinical reporting pipelines. These monolithic systems encode regulatory-grade logic but resist AI integration by producing opaque output with no machine-readable intermediate layer. Existing modernization approaches force a choice between full rewrites and incremental refactoring that preserves structural barriers. We present a non-destructive methodological framework achieving AI-driven pharmacoinformatics readiness without altering legacy source code. A metadata layer--comprising a bridge map, a typed Intermediate Representation (IR), and an orchestrator--wraps existing components and re-exposes their outputs as structured data consumable by LLMs. It enables optional incremental consolidation, replacing selected legacy components with metadata-configured core routines while the remainder operates unchanged. Validated on a 558-component SAS reporting library (373,000 lines of code), the framework demonstrated immediate AI-readiness under coexistence mode, yielding machine-readable output. Where consolidation was elected, the modernized core achieved a 92% reduction in proprietary code. Parity validation on 14 report types from a Phase III study achieved cell-level parity of 80% or above on 11 reports (mean 82.7%, best 99.2%). A benchmark using CDISC CDISCPilot01 data achieved 100% parity across 5 reports. LLM experiments confirmed the IR enables automated pharmacovigilance, table summarization, and trial configuration generation. The framework offers a regulation-aware path to AI-integrated clinical reporting, accelerating drug development without interrupting regulatory submissions.

</details>


### [63] [LLM-Based Robustness Testing of Microservice Applications: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14202)
*Hrushitha Goud Tigulla,Marco Vieira*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过控制实验，探索了7种提示策略与3种开源LLM（14B-70B参数）在微服务API鲁棒性测试中的效果，发现提示策略对测试多样性影响大于模型大小，其中GuidedFewShot策略在两种架构不同的微服务系统上实现了最高的单次运行覆盖率（5/9和8/14故障模式），并指出仅靠分类规则不足，LLM需要具体示例才能区分键缺失与值空突变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务API中的畸形、缺失或边界值输入可能级联传播，威胁系统可靠性。鲁棒性测试通过系统性地生成此类输入来暴露服务端故障，但如何生成多样化且有效的测试仍具挑战。LLM可从API规范生成测试，但不同模型和提示策略是否会产生多样化的故障集或收敛于相同故障尚不清楚。

Method: 进行受控实验，应用7种提示策略到3个开源LLM（参数范围14B-70B），针对两个架构不同的微服务系统：一个Java单语言系统（6项服务，9种故障模式）和一个多语言系统（27项服务，14种故障模式），共获得38次有效运行和663个生成测试。引入Guided和GuidedFewShot两种策略，将先前鲁棒性测试研究的突变分类法作为领域上下文嵌入。

Result: 提示策略对测试多样性的解释力大于模型大小：结构化提示完全消除了多样性，而一个模型在三种提示策略下即可在一个系统上实现完全的故障模式覆盖，优于任何固定提示下的多模型集成。GuidedFewShot在两种系统上均实现最高单次运行覆盖率（5/9和8/14故障模式），同时保持低跨模型相似性。仅靠分类规则不足，LLM无法区分键缺失与值空突变，需要具体示例。

Conclusion: 提示策略在LLM驱动的微服务鲁棒性测试中比模型大小更重要；GuidedFewShot策略通过嵌入突变分类法并结合示例，能有效提升故障覆盖率和多样性；仅提供抽象分类规则而不给具体示例，LLM难以区分细微的输入变异类型。研究结果在两个不同架构的系统上得到复现验证。

Abstract: Malformed, missing, or boundary-value inputs in microservice APIs can cascade across dependent services, threatening reliability. Robustness testing systematically exercises such inputs to expose server-side failures, but generating diverse, effective tests remains challenging. Large Language Models can generate such tests from API specifications; however, it is unknown whether different models and prompt strategies produce diverse failure sets or converge on the same failures. We report a controlled experiment applying 7 prompt strategies to 3 open-source LLMs (14B-70B parameters) targeting 2 architecturally distinct microservice systems: one Java monolingual (6 services, 9 failure modes) and one polyglot (27 services, 14 failure modes), yielding 38 valid runs and 663 generated tests. We find that prompt strategy explains more variation in diversity than model size: a Structured prompt collapses diversity entirely, while a single model varied across three prompt strategies achieves complete failure-mode coverage on one system, outperforming any multi-model ensemble under a fixed prompt. We introduce two strategies, Guided and GuidedFewShot, that embed a mutation taxonomy from prior robustness testing research as domain context. GuidedFewShot achieves the highest single-run coverage on both systems (5 of 9 and 8 of 14 failure modes) while maintaining low cross-model similarity. A key lesson is that taxonomy rules alone are insufficient: LLMs cannot distinguish key-absent from value-empty mutations without concrete examples. Findings replicate across both systems.

</details>


### [64] [Failure-Guided Fuzzing for Hybrid Quantum-Classical Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14219)
*Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向混合量子-经典（HQC）程序的失败引导模糊测试方法，通过两阶段策略（先搜索非收敛种子，再对种子附近的电路参数进行局部模糊测试）来检测HQC程序中的故障或非收敛配置。在VQE和QAOA实例上的实验表明，失败引导的局部模糊测试是提升测试效果的主要因素，而符号执行种子发现仅在VQE上有额外收益，在QAOA上效果不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子-经典算法（如VQE、QAOA）是近期量子计算的核心，但测试非常困难。基于采样的模糊测试在现实执行预算下可能遗漏经典优化器超参数与量子电路参数联合空间中的故障密集区域，因此需要更高效的测试方法。

Method: 将混合输入建模为经典优化器超参数与量子电路参数的二元组，提出两阶段策略：第一阶段搜索非收敛种子（通过随机、枚举或符号执行方式），第二阶段对种子附近的电路参数进行局部模糊测试。比较了五种策略：随机混合测试、无模糊的经典枚举、随机种子局部模糊、枚举种子局部模糊、符号执行种子局部模糊。

Result: 在Qiskit实现的VQE和QAOA MaxCut实例上，失败引导的局部模糊测试是提升效果的主要驱动力；符号执行种子发现在VQE上有额外收益，但在QAOA上效果不稳定。

Conclusion: 复用失败信息是HQC程序测试中一个有前景的方向，但符号执行种子发现的价值取决于具体工作负载。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical (HQC) algorithms, such as the Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) and the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA), are central to near-term quantum computing but remain challenging to test. Sampling-based fuzzing can expose faulty or non-convergent configurations, but under realistic execution budgets, it may miss failure-prone regions in the joint space of classical optimizer settings and quantum circuit parameters.
  This paper studies failure-guided fuzzing for HQC programs. It models a hybrid input as a pair of classical optimizer hyperparameters and quantum circuit parameters, and evaluates a two-phase strategy that first searches for non-convergent seeds and then locally fuzzes circuit parameters around those seeds. To understand where the gains come from, five budgeted strategies are compared: random hybrid testing, classical enumeration without fuzzing, random-seed local fuzzing, enumeration-seed local fuzzing, and concolic-seed local fuzzing. The study is implemented on a VQE instance and a QAOA MaxCut instance in Qiskit. The results show that failure-guided local fuzzing is the main driver of improvement over random testing, while concolic seed discovery provides additional benefits on VQE but is less stable on QAOA. These findings suggest that reusing failure information is a promising direction for HQC testing, but that the value of concolic seed discovery is workload-dependent.

</details>


### [65] [FuzzAgent: Multi-Agent System for Evolutionary Library Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14431)
*Yunlong Lyu,Peng Chen,Fengyi Wu,Junzhe Yu,Kit Long Hon,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FuzzAgent是一个多智能体系统，通过进化式的迭代库模糊测试框架，在20个真实C/C++库中覆盖了179619个分支，相比四种基线方法提升45.1%-191.2%，并发现了102个真实库漏洞（其中78个已被上游维护者确认修复）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有库模糊测试在规模化应用时面临环境搭建成本高、测试桩生成难以满足复杂API约束、无法区分真实漏洞与测试桩引发的崩溃等问题。现有基于LLM的系统通常作为一次性代码生成器运作，忽视了运行时反馈，限制了代码覆盖深度和漏洞报告的有效性。

Method: 提出FuzzAgent多智能体系统，将库模糊测试转化为进化过程：一组专业化智能体在完整模糊测试生命周期中协作，每次决策都基于具体运行时证据，通过多轮迭代不断优化测试桩套件以提升覆盖深度和崩溃分析保真度。

Result: 在20个真实C/C++库上，FuzzAgent无需人工干预完成完整模糊测试生命周期，覆盖179619个分支，超越OSS-Fuzz(45.1%)、PromptFuzz(73.2%)、PromeFuzz(92.1%)和OSS-Fuzz-Gen(191.2%)；发现102个真实库漏洞，其中78个已被上游维护者确认和修复。

Conclusion: 有效的库模糊测试本质上是迭代过程：每次测试活动暴露新的覆盖瓶颈和崩溃，下一次测试应基于这些信号进化而非从头开始。FuzzAgent验证了将运行时反馈融入多智能体协作框架可显著提升库模糊测试的效果和效率。

Abstract: Library fuzzing is essential for hardening the software supply chain, but adopting it at scale remains expensive. Practitioners still spend substantial effort on environment setup, struggle to generate harnesses that respect intricate API constraints, and lack reliable means to tell genuine library bugs from harness-induced crashes. Recent LLM-based systems automate parts of this pipeline, yet they typically operate as one-shot code generators that ignore runtime feedback, which limits both the depth of code they reach and the validity of the bugs they report. We argue that effective library fuzzing is iterative by nature: each campaign exposes new coverage bottlenecks and crashes, and the next campaign should evolve from these signals rather than restart from scratch. Building on this insight, we present FuzzAgent, a multi-agent system that turns library fuzzing into an evolutionary process, in which a team of specialized agents collaborates over the full fuzzing lifecycle and grounds every decision in concrete runtime evidence, so that the harness suite is successively refined toward deeper coverage and higher-fidelity crash analysis across rounds.
  We evaluate FuzzAgent on 20 real-world C/C++ libraries against four state-of-the-art baselines (OSS-Fuzz, OSS-Fuzz-Gen, PromptFuzz, and PromeFuzz). FuzzAgent completes the full fuzzing lifecycle for all 20 libraries without human intervention and reaches 179619 branches, exceeding OSS-Fuzz, PromptFuzz, PromeFuzz, and OSS-Fuzz-Gen by 45.1%, 73.2%, 92.1%, and 191.2%, respectively. FuzzAgent also identifies 102 genuine library bugs, 78 of which have already been acknowledged and fixed by upstream maintainers.

</details>


### [66] [When Retrieval Hurts Code Completion: A Diagnostic Study of Stale Repository Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14478)
*Haojun Weng,Qianqian Yang,Hao Fu,Haobin Pan,Xinwei Lv*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究表明，过时的检索代码片段（stale context）并非无害噪声，而是会主动诱导模型生成与当前代码库不兼容的代码。在17个样本中，仅提供过期上下文时，Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct有88.2%（15/17）产生过期引用，gpt-4.1-mini有76.5%（13/17），而当前上下文仅分别导致0%和5.9%的过期引用。混合条件下添加当前上下文可基本挽回此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究检索增强代码生成中，从仓库检索到的代码片段可能来自过时的项目状态，需要探究这些临时过时的检索片段是只会作为无害噪声，还是会主动诱导模型生成与当前状态不兼容的代码。

Method: 在5个Python仓库中收集17个生产环境辅助函数签名变更样本，设置当前上下文仅检索、过期上下文仅检索、无检索、混合当前/过期检索四种条件，在隐藏提交新鲜度和期望签名的中性化提示下进行对照诊断实验。

Result: 过期上下文检索条件下，Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct的88.2%（15/17）样本和gpt-4.1-mini的76.5%（13/17）样本产生过期辅助函数引用，而当前上下文检索分别只有0%和5.9%。无检索虽无过期引用但仅1/17通过编译。两种模型在触发过期的样本上有75%的Jaccard重叠。混合条件下添加当前上下文可基本消除过期上下文的负面影响。

Conclusion: 检索上下文的时序有效性是代码RAG鲁棒性的一个独立诊断变量：过期上下文不仅仅是移除有用证据，而是会主动将模型偏向过时的仓库状态。

Abstract: Context: Retrieval-augmented code generation relies on cross-file repository context, but retrieved snippets may come from obsolete project states.
  Objectives: We study whether temporally stale repository snippets act as harmless noise or actively induce current-state-incompatible code.
  Methods: We conduct a controlled diagnostic study on a curated 17-sample set of production-helper signature changes from five Python repositories. For each sample, we compare current-only, stale-only, no-retrieval, and mixed current/stale retrieval conditions under prompts that hide commit freshness and expected current signatures.
  Results: Under neutralized prompts, stale-only retrieval induces stale helper references on 15/17 Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct samples and 13/17 gpt-4.1-mini samples, corresponding to 88.2 and 76.5 percentage-point increases over current-only retrieval. No retrieval produces zero stale references but only 1/17 passing completions. The two models share 75.0% Jaccard overlap among stale-triggering samples, and mixed conditions show that adding valid current evidence largely rescues stale-only failures.
  Conclusion: Temporal validity of retrieved repository context is a distinct diagnostic variable for Code RAG robustness: stale context can actively bias models toward obsolete repository state rather than merely removing useful evidence.

</details>


### [67] [Not All RAGs Are Created Equal: A Component-Wise Empirical Study for Software Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14503)
*Qiang Ke,Yanjie Zhao,Hongjin Leng,Shengming Zhao,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对RAG系统在软件工程任务中的各组件进行了系统性实证研究，评估了21种模型和方法，发现检索器侧组件（尤其是检索算法）对系统性能的影响大于生成器模型的选择，且经典词法检索器BM25在多项任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RAG在软件工程中日益普及，但其组件的优化配置缺乏系统性指导，导致实践者需要进行昂贵且随意的实验探索。

Method: 对RAG流水线进行组件级剖析，系统评估了4种查询处理技术、7种检索模型（含稀疏、稠密和混合范式）、4种上下文精炼方法及6种不同的生成器，在代码生成、代码摘要和代码修复3项核心软件工程任务上进行测试。

Result: 检索器侧组件（尤其是检索算法）对系统最终性能的影响通常大于生成器模型的选择；经典词法检索器BM25在不同任务中展现出异常稳健的性能。

Conclusion: 为研究人员和实践者提供了基于数据的实用路线图，明确了在构建面向软件工程的高效RAG系统时应优先优化的关键方向。

Abstract: While Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is increasingly adopted to ground Large Language Models (LLMs) in software artifacts, the optimal configuration of its components remains an open question for software engineering (SE) tasks. The lack of systematic guidance forces practitioners into costly, ad-hoc experimentation. This paper presents a comprehensive, component-wise empirical study that dissects the RAG pipeline, evaluating over 21 distinct models and methods. Our study systematically isolates and evaluates 4 query processing techniques, 7 retrieval models spanning sparse, dense, and hybrid paradigms, 4 context refinement methods, and 6 distinct generators. We test these components on a suite of 3 core SE tasks: code generation, summarization, and repair. Our empirical findings reveal a crucial insight: the retriever-side components, particularly the choice of the retrieval algorithm, often exert a more significant influence on final system performance than the selection of the generator model. Strikingly, the classic lexical retriever BM25 demonstrates exceptionally robust performance across diverse tasks. Our analysis provides a practical, data-driven roadmap for researchers and practitioners, offering clear guidance on prioritizing optimization efforts when constructing effective RAG systems for software engineering contexts.

</details>


### [68] [Viverra: Text-to-Code with Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14972)
*Haoze Wu,Rocky Klopfenstein,Keith Farkas,Nina Narodytska*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Viverra是一个系统，它能自动为LLM生成的C代码附上经形式化验证的断言（assertions），通过组合式有界模型检查器验证代码正确性，从而帮助开发者理解代码。在18个编程任务和400+参与者的用户研究中，Viverra高效生成了带验证断言的代码，并显著提升了用户的代码理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Text-to-Code生成代码缺乏正确性保证，人工审查和测试耗时费力，可能抵消AI编码工具带来的生产力提升。

Method: Viverra首先利用LLM根据自然语言任务描述合成C程序及候选断言（表达安全性和正确性属性），然后通过组合式有界模型检查器（portfolio of bounded model checkers）以组合式（compositional）和尽力而为（best-effort）的方式验证这些断言。

Result: 在18个多样化编程任务上，Viverra能高效生成带验证断言的代码；在400+参与者的用户研究中，这些断言显著提升了用户在代码理解任务上的表现。

Conclusion: Viverra通过形式化验证自动为LLM生成代码附加可信注释，有效帮助开发者理解代码，为解决Text-to-Code正确性不可保证的问题提供了可行的自动化方案。

Abstract: A fundamental limitation of Text-to-Code is that no guarantee can be obtained about the correctness of the generated code. Therefore, to ensure its correctness, the generated code still has to be reviewed, tested, and maintained by developers. However, parsing through LLM-generated code can be tedious and time-consuming, potentially negating the productivity gains promised by AI-coding tools. To address this challenge, we present Viverra, a system that automatically produces formally verified annotations alongside generated code to aid user's understanding of the generated program. Given a natural-language task description, Viverra prompts an LLM to synthesize a C program together with candidate assertions expressing safety and correctness properties. It then verifies those assertions in a compositional and best-effort manner via a portfolio of bounded model checkers. Evaluation on 18 diverse programming tasks suggests that Viverra can efficiently generate code with verified assertions, and that these assertions improve users' performance on code-comprehension tasks in a user study with more than 400 participants.

</details>


### [69] [Remember Your Trace: Memory-Guided Long-Horizon Agentic Framework for Consistent and Hierarchical Repository-Level Code Documentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14563)
*Suyoung Bae,Jaehoon Lee,Changkyu Choi,YunSeok Choi,Jee-Hyong Lee*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MemDocAgent是一个长时程智能体框架，通过依赖感知遍历引导和记忆引导的智能体交互，在单一上下文中为整个代码仓库生成结构化文档，优于现有开源和闭源基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级代码文档生成方法独立处理各组件，导致冗余检索、描述冲突且输出缺乏层次结构，亟需一种统一上下文、感知依赖关系的文档生成方法。

Method: 提出MemDocAgent框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 依赖感知遍历引导——预定义遵循依赖关系和粒度层次的遍历顺序；(2) 记忆引导的智能体交互——智能体通过读写验证操作与共享内存RepoMemory交互，累积先前工作轨迹。

Result: 在多标准深度评估中，MemDocAgent在开源和闭源基线方法上均取得最佳性能，并在真实软件开发工作流中展示了实际应用价值。

Conclusion: MemDocAgent通过将仓库级文档生成整合到单一上下文中，利用依赖感知遍历和共享记忆机制，有效解决了现有方法的冗余和冲突问题，具有实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Automated code documentation is essential for modern software development, providing the contextual grounding that both human developers and coding agents rely on to navigate large codebases. Existing repository-level approaches process components independently, causing redundant retrieval and conflicting descriptions across documents while producing outputs that lack hierarchical structure. Therefore, we propose MemDocAgent, a long-horizon agentic framework that generates documentation within a single, integrated context spanning the entire repository. It combines two components: (i) Dependency-Aware Traversal Guiding that predetermines a traversal order respecting dependency and granularity hierarchies; (ii) Memory-Guided Agentic Interaction, in which the agent interacts with RepoMemory, a shared memory accumulating prior work traces through read, write, and verify operations. Through an in-depth multi-criteria evaluation, MemDocAgent achieves the best performance over both open and closed-source baselines and demonstrates practical applicability in real software development workflows.

</details>


### [70] [Mining Subscenario Refactoring Opportunities in Behaviour-Driven Software Test Suites: ML Classifiers and LLM-Judge Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14568)
*Ali Hassaan Mughal,Noor Fatima,Muhammad Bilal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一套自动化流程，用于从BDD测试套件中发现、排序并映射可重构的重复步骤子序列（切片），基于339个仓库的Gherkin语料库，使用XGBoost分类器达到F1=0.891，优于规则基线和LLM法官，并量化了三类重构模式（文件内Background、仓库内可复用场景、跨组织共享步骤）在生态系统中的流行率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BDD测试套件中存在大量重复步骤子序列，而此前没有自动化方法判断哪些重复子序列值得提取，也没有机制将候选序列映射到已有的三种发布重构模式（文件内Background、仓库内可复用场景调用、跨组织共享高层步骤）。

Method: 使用339个仓库的Gherkin语料库，对长度L∈[2,18]的连续L步窗口进行切片；通过SBERT+UMAP+HDBSCAN聚类恢复释义等价的切片；三位标注者按书面规范对分层采样的200个切片进行标注；使用XGBoost训练提取价值分类器（5折交叉验证），并与规则基线和两种开源LLM法官进行比较。

Result: 共产生5,382,249个切片，聚类为692,020个重复模式；三位标注者Fleiss' kappa=0.56（提取价值）和0.79（机制分类）；XGBoost分类器F1=0.891（95% CI [0.852, 0.927]），优于规则基线（F1=0.836, p=0.017）和最佳LLM法官（F1=0.728, p<1e-4）；75.0%、59.5%、11.7%的场景分别存在文件内Background、仓库内可复用场景、跨组织共享步骤的候选。

Conclusion: 释义鲁棒的子场景发现方法能够生成BDD重构机会的全语料库普查；管道、分类器预测、标注池和规范均已Apache-2.0协议开源发布。

Abstract: Context. Behaviour-Driven Development (BDD) software test suites accumulate duplicated step subsequences. Three published refactoring patterns are available (within-file Background, within-repo reusable-scenario invocation, cross-organisational shared higher-level step), but no prior work automates which recurring subsequences are worth extracting or which mechanism applies. Objective. Rank recurring step subsequences ("slices") by refactoring suitability (extraction-worthy), pre-map each to one of the three patterns, and quantify prevalence across the public BDD ecosystem. Method. Every contiguous L-step window (L in [2, 18]) in a 339-repository / 276-upstream-owner Gherkin corpus is keyed by paraphrase-robust cluster identifiers and counted under three scopes. Sentence-BERT (SBERT) / Uniform Manifold Approximation and Projection (UMAP) / Hierarchical Density-Based Clustering (HDBSCAN) recovers paraphrase-equivalent slices. Three authors label a stratified 200-slice pool against a written rubric. An eXtreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) extraction-worthy classifier trained under 5-fold cross-validation is compared with a tuned rule baseline and two open-weight Large Language Model (LLM) judges. Results. The miner produces 5,382,249 slices collapsing to 692,020 recurring patterns. Three-author Fleiss' kappa = 0.56 (extraction-worthy) and 0.79 (mechanism). The classifier reaches out-of-fold F1 = 0.891 (95% CI [0.852, 0.927]), outperforming both the rule baseline (F1 = 0.836, p = 0.017) and the better LLM judge (F1 = 0.728, p < 1e-4). 75.0%, 59.5%, and 11.7% of scenarios carry a within-file Background, within-repo reusable-scenario, or cross-organisational shared-step candidate. Conclusion. Paraphrase-robust subscenario discovery yields a corpus-wide census of BDD refactoring opportunities; pipeline, classifier predictions, labelled pool, and rubric are released under Apache-2.0.

</details>


### [71] [Agentic AI in Industry: Adoption Level and Deployment Barriers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14675)
*Spyridon Alvanakis Apostolou,Jan Bosch,Helena Holmström Olsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对16位来自12家不同规模与领域公司的从业者进行质性访谈，本文刻画了当前工业界对Agentic AI的采用现状，提出了一个六级成熟度框架。研究发现7家公司处于Level 1（AI助手）、4家处于Level 2（AI补偿器）、仅1家达到Level 3（多智能体编排）。核心发现是**能力-部署验证鸿沟**：四家公司虽具备较高水平的实验性AI能力，但因缺乏足够的输出验证机制而无法集成到生产流程中，人类-in-the-loop是唯一可信的验证手段。该鸿沟由四个障碍塑造：LLM上下文窗口限制、专有语言与协议的低性能、非确定性与资格标准的不兼容、以及数据保密顾虑。由此衍生出信息不对称和资格缺失两个相互依赖的维度，构成了工业级Agentic集成的核心开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Agentic AI正进入软件工程工作流，但关于工业组织如何实际采用这些系统的实证证据极为匮乏。本研究旨在填补这一空白，通过质性研究揭示真实工业环境中的采用现状、成熟度分布及关键障碍。

Method: 质性访谈研究。对16位来自12家不同规模和领域公司的从业者进行半结构化访谈，基于已有AI驱动组织的成熟度框架，提出六级成熟度模型对参与公司进行分类，并通过主题分析识别核心模式和障碍。

Result: 7家公司处于Level 1（AI助手）、4家处于Level 2（AI补偿器）、1家达到Level 3（多智能体编排），大型和安全监管严格的公司在采用上最为先进。核心发现是能力-部署验证鸿沟：四家公司拥有高级实验性能力但因缺乏验证机制无法投入生产。四个障碍为：LLM上下文窗口限制、专有语言/协议性能不足、非确定性与资格标准不兼容、数据保密顾虑。由此提炼出信息不对称和资格缺失两个相互依赖的维度。

Conclusion: 当前工业界Agentic AI的采用仍处于早期阶段（多数在Level 1-2），存在显著的能力-部署验证鸿沟。要实现从实验到生产的跨越，必须解决信息不对称和资格缺失这两个核心问题。该鸿沟的消除是工业级Agentic集成的关键开放挑战。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are entering software engineering workflows, yet empirical evidence on how industrial organizations actually adopt them remains sparse. We present a qualitative interview study with sixteen practitioners across twelve companies of varying size and domain. This study characterizes the current agentic AI adoption state of these companies, employing a six-level maturity framework adapted from established AI-driven organizations. The findings reveal that seven companies operate at Level~1 (AI Assistants), four companies at Level~2 (AI Compensators), and only one in Level~3 (Multi-Agent Orchestration), with large and safety-regulated organizations among the most advanced adopters. The primary finding is a capability-deployment verification gap, four companies demonstrated higher-level experimental AI capabilities but cannot integrate them into production workflows because adequate output verification mechanisms are absent, leaving human-in-the-loop as the only trusted verification mechanism. This gap is shaped by four recurring barriers: context window of LLMs constraints especially when diverse knowledge aggregation is needed, under-performance on proprietary programming languages and protocols, non-determinism incompatible with qualification standards, and data confidentiality concerns. Two interdependent dimensions of this gap emerge from these findings (information asymmetry and qualification absence) framing a core open problem for industrial agentic integration.

</details>


### [72] [Towards In-Depth Root Cause Localization for Microservices with Multi-Agent Recursion-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14866)
*Lingzhe Zhang,Tong Jia,Kangjin Wang,Chiming Duan,Minghua He,Rongqian Wang,Xi Peng,Meiling Wang,Gong Zhang,Renhai Chen,Ying Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RCLAgent框架，通过多智能体递归思维与并行推理机制解决微服务系统根因定位中现有LLM方法面临的上下文爆炸和串行推理结构问题，在多个公开基准上显著提升了定位准确性和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统因动态交互和运行时环境演化而故障频发，现有传统机器学习和深度学习方法可解释性差且跨部署迁移能力弱，而基于LLM的方法存在上下文爆炸（稀释关键证据，降低定位精度）和串行推理结构（阻碍深度因果探索，损害推理效率）两大根本性限制。

Method: RCLAgent框架将诊断过程沿Trace图分解，为每个Span分配专用智能体（Dedicated Agent），并依据图拓扑结构以递归并行方式组织智能体协作，最终通过综合根级诊断报告（Root-Level Diagnosis Report）和全局证据图（Global Evidence Graph）得出根因诊断结果。

Result: 在多个公开基准上的大量实验表明，RCLAgent在定位准确性和推理效率两方面均持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: RCLAgent通过模仿人类SRE的根因定位实践，以多智能体递归并行推理有效克服了现有LLM方法的上下文爆炸和串行推理缺陷，为微服务系统根因定位提供了一种更准确高效的解决方案。

Abstract: As modern microservice systems grow increasingly complex due to dynamic interactions and evolving runtime environments, they experience failures with rising frequency. Ensuring system reliability therefore critically depends on accurate root cause localization (RCL). While numerous traditional machine learning and deep learning approaches have been explored for this task, they often suffer from limited interpretability and poor transferability across deployments. More recently, large language model (LLM)-based methods have been proposed to address these issues. However, existing LLM-based approaches still face two fundamental limitations: context explosion, which dilutes critical evidence and degrades localization accuracy, and serial reasoning structures, which hinder deep causal exploration and impair inference efficiency. In this paper, we conduct a comprehensive study of both how human SREs perform root cause localization in practice and why existing LLM-based methods fall short. Motivated by these findings, we introduce RCLAgent, an in-depth root cause localization framework for microservice systems that realizes multi-agent recursion-of-thought with parallel reasoning. RCLAgent decomposes the diagnostic process along the trace graph by assigning each span to a Dedicated Agent and organizing agents recursively and in parallel according to the graph topology, with the final diagnosis obtained by synthesizing the Root-Level Diagnosis Report and the Global Evidence Graph. Extensive experiments on multiple public benchmarks demonstrate that RCLAgent consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both localization accuracy and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [73] [Veritas: A Semantically Grounded Agentic Framework for Memory Corruption Vulnerability Detection in Binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15097)
*Xinran Zheng,Alfredo Pesoli,Marco Valleri,Suman Jana,Lorenzo Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Veritas是一个面向二进制内存损坏漏洞检测的语义驱动的框架，通过静态切片器、双视图LLM检测器和多智能体验证器协同工作，在真实世界二进制漏洞基准上达到90%召回率，零误报（在623个候选中），并发现了一个此前未知的Apple CVE漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剥离二进制文件中检测内存损坏漏洞需要从底层、有损表示中恢复对象语义、过程间传播和可行触发条件，现有方法缺乏内存相关语义和运行时可行性证据的支撑。

Method: Veritas结合三个组件：1) 基于RetDec提升的LLVM IR的静态切片器，重构值流关系并生成精简的见证支持流对象；2) 双视图LLM检测器，利用反编译C代码和选择性LLVM IR逐步推理；3) 多智能体验证器，通过调试器可见构件和运行时证据检查假设。

Result: 在真实世界二进制漏洞基准上达到90%召回率；623个候选的穷举验证子集无假阳性；额外审计发现2个确认的假阳性；在实际应用中发现了此前未知的Apple漏洞并获得CVE编号。

Conclusion: 语义驱动是实用二进制漏洞检测的可行设计原则，将语义信息与运行时证据结合能够显著提高检测的准确性和可靠性。

Abstract: Detecting memory corruption vulnerabilities in stripped binaries requires recovering object semantics, interprocedural propagation, and feasible triggers from low-level, lossy representations. Recent LLM-based approaches improve code understanding, but reliable detection still requires grounding in memory-relevant semantics and runtime feasibility evidence. We present Veritas, a semantically grounded framework for binary memory corruption vulnerability detection. Veritas combines a static slicer over RetDec-lifted LLVM IR, a dual-view LLM detector that reasons step by step over grounded flows using decompiled C and selective LLVM IR, and a multi-agent validator that checks hypotheses against debugger-visible artifacts and runtime evidence. The slicer reconstructs value-flow relations from LLVM-IR facts, including def-use, calls, returns, globals, and pointer operations, and emits compact witness-backed flow objects. The detector uses these artifacts to reason about control flow, bounds, and object correspondence without rediscovering whole-binary propagation. The validator confirms or rejects candidates through guided debugging, breakpoint inspection, and memory-checking oracles. We implement Veritas as a modular pipeline and evaluate it on a curated benchmark of real-world binary vulnerability cases. Veritas achieves 90\% recall. For false-positive assessment, we exhaustively validate and manually verify 623 detector candidates and audit additional candidates from larger cases. The exhaustive subset produces no false positives, while the additional audit identifies two confirmed false positives. In a real-world application, Veritas discovered a previously unknown Apple vulnerability that was confirmed and assigned a CVE. These results support semantic grounding as an operational design principle for practical binary vulnerability detection.

</details>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [74] [WirelessSenseLLM: Zero-Shot Human Activity Understanding by Bridging Wireless Signals and Human Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14070)
*Mahmuda Keya,Sneh Pillai,Jiawei Yuan,Kai Zeng,Long Jiao*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出WirelessSenseLLM，一种利用大语言模型实现从无分割Wi-Fi CSI信号中进行零样本人体运动理解的语言驱动框架，通过CSI-to-Language适配器和跨模态投影机制弥合模态差异，在零样本动作理解、语言推理质量和多人运动解释上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CSI的人体运动理解方法高度依赖精确的信号分割和预定义动作标签，在零样本场景下适用性受限。作者希望突破这一限制，实现从无分割无线信号中直接进行零样本人体运动理解。

Method: 提出WirelessSenseLLM框架，核心包括：(1) CSI-to-Language适配器，将时序CSI特征映射到语言对齐的语义空间；(2) 跨模态投影机制，弥合CSI特征与语言嵌入之间的模态差异；(3) 利用大语言模型进行细粒度自然语言描述生成与下游推理。

Result: 零样本动作理解准确率92%、F1-score 91%；语言推理质量在事实性和推理能力上分别提升30%和15%；多人运动解释较先前方法平均提升12.33%。

Conclusion: WirelessSenseLLM能有效从CSI信号中实现鲁棒且可解释的人体运动理解，解决了模态不匹配和重叠动作两大技术挑战，展现了在零样本场景下的强大性能。

Abstract: There is growing interest in enabling wireless sensing systems to interpret human motion from unsegmented wireless signals; however, existing CSI-based applications rely heavily on accurate signal segmentation and predefined action labels, limiting their applicability in zero-shot scenarios. We present WirelessSenseLLM, a language-driven framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) to enable zero-shot human motion understanding from unsegmented Wi-Fi Channel State Information (CSI). To bridge the modality gap between time-series CSI and discrete language representations, we introduce a CSI-to-Language Adapter and a cross-modal projection mechanism that maps CSI features into a language-aligned semantic space. This design enables the generation of fine-grained natural language descriptions of sequential and overlapping human motions, supporting downstream reasoning without segmented training data. We address two core technical challenges: modality mismatch between CSI features and language embeddings, and overlapping actions in unsegmented CSI streams. Extensive experiments demonstrate strong performance in zero-shot action understanding (92% accuracy and 91% F1-score), language-based reasoning quality (30% factual and 15% reasoning improvements), and multi-person motion explanation with an average 12.33% improvement over prior methods. These results highlight WirelessSenseLLM's effectiveness for robust and interpretable human motion understanding from CSI signals.

</details>


### [75] [Geographic Patterns in I2P Peer Selection: An Empirical Network Topology Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14435)
*Siddique Abubakr Muntaka,Jess Kropczynski,Jacques Bou Abdo,Murat Ozer*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: I2P路由拓扑中不存在显著的地理同质性，节点选择呈现高度随机的地理混合，这与I2P的设计原则一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究地理因素是否系统性影响I2P路由拓扑，以理解其安全性与性能之间的权衡。

Method: 对327个路由器和254条连接数据(SWARM-I2P)进行同配性分析、社区检测和排列检验。

Result: 同配性系数r=0.017(p=0.222)，无显著地理同质性；同国连接占比11.1%接近随机期望10.91%；社区检测发现110个高度模块化群组(Q=0.972)，与地理分布的匹配度中等(NMI=0.521)。

Conclusion: I2P的聚合节点选择导致高度异质、随机的地理混合，为理解性能-匿名权衡提供了基础。

Abstract: The Invisible Internet Project (I2P) routes data via encrypted, decentralized tunnels. Peer selection can significantly affect security and performance. This empirical study examines whether geographic location systematically influences I2P's routing topology. Consistent with I2P's design principles, which include avoiding multiple peers from the same /16 IP subnet to maximize anonymity, we conducted assortativity analysis, community detection, and permutation testing on data from 327 routers and 254 connections (SWARM-I2P). We found a network-level absence of significant geographic homophily. The assortativity coefficient was r = 0.017 (p = 0.222). Same-country connections (11.1%) are statistically near random expectation (10.91%). Community detection found 110 highly modular groups (Q = 0.972) only moderately aligned geographically (NMI = 0.521). We conclude that aggregate peer selection in I2P leads to a highly heterogeneous, random geographical mixing, providing a foundation for understanding the performance-anonymity tradeoff.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

### [76] [Memory Forensics Techniques for Automated Detection and Analysis of Go Malware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14020)
*Hala Ali,Andrew Case,Irfan Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向Go语言二进制文件的运行时内存取证分析框架，通过解析Go内部结构、ABI感知的逆向分析和goroutine栈分析，成功从内存中恢复类型元数据、函数信息、执行路径和运行时参数值，并在BRICKSTORM后门、Obscura勒索软件等实际恶意样本中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Go二进制文件的静态分析和逆向工程工具仅能处理编译时产物，无法恢复仅存在于内存中的运行时执行状态和痕迹，导致对Go恶意软件的取证分析存在关键空白。

Method: 解析Go内部运行时结构以重建类型和函数元数据；通过ABI感知的逆向分析从调用点推导执行路径和参数值；分析goroutine栈以识别正在执行的函数并恢复运行时参数值；所有能力实现为Volatility 3插件。

Result: 在BRICKSTORM后门、Obscura勒索软件、Pantegana RAT等真实恶意样本中，成功恢复C2端点、持久化机制、加密密钥、赎金票据和执行状态，包括已发布的威胁情报中缺失的关键运行时痕迹。

Conclusion: 该框架有效弥补了现有Go恶意软件分析工具在运行时状态恢复方面的不足，能够从内存中提取静态分析无法获取的关键证据，提升了Go二进制文件的取证能力。

Abstract: The Go programming language has become increasingly popular among malware developers due to its ability to produce statically linked, cross-platform executables that challenge traditional analysis techniques. These binaries embed a substantial runtime and compiler-generated metadata and are compiled with aggressive optimizations that discard type information for function parameters and local variables. Go's design further complicates analysis by representing strings as pointer-length pairs rather than null-terminated sequences, employing a caller-allocated stack model that obscures argument boundaries, and fragmenting program state across concurrent goroutines. Although existing static analysis and reverse engineering tools provide Go-specific support, they remain limited to compile-time artifacts and cannot recover runtime execution state and artifacts that persist solely in memory. To address this gap, we present the first memory forensics framework for runtime analysis of Go binaries. By parsing Go's internal structures, our framework reconstructs type and function metadata, recovers heap-allocated and static strings, and distinguishes application-level functions. Through ABI-aware backward analysis, it derives execution paths and argument values from call sites. To capture runtime state beyond what static analysis reveals, it analyzes goroutine stacks to identify actively executing functions and recover their runtime argument values. We implemented all capabilities as Volatility 3 plugins and evaluated them against malware seen in recent incidents, such as the BRICKSTORM backdoor, Obscura ransomware, and Pantegana RAT, as well as open-source samples for reproducibility. The framework successfully recovered C2 endpoints, persistence mechanisms, encryption keys, ransom notes, and execution state, including critical runtime artifacts that were absent from published threat intelligence.

</details>


### [77] [ExploitBench: A Capability Ladder Benchmark for LLM Cybersecurity Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14153)
*Seunghyun Lee,David Brumley*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ExploitBench是一个能力分级的利用基准测试，将漏洞利用分解为16个可测量的能力标志，从代码覆盖到任意代码执行。在41个V8漏洞上的测试表明，公共前沿模型能常规触发崩溃但无法实现任意代码执行，而私有模型在约半数案例中实现了任意代码执行。利用构建正成为一项新兴的前沿能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全基准将崩溃视为利用成功，这种二元结果忽略了从触发漏洞到构建可重用原语和控制权的关键步骤。作者希望创建一个能精细测量模型在漏洞利用全链条中各个阶段能力的分级基准。

Method: 将漏洞利用分解为16个可测量标志，从覆盖、崩溃到沙箱原语、任意读写、控制流劫持和任意代码执行。每个能力由确定性预言机验证：使用每次运行的随机挑战-响应验证原语、基于真实二进制文件的差异执行衡量进度、以及信号处理程序证明验证代码执行。在41个V8漏洞上实例化，设置三个实验臂：<模型,环境>主测量、<模型,环境,自适应指导>、<模型,环境,工具集>消融实验。

Result: 公共前沿模型（8个）在到达漏洞代码和触发崩溃方面表现常规，但无法实现任意代码执行。私有模型在约半数案例中实现了任意代码执行。结果显示了公共模型与私有模型之间存在明显的能力分水岭。

Conclusion: 针对加固目标的利用构建是一项新兴的前沿能力，ExploitBench作为能力分级基准能有效区分不同模型在漏洞利用全链条上的能力差异。

Abstract: Exploitation is not a binary event. It is a ladder of acquiring progressive capabilities, from executing a single buggy line of code to taking full control of the target. However, existing LLM security benchmarks treat a crash as exploitation success. That single binary outcome collapses the hard parts of exploitation: the transition from triggering a bug to constructing reusable primitives and control.
  We present ExploitBench, a capability-graded benchmark that decomposes exploitation into 16 measurable flags, from coverage and crash through sandbox primitives, arbitrary read/write, control-flow hijack, and arbitrary code execution. Each capability is verified by a deterministic oracle that uses a per-run randomized challenge-response for primitives, differential execution against ground-truth binaries to measure progress, and a signal-handler proof for code execution.
  We instantiate ExploitBench on 41 V8 bugs because V8 is both widely deployed and exploitation-hardened. We report three arms: <model,env> as the primary measurement of model-environment capability, <model,env, adaptive coaching> as a secondary arm that adds adaptive coaching to test whether targeted feedback shifts outcomes, and <model,env,harness> as an ablation that swaps in the model's native CLI to check whether vendor-side optimizations increase exploitation capabilities.
  Our results show a sharp capability split between publicly deployed frontier models and the private frontier. Across the 8 publicly deployed models tested, reaching the vulnerable code and triggering a crash is routine, but arbitrary code execution is not. The private model shows arbitrary code execution on approximately half. Overall, results suggest that exploit construction against hardened targets is an emerging frontier capability.

</details>


### [78] [On the (non-)resilience of encrypted controllers to covert attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14230)
*Philipp Binfet,Janis Adamek,Moritz Schulze Darup*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明了公钥同态加密（HE）无法单独解决加密控制系统中的完整性攻击问题，因为其固有的可塑性使得攻击者可利用同态性质实施隐蔽攻击（即使不知道未加密模型）。但通过引入可验证计算（VC）技术，可以实现渐近安全性且无通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密控制领域近年尝试修改底层HE方案以同时实现机密性和对某些完整性攻击的韧性。本文旨在揭示单纯的公钥HE方案在解决加密控制完整性问题上存在根本性局限，并寻找可行的补充方案。

Method: 理论分析证明公钥HE的可塑性导致加密控制系统无法抵御隐蔽攻击；提出基于可验证计算（VC）的补充方法，与现代同态密码系统集成，实现渐近安全且零通信开销。

Result: 证明了即使采用加密控制，NCS仍易受隐蔽攻击，且攻击者可无需知晓未加密模型；提出的VC方法可实现渐近安全性且不增加通信开销。

Conclusion: 公钥HE方案本身无法解决加密控制系统的完整性攻击问题，必须通过可验证计算等互补技术来获得韧性保障。

Abstract: The security of networked control systems (NCS) is receiving increasing attention from both cyber-security and system-theoretic perspectives. The former focuses on classical IT security goals such as confidentiality, integrity, and availability of process data, while the latter investigates tailored attacks (and detection schemes), including covert and zero-dynamics attacks. Confidentiality in control systems can, for instance, be achieved by securely outsourcing the evaluation of the controller to third-party platforms, such as cloud services. The underlying technology enabling such secure computation often is homomorphic encryption (HE).
  Recent works in encrypted control have proposed modifications to underlying HE schemes to achieve not only confidentiality but also resilience to certain types of integrity attacks. While extensions in this direction are desirable in principle, we show that the integrity problem in encrypted control cannot be solved by public-key HE schemes alone due to their inherent malleability. In other words, the same homomorphisms that enable encrypted control % in the first place can be leveraged not only constructively but also destructively. More precisely, we demonstrate that NCS are vulnerable to covert attacks, even when encrypted control is employed. Remarkably, this remains possible without knowledge of an unencrypted model.
  Yet, resilience to such attacks can still be achieved through complementary techniques. We present an approach based on verifiable computation that integrates with modern homomorphic cryptosystems and is asymptotically secure while incurring no communication overhead.

</details>


### [79] [DSTAN-Med: Dual-Channel Spatiotemporal Attention with Physiological Plausibility Filtering for False Data Injection Attack Detection in IoT-Based Medical Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14165)
*Md Mehedi Hasan,Rafiqul Islam,Md Zakir Hossain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出DSTAN-Med框架，通过双通道注意力机制解耦空间与时间维度特征，结合生理合理性滤波（PPF）约束，有效检测IoMT传感器流中的虚假数据注入攻击。在三个数据集上相比最强基线TranAD敏感度提升7.4-8.3个百分点（p<0.01）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法将跨传感器空间关联与时间依赖在共享隐空间中混叠，无法解耦FDI攻击同时注入的空间和时间特征；且缺乏利用领域知识约束输出的机制，无法排除生理上不可能的攻击模式。

Method: DSTAN-Med包括：1) 双通道注意力机制（DAM），在正交张量轴上分别运行传感器级自注意力（SWA）和时间级自注意力（TWA）；2) 残差1D-CNN模块提取局部时间特征；3) 零参数生理合理性滤波（PPF），抑制违反领域知识边界的攻击特征。

Result: 在PhysioNet/CinC 2012、MIMIC-III Waveform、WESAD三个数据集上，DSTAN-Med相比最强Transformer基线TranAD敏感度提升7.4-8.3个百分点（p<0.01，McNemar检验+Holm-Bonferroni校正）。PPF独立贡献3.1-4.2个百分点精确率提升，敏感性损失极小。消融实验中移除残差连接导致敏感度下降14个百分点。

Conclusion: DSTAN-Med通过解耦空间-时间特征并引入生理合理性约束，显著提升了IoMT环境下FDI攻击检测性能，各组件均不可或缺。

Abstract: False data injection (FDI) attacks on Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) sensor streams falsify vital signs in transit, threatening patient safety and defeating clinical monitoring systems that lack cyber-physical anomaly detection capability. Existing deep learning detectors conflate inter-sensor spatial correlations with temporal dependencies in a shared latent space, preventing disentanglement of the distinct spatial and temporal signatures that FDI attacks imprint simultaneously; no current method exploits domain knowledge to constrain outputs against physiologically impossible attack patterns. We propose DSTAN-Med, a supervised framework comprising a Dual-channel Attention Mechanism (DAM) that routes multivariate sensor windows through independent sensor-wise (SWA) and time-wise (TWA) self-attention pathways operating on orthogonal tensor axes, a residual 1D-CNN block for local temporal feature extraction, and a zero-parameter Physiological Plausibility Filter (PPF) that suppresses attack signatures violating domain-knowledge bounds. Evaluated across three IoMT sensor datasets - PhysioNet/CinC 2012 (ICU vital signs), MIMIC-III Waveform (continuous ICU waveforms), and WESAD (wearable biosensor signals) - DSTAN-Med achieves mean sensitivity gains of 7.4-8.3 percentage points over the strongest Transformer baseline (TranAD), with improvements significant at p < 0.01 (McNemar's test, Holm-Bonferroni correction). The PPF contributes independent precision gains of 3.1-4.2 percentage points at negligible sensitivity cost across all three corpora. Ablation studies confirm that each component is individually necessary; removal of residual connections alone reduces sensitivity by 14.0 percentage points. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/mehedi93hasan/DSTAN-MED.

</details>


### [80] [Web Agents Should Adopt the Plan-Then-Execute Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14290)
*Julien Piet,Annabella Chow,Yiwei Hou,Muxi Lyu,Sylvie Venuto,Jinhao Zhu,Raluca Ada Popa,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文论证ReAct（思考-行动循环）不是Web智能体的合适默认架构，提出应默认采用"计划-执行"范式：在观察运行时网页内容之前，先承诺一个任务特定的程序，然后执行之。原因是ReAct下所有网页内容（包括恶意注入）都会流入模型影响决策，而计划-执行将不可信数据限制在预定义执行图内，无法重新定义用户任务或生成新动作。80%的WebArena任务可通过纯编程计划完成。主要障碍是浏览器工具（点击、输入、滚动）具有页面依赖语义，规划时无法预知效果，需要将网站交互从点击/按键提升为任务级操作的类型化接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReAct已成为LLM智能体的默认架构，但在Web场景下，网页内容混合了多方输入（卖家列表、客户评论、赞助广告），ReAct让所有内容直接影响模型的下一个动作决策，为提示注入提供了直接路径以劫持智能体的控制流。因此需要探索更安全的默认架构。

Method: 提出计划-执行（plan-then-execute）范式：在观察运行时内容之前先提交任务特定程序，然后执行。分析WebArena基准测试，评估所有任务与计划-执行的兼容性；识别浏览器工具（click/type/scroll）的页面依赖语义是规划的主要障碍。

Result: WebArena中所有任务都与计划-执行兼容，80%可仅通过纯编程计划完成（无需运行时LLM子程序）。主要障碍在于浏览器工具具有页面依赖含义，规划层是短视的——智能体只能看到当前页面的动作，后续动作只有执行后才能出现。

Conclusion: Web任务默认不需要反应性（ReAct），需要的是类型化、完整、可审计的网站API。将网站交互从点击/按键提升为任务级操作是基础设施问题而非建模问题。关闭这一差距需要类型化接口。

Abstract: ReAct has become the default architecture across LLM agents, and many existing web agents follow this paradigm. We argue that it is the wrong default for web agents. Instead, web agents should default to plan-then-execute: commit to a task-specific program before observing runtime web content, then execute it. The reason is that web content mixes inputs from many parties. An e-commerce product page may combine a seller's listing, customer reviews and sponsored advertisements. Under ReAct, all of this content flows into the model when deciding on the next action, creating a direct path for prompt injections to steer the agent's control flow. Plan-then-execute changes this boundary: untrusted data may influence values or branches inside a predefined execution graph, but it cannot redefine the user task or cause the model to synthesize new actions at runtime. We analyze WebArena, a popular web agent benchmark, and find that all tasks are compatible with plan-then-execute, while 80% can be completed with a purely programmatic plan, without any runtime LLM subroutine. We identify the main barrier to adopting plan-then-execute on the web: For it to work well, tools must map cleanly to semantic actions, with effects known before execution, so agents have enough information to plan. The web does not naturally expose that interface. Browser tools such as click, type, and scroll have page-dependent meanings. Planning at this layer is near-sighted: the agent can only see actions on the current page, and later actions appear only after it acts. Closing this gap requires typed interfaces that turn website interactions from clicks and keystrokes to task-level operations. This is an infrastructure problem, not a modeling problem. Web tasks do not need reactivity by default; they need typed, complete, auditable website APIs.

</details>


### [81] [The Great Pretender: A Stochasticity Problem in LLM Jailbreak](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14418)
*Jean-Philippe Monteuuis,Cong Chen,Jonathan Petit*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 现有越狱攻击方法存在评估不稳定问题，论文提出的ASR指标具有随机性导致结果不可比，作者提出CAS-eval评估框架和CAS-gen生成框架，可恢复因随机性导致的30个百分点的ASR下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到相同越狱提示词在多次攻击中的成功率不稳定（如声称80% ASR实际仅50%），且论文间ASR指标不可比、被系统性高估，因此探究随机性对攻击生成和评估的影响。

Method: 系统评估多种越狱攻击、不同规模/提供商的模型及多种评估器，分析随机性在攻击生成和评估阶段的影响；提出新指标（CAS）及两个框架：CAS-eval（一致性感知评估）和CAS-gen（一致性感知生成）。

Result: 发现当越狱提示词需要在多次尝试中连续成功时，ASR可下降高达30个百分点；CAS-gen框架可帮助现有越狱方法恢复这30个百分点的损失。

Conclusion: ASR作为主要评估指标不稳定且缺乏可重复性，需要引入一致性评估指标；CAS-eval和CAS-gen分别从评估和生成两个维度解决了该问题。

Abstract: "Oh-Oh, yes, I'm the great pretender. Pretending that I'm doing well. My need is such, I pretend too much..." summarizes the state in the area of jailbreak creation and evaluation. You find this method to generate adversarial attacks proposed by a reputable institution (e.g., BoN from Anthropic or Crescendo from Microsoft Research). However, this method does not deliver on the promise claimed in the paper despite having top ASR scores against industry-grade LLMs. You successfully generate the jailbreak prompts against your target (open) model. However, the generated jailbreak prompt works against the target model with a 50% consecutive success rate (5 out of 10 attempts) despite having an 80% ASR (on paper) on the latest closed-source model (with a guardrail system)! This observation leads us to think. First, Attack Success Rate (ASR), the primary metric for LLM jailbreak benchmarking, is not a stable quantity. Second, published ASR numbers are therefore systematically inflated and incomparable across papers. Therefore, we wonder "Why a successful jailbreak prompt does not perform consistently well against a target model on which the prompts have been optimized?". To answer this question, we study the impact of stochasticity not only during attack evaluation but also during attack generation. Our evaluation includes several jailbreak attacks, models (different sizes and providers), and judges. In addition, we propose a new metric and two new frameworks (CAS-eval and CAS-gen). Our evaluation framework, CAS-eval, shows that an attack can have an ASR drop of up to 30 percentage points when a jailbreak prompt needs to succeed on more than one attempt. Thankfully, our attack generation framework (CAS-gen) improves previous jailbreak methods and helps them recover this loss of 30 percentage points!

</details>


### [82] [MemLineage: Lineage-Guided Enforcement for LLM Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14421)
*Ciyan Ouyang,Rui Hou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MemLineage 是一种针对LLM智能体记忆的防御系统，通过将加密溯源和LLM中介的衍生谱系附加到每条记忆条目，将记忆中毒问题视为链式 custody 问题而非过滤问题，在保持有用记忆召回的同时阻止敏感操作被外部来源污染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明不可信内容可写入持久化智能体状态并在后续会话中作为指令重新进入，如何在保留有用记忆召回的同时阻止此类状态被用于证明敏感操作是核心系统问题。

Method: 采用六模块设计，基于RFC-6962 Merkle日志和Ed25519签名条目，构建加权衍生DAG记录检索条目对新记忆的影响，使用最大强边传播规则确保Untrusted-Path Persistence，敏感操作门拒绝从外部祖先推导的操作。

Result: 在确定性机制隔离框架中，MemLineage在所有三种记忆投毒工作负载下将ASR降至零，且亚毫秒级开销远低于LLM调用噪声。在AgentDojo桥接测试中，无防御和仅签名基线在六个银行对偶上全部失败，而MemLineage将所有严格AgentDojo ASR降至零。

Conclusion: MemLineage通过链式custody方法有效解决了LLM智能体记忆中毒问题，在保持功能性的同时实现了零ASR，且开销极小。

Abstract: We introduce MemLineage, a defense for LLM agent memory that attaches both cryptographic provenance and LLM-mediated derivation lineage to every entry. Recent and concurrent work shows that untrusted content can be written into persistent agent state and re-enter later sessions as an instruction; the remaining systems question is how to preserve useful memory recall while preventing such state from justifying sensitive actions. MemLineage treats this as a chain-of-custody problem rather than a filtering problem. It is a six-module design around an RFC-6962 Merkle log over per-principal Ed25519-signed entries: a weighted derivation DAG records which retrieved entries influenced each new memory, and a max-of-strong-edges propagation rule makes Untrusted-Path Persistence hold for any chain whose attribution edges remain above threshold. The sensitive-action gate then refuses dispatches whose active justification descends from an external ancestor, while still allowing benign recall. We evaluate three defense cells against three memory-poisoning workloads on a deterministic mechanism-isolation harness; MemLineage is the only configuration in that harness that drives all three columns to zero ASR, while sub-millisecond per-operation overhead keeps it well below the noise floor of any LLM call. A Codex-backed AgentDojo bridge further separates strong-model behavior from defense-layer behavior: under an intentionally vulnerable tool-output profile, no-defense and signature-only baselines fail on all six banking pairs, while all MemLineage rows reduce strict AgentDojo ASR to zero. The core deterministic artifacts are byte-equal CI-verified; hosted-model AgentDojo and live-model sweeps are recorded as auditable logs rather than byte-pinned artifacts.

</details>


### [83] [Exploiting LLM Agent Supply Chains via Payload-less Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14460)
*Xinyu Liu,Yukai Zhao,Xing Hu,Xin Xia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为语义合规劫持（SCH）的无载荷供应链攻击方法，利用LLM智能体的生成能力将恶意目标转化为自然语言合规规则，诱导智能体在运行时动态合成并执行未授权代码。实验表明，该方法在主流智能体框架和基础模型上实现了高达77.67%的机密性破坏成功率和67.33%的远程代码执行成功率，且因无显式恶意载荷，检测率为0.00%，成功绕过了现有安全扫描机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有审计机制虽能有效识别显式代码载荷和预定义威胁内容，但无法检测那些没有直接注入、而是通过智能体自身生成能力在运行时动态合成恶意行为的方式。本文旨在探索这一盲点，揭示智能体供应链中未被充分研究的攻击面。

Method: 提出语义合规劫持（SCH）攻击方法：将恶意目标转化为非结构化的自然语言指令，格式化为必要的合规规则，诱导智能体生成并执行未授权代码。同时引入多技能自动优化（MS-AO）进一步提升攻击效果。构建自动化评估管线，在包含3种主流智能体框架和3种不同基础模型的测试矩阵上进行实验。

Result: 在最脆弱配置下，SCH对机密性破坏的成功率达77.67%，对远程代码执行的成功率达67.33%。由于操纵后的技能文件不含可识别的AST签名和显式恶意意图，检测率为0.00%，完全规避了现有扫描工具。

Conclusion: 智能体供应链中存在一个被忽视的攻击面，需要从基于签名的检测模型转向语义意图验证机制。

Abstract: Autonomous agents powered by Large Language Models (LLMs) acquire external functionalities through third-party skills available in open marketplaces. Adopting these integrations broadens the potential attack surface, prompting a need for systematic security evaluation. Current auditing mechanisms are effective at identifying explicit code payloads and predefined threat contents through security scanning. These detection mechanisms are bypassed if malicious behaviors lack direct injection and are instead synthesized dynamically at runtime through the agent's inherent generative capabilities. Exploring this blind spot, we introduce Semantic Compliance Hijacking (SCH), a payload-less supply chain attack targeting autonomous coding environments. The SCH approach translates malicious goals into unstructured natural language instructions formatted as necessary compliance rules, leading the agent to generate and execute unauthorized code. To assess the real-world viability of this attack, we developed an automated pipeline to evaluate its effectiveness across a test matrix comprising three mainstream agent frameworks and three distinct foundation models using contextualized scenarios. The findings demonstrate the pervasive nature of this threat, with SCH achieving peak success rates of up to 77.67% for confidentiality breaches and 67.33% for Remote Code Execution (RCE) under the most vulnerable configurations. Furthermore, the introduction of Multi-Skill Automated Optimization (MS-AO) further boosted attack efficacy. By omitting recognizable Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) signatures and explicit harmful intents, the manipulated skill files maintained a 0.00% detection rate, evading current scanning tools. This research highlights an underexplored attack surface within agent supply chains, pointing to a necessary transition from signature-based detection models toward semantic intent validation.

</details>


### [84] [Defenses at Odds: Measuring and Explaining Defense Conflicts in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14514)
*Xiangtao Meng,Wenyu Chen,Chuanchao Zang,Xinyu Gao,Jianing Wang,Li Wang,Zheng Li,Shanqing Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首次系统研究大模型顺序部署中防御间的交叉交互，发现38.9%的序列存在风险加剧现象，通过机制分析定位到关键层的反对齐参数更新和激活模式反转，并提出冲突引导的层冻结缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在高风险场景中需同时管理多种风险，但现有防御仅在孤立的一次性部署假设下评估；实际中模型通过增量补丁更新，先前防御可能被后续防御破坏，这一问题尚未被系统研究。

Method: 评估144个有序序列（3个风险维度×3个模型家族），利用层表示散度、激活修补定位关键层，通过PCA轨迹分析和层间冲突分数量化防御间几何张力，并提出冲突引导层冻结策略。

Result: 38.9%的序列在原本防御的维度上出现风险加剧；冲突序列中关键层参数更新呈强反对齐，良性排序保持近正交；防御失效源于共享层激活模式反转。

Conclusion: 顺序部署中防御交互普遍存在且不对称、依赖顺序；层间冲突分数可解释激活反转；冲突引导层冻结能有效保留先前保护且不降低后续防御性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) deployed in high-stakes applications must simultaneously manage multiple risks, yet existing defenses are almost exclusively evaluated in isolation under a one-shot deployment assumption. In practice, providers patch models incrementally throughout their lifecycle-responding to newly exposed vulnerabilities or targeted data-removal requests without retraining from scratch. This raises a fundamental but underexplored question: does a later defense preserve the protections established by an earlier one? We present the first systematic study of cross-defense interactions under sequential deployment. Evaluating 144 ordered sequences across three risk dimensions and three model families, we find that 38.9% exhibit measurable risk exacerbation on the originally defended dimension. These interactions are highly asymmetric and order-dependent. To explain these phenomena, we conduct a mechanistic analysis on representative deployment sequences. Using layer-wise representational divergence and activation patching, we localize each defense to a compact set of critical layers. In conflicting sequences, the overlapping critical layers exhibit strongly anti-aligned parameter updates, whereas benign orderings maintain near-orthogonal updates. PCA trajectory analysis reveals that defense collapse stems from activation pattern reversals in these shared layers. We further introduce a layer-wise conflict score that quantifies the geometric tension between defense-induced activation subspaces, offering mechanistic insight into the observed reversals. Guided by this diagnosis, we propose conflict-guided layer freezing, a lightweight mitigation that selectively freezes high-conflict layers during sequential deployment, preserving prior protections without degrading secondary defense performance.

</details>


### [85] [Privacy Auditing with Zero (0) Training Run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14591)
*Tudor Cebere,Mathieu Even,Linus Bleistein,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Zero-Run隐私审计框架，利用两个固定数据集（已知成员和非成员）对已部署模型进行事后隐私审计，通过因果推断方法校正分布偏移带来的混淆效应，无需重新训练或随机化数据包含。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私审计方法需要干预式访问训练流程（如多次重训练或随机化数据包含），对于已部署的大型系统（如基础模型）往往不可行，亟需一种事后观测性的审计方法。

Method: 提出Zero-Run审计框架：(1) 将分布偏移与算法泄露的联合效应建模为自适应组合，进行保守的全局校正；(2) 基于观测数据条件化，调整逐点成员猜测，得到更锐化的实例依赖界限。两种方法均借鉴因果推断思想形式化混淆效应。

Result: 在合成数据和大规模模型上的实验表明，Zero-Run审计能在无法重训练或可控数据插入的场景下实现实用的隐私评估。

Conclusion: Zero-Run框架填补了事后观测性隐私审计的空白，通过因果校正使得在固定数据集上也能获得有效的差分隐私下界估计。

Abstract: Privacy auditing provides empirical lower bounds on the differential privacy parameters of learning algorithms. Existing methods, however, require interventional access to the training pipeline, either to retrain multiple times or to randomize data inclusion. This is often infeasible for large deployed systems such as foundation models. We introduce Zero-Run privacy auditing, a post-hoc framework for auditing models using two fixed datasets: examples known to be training-set members and examples known to be non-members. In this observational regime, membership is no longer randomized; instead, member and non-member data often differ in distribution, so membership inference scores may reflect a distribution shift rather than algorithmic leakage. Drawing on ideas from causal inference, we formalize this confounding effect and propose two complementary corrections that yield valid privacy audits. Our first approach models the combined effect of distribution shift and algorithmic leakage as an adaptive composition, producing conservative global corrections. Our second approach conditions on observed data and adjusts pointwise membership guesses, yielding sharper instance-dependent bounds. Experiments on synthetic data and large-scale models show that Zero-Run auditing enables practical privacy evaluation when retraining or controlled data insertion is infeasible.

</details>


### [86] [One Step to the Side: Why Defenses Against Malicious Finetuning Fail Under Adaptive Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14605)
*Itay Zloczower,Eyal Lenga,Gilad Gressel,Yisroel Mirsky*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了15种针对大模型微调阶段的安全防御机制，发现这些防御均存在共同缺陷：仅掩盖或误导有害行为路径而未真正消除有害行为。作者提出了一种统一的自适应攻击方法，成功突破了所有现有防御机制，表明当前防御仅能应对预设攻击，缺乏鲁棒安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对大模型微调阶段的安全防御方法虽然声称具有鲁棒性，但大多仅在固定攻击场景下评估，未考虑针对防御本身设计的自适应攻击。作者旨在揭示这些防御的真实安全性缺陷。

Method: 首先对15种近期防御方法进行系统梳理，识别其核心防护机制；然后设计一种统一的自适应攻击方法，针对性攻破各类防御机制的共同弱点，即防御仅掩盖或误导有害行为路径但未移除行为本身。

Result: 统一自适应攻击成功突破了所有15种防御机制，证明现有防御主要阻止了其设计时所针对的特定攻击，但无法抵御自适应攻击者。

Conclusion: 当前针对恶意微调的安全防御方法无法提供鲁棒的安全性，需要更严格的压力测试与新的防御范式。

Abstract: Model providers increasingly release open weights or allow users to fine-tune foundation models through APIs. Although these models are safety-aligned before release, their safeguards can often be removed by fine-tuning on harmful data. Recent defenses aim to make models robust to such malicious fine-tuning, but they are largely evaluated only against fixed attacks that do not account for the defense. We show that these robustness claims are incomplete. Surveying 15 recent defenses, we identify several defense mechanisms and show that they share a single weakness: they obscure or misdirect the path to harmful behavior without removing the behavior itself. We then develop a unified adaptive attack that breaks defenses across all defense mechanisms. Our results show that current approaches do not provide robust security; they mainly stop the attacks they were designed against. We hope that our unified adaptive adversary for this domain will help future researchers and practitioners stress-test new defenses before deployment.

</details>


### [87] [Known By Their Actions: Fingerprinting LLM Browser Agents via UI Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14786)
*William Lugoloobi,Samuelle Marro,Jabez Magomere,Joss Wright,Chris Russell*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明，基于LLM的智能体在浏览网页时，其动作序列和交互时序可通过被动JavaScript追踪捕获，并以高达96%的F1分数识别出底层模型；这种行为指纹难以通过随机延迟等简单防御有效消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Web端能否被动识别驱动智能体的底层LLM模型，揭示由此带来的安全风险（如针对性攻击利用已知模型漏洞）。

Method: 在14个前沿LLM和4种Web环境（信息检索、购物任务）中，通过被动JavaScript追踪器捕获智能体的动作和交互时序，训练分类器进行模型识别，并测试跨模型规模/系列泛化性、少样本训练效果、早期识别能力以及随机延迟防御的有效性。

Result: 分类器识别底层模型的F1分数高达96%；跨模型规模/系列泛化良好；少量交互轨迹即可训练强分类器；可在回合早期推断模型身份；随机时间延迟会降低分类器性能，但重训练后性能基本恢复。

Conclusion: 智能体的行为模式可作为模型指纹被被动识别，构成重大安全风险，简单防御（如随机延迟）不足以提供稳健保护。

Abstract: As LLM-based agents increasingly browse the web on users' behalf, a natural question arises: can websites passively identify which underlying model powers an agent? Doing so would represent a significant security risk, enabling targeted attacks tailored to known model vulnerabilities. Across 14 frontier LLMs and four web environments spanning information retrieval and shopping tasks, we show that an agent's actions and interaction timings, captured via a passive JavaScript tracker, are sufficient to identify the underlying model with up to 96\% F1. We formalise this attack surface by demonstrating that classifiers trained on agent actions generalise across model sizes and families. We further show that strong classifiers can be trained from few interaction traces and that agent identity can be inferred early within an episode. Injecting randomised timing delays between actions substantially degrades classifier performance, but does not provide robust protection: a classifier retrained on delayed traces largely recovers performance. We release our harness and a labelled corpus of agent traces \href{https://github.com/KabakaWilliam/known_actions}{here}.

</details>


### [88] [Capacitive Touchscreens at Risk: A Practical Side-Channel Attack on Smartphones via Electromagnetic Emanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14633)
*Yukun Cheng,Changhai Ou,Shiyu Zhu,Jinyuan Zhang,Zhenfang Qiu,Xingshuo Han,Tianwei Zhang,Yuan Li,Shihui Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TESLA是一种新型、非接触式的电磁侧信道攻击方法，通过悄悄放置EM探头靠近受害者设备，利用触摸屏扫描时的固有电磁辐射提取敏感信息（如解锁PIN码、键盘输入、应用类别和连续手写轨迹），在多种商用手机上实现了高达99.3%的PIN码识别成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电容式触摸屏侧信道攻击存在限制条件苛刻或需要侵入式测量的问题，缺乏一种在实用攻击场景下更广泛、更高效的非接触式攻击方法。

Method: 秘密放置EM探头靠近受害者设备，捕获触摸屏扫描过程中产生的固有电磁辐射，该辐射编码了触摸交互的时空演化信息，形成统一的泄漏基，从而提取多种敏感信息。

Result: 在iPhone X、小米10 Pro、三星S10和华为Mate 30 Pro等商用手机上，PIN码识别成功率99.3%，键盘输入重建97.6%，应用推断95.0%，字符识别准确率76.8%，2D手写轨迹重建Jaccard指数0.74。

Conclusion: TESLA相比现有攻击具有更广泛的攻击目标、更高效的样本采集和更实用的攻击场景，在私密会议室和公共图书馆等多样化环境中均验证了有效性。

Abstract: Capacitive touchscreens in modern smartphones introduce severe side-channel vulnerabilities. However, existing attacks often require restrictive conditions or invasive measurements. This paper presents TESLA, a novel, contactless electromagnetic (EM) side-channel attack that exploits inherent EM emanations during touchscreen scanning. We demonstrate that these emanations encode the spatiotemporal evolution of touch interactions, forming a unified leakage basis. By secretly placing an EM probe near the victim's device, TESLA enables attackers to extract highly sensitive information, including screen-unlocking PIN codes, keyboard inputs, interacting application categories, and continuous handwriting trajectories. Compared to existing attacks, TESLA offers a broader range of attack targets, more efficient sample acquisition, and operations in practical attack scenarios. Extensive evaluations on popular commercial smartphones, specifically the iPhone X, Xiaomi 10 Pro, Samsung S10, and Huawei Mate 30 Pro, validate the effectiveness of TESLA. It achieves remarkable inference accuracy in diverse settings such as private meeting rooms and public libraries, with success rates of 99.3% for PIN code recognition, 97.6% for keyboard input reconstruction, and 95.0% for application inference, respectively. Simultaneously, it attains a 76.8% character recognition accuracy and a high geometric similarity (Jaccard index of 0.74) for 2D handwriting trajectory reconstruction.

</details>


### [89] [Adapting AlphaEvolve to Optimize Fully Homomorphic Encryption on TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14718)
*Shruthi Gorantala,Jianming Tong,Asra Ali,Baiyu Li,Jonathan Katz,Jeremy Kun,Thomas Steinke,Abhradeep Thakurta,Julian Walker,Amir Yazdanbakhsh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AlphaEvolve，一个利用LLM驱动代码生成和硬件执行反馈的自动化进化搜索框架，用于优化FHE加密核在TPU上的执行效率。在TPUv5e上，24小时内自动发现了TFHE bootstrapping延迟2.5倍、CKKS旋转和乘法延迟1.31倍和1.18倍的优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全同态加密(FHE)因计算开销过大难以大规模部署，现有编译器栈难以充分利用TPU等硬件加速器的资源，开发者需要手动试错过程导致执行碎片化和资源利用率低下。

Method: 将FHE加密核的硬件感知优化建模为进化搜索问题，利用AlphaEvolve的闭环系统，结合LLM驱动代码生成、真实硬件执行反馈和严格的正确性测试来引导进化过程。

Result: 在Google Cloud TPUv5e上，24小时内自动发现的优化使TFHE bootstrapping延迟提升2.5倍，CKKS旋转和乘法延迟分别提升1.31倍和1.18倍。

Conclusion: AlphaEvolve能够有效帮助研究人员在密码学、编译器和硬件加速器之间进行优化权衡的探索。

Abstract: The deployment of Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) at scale is hindered due to its heavy computational overhead. While specialized hardware accelerators like Google Tensor Processing Units (TPUs) can help, mapping complex cryptographic kernels onto such architectures remains a challenge. Efficient execution requires co-optimization between the systolic array-based Matrix Multiplication Unit (MXU) and Vector Processing Units (VPUs), as well as the orchestration of data movement across the vector register files. Existing compiler stacks often abstract low-level hardware utilization, requiring developers to adopt a manual trial-and-error process that often results in fragmented execution and underutilized resources. To accelerate this development process, we use AlphaEvolve to automate the exploration of hardware-aware cryptographic-kernel optimizations. We frame optimization as an evolutionary search problem, utilizing the closed-loop system provided by AlphaEvolve, that leverages LLM-driven code generation. We use real-world feedback from hardware execution and rigorous correctness testing to guide the evolution process. We evaluate AlphaEvolve optimization on primitives for both the TFHE (Jaxite) and CKKS (CROSS) FHE schemes on Google Cloud TPUv5e, a contemporary TPU architecture. Within 24 hours of automated exploration, AlphaEvolve discovered implementation-level optimizations that improve TFHE bootstrap latency by 2.5x and CKKS rotation and multiplication latency by 1.31x and 1.18x, respectively, relative to human-engineered state of the art. These results demonstrate that AlphaEvolve can be used to enable researchers to navigate the optimization trade-offs between cryptography, compilers, and hardware accelerators.

</details>


### [90] [Analyzing Codes of Conduct for Online Safety in Video Games at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15047)
*Jiuming Jiang,Shidong Pan,Daniel W Woods,Jingjie Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文开发了CONDUCTIFY流水线，对Steam平台上9,586款多人游戏中350款游戏的行为准则（CoC）进行分析，发现大多数多人游戏缺乏CoC，且CoC对人际伤害和未成年人安全的表述不够具体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线游戏中的各种危害（如骚扰、歧视、隐私泄露、作弊等）日益严重，游戏公司通过行为准则（CoC）来规范玩家行为，但CoC的普及程度、覆盖的安全隐私违规类型以及是否满足监管期望尚不清楚。

Method: 开发了CONDUCTIFY流水线，用于大规模识别和分析游戏行为准则（CoC），并将其应用于Steam平台上9,586款多人游戏。

Result: 在9,586款游戏中仅找到350款游戏的CoC；CoC在热门、面向成人和社区驱动型游戏中更常见；80%以上的CoC一致涵盖传统安全和安全违规，但对不同类型违规的治理方式差异显著；与游戏机制相关危害相比，人际伤害和未成年人安全的表述不够具体。

Conclusion: 研究发现表明当前在线游戏安全治理存在不足，大多数多人游戏缺乏CoC，且CoC对人机交互危害的表述不够具体，需要改进在线安全治理、CoC执行实践，并为玩家和开发者社区建设更好的安全基础设施。

Abstract: Online video games have become major online social spaces where users interact, compete, and create together. These spaces, however, expose users to a wide spectrum of online harms, including harassment, discrimination, inappropriate content, privacy breach, cheating, and more. The shape and severity of such harms vary across game design, mechanics, and community context. To mitigate these harms, game companies issue Codes of Conduct (CoCs) that articulate online safety rules and direct players to safety resources. However, it remains unclear how prevalent CoCs are, what safety, security and privacy violations they govern, and whether they meet growing regulatory and industry expectations. We develop and leverage CONDUCTIFY, a pipeline for identifying and analyzing CoCs at scale. Applied to Steam, the largest PC game marketplace, it located the available CoCs for 350 of the 9,586 multiplayer titles on Steam. We found that CoCs are more available among popular, adult-oriented, and community-driven games, while most multiplayer games operate without CoCs despite regulatory and industry recommendations. Although over 80% of the games with CoCs available consistently address traditional security and safety violations, their governance approaches vary substantially across types of violations. A further asymmetry emerges in specificity. Compared with harms related to gameplay mechanics, the articulations of interpersonal harm and the underage player safety are often less specific, despite their relevance to many game communities. Together, these results inform the improvement of online safety governance and CoC enforcement practices, and building better safety infrastructure for the community of players and developers.

</details>


### [91] [EVA: Editing for Versatile Alignment against Jailbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14750)
*Yi Wang,Hongye Qiu,Yue Xu,Sibei Yang,Zhan Qin,Minlie Huang,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EVA是一种通过直接模型编辑（而非微调或外部过滤）来对齐LLM/VLM安全性的新框架，通过精确识别并编辑与有害指令相关的神经元，在有效防御越狱攻击的同时保持模型通用推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全对齐方法（安全微调、外部过滤器）存在计算开销大和安全-效用权衡问题，即在减少有害内容的同时会降低模型在良性任务上的性能。

Method: EVA将安全对齐重构为精确的知识修正任务：定位并手术式编辑模型中与对有害指令敏感度相关的特定神经元，仅更新极小部分参数，保留大部分模型结构不变。

Result: 在LLM和VLM上的广泛实验表明，EVA在缓解越狱攻击方面优于基线方法，实现了高效且精准的后部署安全对齐。

Conclusion: EVA通过直接模型编辑的方式有效解决了安全对齐中的效率与效用权衡问题，为后部署阶段的安全维护提供了一种精确、高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities but remain vulnerable to jailbreaking attacks, where adversaries exploit textual or visual triggers to bypass safety guardrails. Recent defenses typically rely on safety fine-tuning or external filters to reduce the model's likelihood of producing harmful content. While effective to some extent, these methods often incur significant computational overheads and suffer from the safety utility trade-off, degrading the model's performance on benign tasks. To address these challenges, we propose EVA (Editing for Versatile Alignment against Jailbreaks), a novel framework that pioneers the application of direct model editing for safety alignment. EVA reframes safety alignment as a precise knowledge correction task. Instead of retraining massive parameters, EVA identifies and surgically edits specific neurons responsible for the model's susceptibility to harmful instructions, while leaving the vast majority of the model unchanged. By localizing the updates, EVA effectively neutralizes harmful behaviors without compromising the model's general reasoning capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EVA outperforms baselines in mitigating jailbreaks across both LLMs and VLMs, offering a precise and efficient solution for post-deployment safety alignment.

</details>


### [92] [Do Coding Agents Understand Least-Privilege Authorization?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14859)
*Zheng Yan,Jingxiang Weng,Charles Chen,Dengyun Peng,Ethan Qin,Jiannan Guan,Jinhao Liu,Qiming Yu,Yixin Yuan,Fanqing Meng,Carl Che,Mengkang Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出权限边界推断任务和AuthBench基准，发现前沿模型在终端任务授权中难以平衡"充分性"与"紧致性"，并提出"充分性-紧致性分解"方法，通过先模拟任务生成覆盖性策略再逐项审计，提升了敏感任务成功率并降低了攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在访问shell、仓库和用户文件时，最小权限授权是安全部署的前提条件，但当前模型无法自主推断任务所需的权限边界。

Method: 1) 提出权限边界推断任务及AuthBench基准（120个真实终端任务，含人工审核权限标签和可执行验证器）；2) 提出充分性-紧致性分解方法：先通过前向模拟生成覆盖导向的策略，再逐项审计每个授予条目的依据和敏感性。

Result: 前沿模型在授权时既遗漏执行链所需的权限，又授予未使用或敏感访问权限；增加推理时间无法解决此问题，反而使模型向其特定"授权吸引子"收敛；充分性-紧致性分解方法在紧致性偏置模型上提升敏感任务成功率最多15.8%，且在所有评估模型上降低攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 直接策略生成是瓶颈所在，因为单次生成必须同时发现所有必要访问并拒绝所有不必要访问；分解方法能有效缓解此问题。

Abstract: As coding agents gain access to shells, repositories, and user files, least-privilege authorization becomes a prerequisite for safe deployment: an agent should receive enough authority to complete the task, without unnecessary authority that exposes sensitive surfaces.To study whether current models can infer this boundary themselves, we first introduce permission-boundary inference, where a model maps a task instruction and terminal environment to a file-level read/write/execute policy, and AuthBench, a benchmark of 120 realistic terminal tasks with human-reviewed permission labels and executable validators for utility and attack outcomes.AuthBench shows that authorization is not a simple conservative-versus-permissive calibration problem: frontier models often omit permissions required by the execution chain while also granting unused or sensitive accesses.Increasing inference-time reasoning does not resolve this mismatch. Instead, each model moves toward a model-specific authorization attractor: more reasoning makes it more consistent in its own failure mode, whether broad-but-exposed or tight-but-brittle.This suggests that direct policy generation is the bottleneck, because a single generation must both discover all necessary accesses and reject all unnecessary ones.We therefore propose Sufficiency-Tightness Decomposition, which first generates a coverage-oriented policy by forward-simulating the task and then audits each granted entry for grounding and sensitivity.Across tested models, this decomposition improves sensitive-task success by up to 15.8% on tightness-biased models while reducing attack success across all evaluated models.

</details>


### [93] [Toward Securing AI Agents Like Operating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14932)
*Lukas Pirch,Micha Horlboge,Patrick Großmann,Syeda Mahnur Asif,Klim Kireev,Thorsten Holz,Konrad Rieck*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文从操作系统安全视角出发，系统研究了大语言模型驱动的自主智能体（如OpenClaw）的安全问题。通过对比两者在资源隔离、权限分离和通信中介方面的相似挑战，分析了开源智能体的攻击向量，并对四个主流智能体进行了案例评估。研究发现现有防护机制在实践中存在缺陷，但许多漏洞可通过成熟的操作系统安全技术加以缓解，最终提出了安全设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的自主智能体在拓展能力（工具使用、第三方技能、深度集成用户环境）的同时，引入了严重的安全风险——无约束能力与敏感用户数据访问权限结合。作者希望借鉴操作系统领域成熟的资源隔离、权限分离和通信中介等安全机制，系统性地分析和解决智能体系统的安全问题。
