Method: 采用操作系统安全视角进行类比分析，首先梳理开源智能体现状并推导统一架构，系统分析潜在攻击向量；随后通过案例研究对四个广泛使用的OpenClaw类智能体进行安全性评估，检验其在适度攻击者能力下的防护效果。

Result: 在适度攻击者能力下，现有多个防护机制在实践中失效；安全运行需要详细的系统知识和精心配置；部分智能体能力本质上存在不安全设计。

Conclusion: 许多漏洞可通过成熟的操作系统安全技术（如资源隔离、权限分离、通信控制）得到缓解；本文提供了一套智能体系统安全设计建议。

Abstract: Autonomous agents based on large language models (LLMs) are rapidly emerging as a general-purpose technology, with recent systems such as OpenClaw extending their capabilities through broad tool use, third-party skills, and deeper integration into user environments. At the same time, these agentic systems introduce substantial security risks by combining unconstrained capabilities with access to sensitive user data. In this work, we investigate the security of LLM-based agents through the lens of operating systems. We argue that both face strikingly similar challenges in isolating resources, separating privileges, and mediating communication.
  Guided by this perspective, we survey the current landscape of open-source agents, derive a unified agent architecture, and systematically analyze potential attack vectors. To validate this analysis, we conduct a case study evaluating four widely used OpenClaw-like agents. Even under modest attacker capabilities, we find that several protection mechanisms fail in practice and that secure operation requires detailed system knowledge and careful configuration. However, we also observe that while some agentic capabilities remain insecure by design, many vulnerabilities can be mitigated using well-established techniques from operating system security. We conclude with a set of recommendations for the secure design of agentic systems.

</details>


### [94] [PickleFuzzer: A Case Study in Fuzzing for Discrepancies Between Python Pickle Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15084)
*Justin Applegate,Andreas Kellas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PickleFuzzer是一个基于生成式模糊测试的工具，能够有效识别Python三种原生pickle实现之间的不一致性，发现了14个新差异，其中4个关键差异可绕过Hugging Face等平台的安全扫描工具，并获得$750赏金。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python的pickle序列化协议虽被广泛使用（尤其用于保存机器学习模型），但其安全性存在问题。现有缓解措施（限制导入、静态/动态分析）依赖于对Pickle虚拟机（PVM）操作码的准确解析，而Python三种原生PVM模块之间存在差异，导致恶意载荷检测错误，削弱现有防御能力。

Method: 提出PickleFuzzer，一个基于生成式模糊测试的工具。它利用自定义语法生成pickle对象，将生成的pickle对象分别传递给三种pickle实现，通过比较异常抛出或关键内部状态的变化来检测差异。采用差分测试（differential testing）方法，无需规范推导的预言机。

Result: 检测到14个新差异，其中4个为关键差异，可绕过Hugging Face等平台部署的安全扫描工具。已向Python软件基金会报告所有发现，安全漏洞已通过漏洞赏金平台报告并获得$750奖金。

Conclusion: 差分测试是识别pickle实现中安全相关不一致性的可行方法，未来可向更定向的模糊测试方向推进，以发现更深层次的pickle漏洞。

Abstract: Python's native serialization protocol, pickle, is a powerful but insecure format for transferring untrusted data. It is frequently used, especially for saving machine learning models, despite known security challenges. While developers sometimes mitigate this risk by restricting imports during unpickling or using static and dynamic analysis tools, these approaches are error-prone and depend heavily on accurate interpretations of the Pickle Virtual Machine (PVM) opcodes. Discrepancies across Python's three native PVM modules can lead to incorrect detection of malicious payloads and undermine existing defenses.
  To efficiently and scalably identify discrepancies, we present PickleFuzzer, a custom generation-based fuzzer that identifies inconsistencies across pickle implementations. PickleFuzzer generates pickle objects, passes them to each implementation, and detects differences in thrown exceptions or changes to key internal states. It generates pickle objects using a grammar, which we developed to account for the missing pickle specification. It determines discrepancies by comparing the execution behaviors of each test implementation, rather than requiring a specification-derived oracle. PickleFuzzer detected 14 new discrepancies between the pickle implementations. Four discrepancies are critical and can be used to bypass security-critical scanning tools like those deployed on the popular model hosting platform, Hugging Face. We disclosed all findings to the Python Software Foundation for remediation, and additionally disclosed the security issues to a bug bounty platform and were awarded a $750 bounty. We demonstrate that differential testing is a viable approach for identifying security-relevant discrepancies in important pickle implementations, and our work can lead to promising future directions for finding deeper pickle bugs with more directed fuzzing.

</details>


### [95] [Talk is (Not) Cheap: A Taxonomy and Benchmark Coverage Audit for LLM Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15118)
*Karthik Raghu Iyer,Yazdan Jamshidi,Nicholas Bray,Alexey A. Shvets*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于STRIDE模型的4×6目标-技术矩阵框架，通过从932篇arXiv安全研究（2023-2026）中提取的507叶分类法（401个数据填充+106个威胁模型推导）审计LLM攻击基准的集体覆盖范围。应用于六个公开基准发现：主流框架（HarmBench、InjecAgent、AgentDojo）仅覆盖最多25%的矩阵区域，且服务中断、模型内部等完整STRIDE类别缺乏标准化评估，尽管已发表攻击实现了46倍令牌放大和96%攻击成功率。语料库中2521个独特攻击组还揭示了命名碎片化（单一攻击最多29种表面形式）和安全与对齐绕过领域的严重集中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM攻击基准评估缺乏统一框架来审计集体覆盖范围，导致威胁面中大量类别（如服务中断、模型内部攻击）被忽视，且命名碎片化等问题在局部视角下不可见。

Method: 构建4×6目标-技术矩阵（基于STRIDE），从932篇arXiv论文中提取507叶攻击分类法（401个数据填充+106个威胁模型推导），并映射六个公开基准以评估集体覆盖。

Result: 主流基准仅覆盖矩阵的25%，服务中断和模型内部攻击无任何基准测试，已发表攻击的46倍令牌放大和96%成功率未被评估；发现2521个攻击组存在命名碎片化（最多29种同义形式）和安全与对齐绕过领域的过高集中。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为可扩展工件，支持新基准映射到同一矩阵，帮助社区追踪评估缺口是否正在缩小。

Abstract: We introduce a reusable framework for auditing whether LLM attack benchmarks collectively cover the threat surface: a 4$\times$6 Target $\times$ Technique matrix grounded in STRIDE, constructed from a 507-leaf taxonomy -- 401 data-populated and 106 threat-model-derived leaves -- of inference-time attacks extracted from 932 arXiv security studies (2023--2026). The matrix enables benchmark-external validation -- auditing collective coverage rather than individual benchmark consistency. Applying it to six public benchmarks reveals that the three primary frameworks (HarmBench, InjecAgent, AgentDojo) occupy non-overlapping cells covering at most 25\% of the matrix, while entire STRIDE threat categories (Service Disruption, Model Internals) lack any standardized evaluation, despite published attacks in these categories achieving 46$\times$ token amplification and 96\% attack success rates through mechanisms which no benchmark tests. The corpus of 2,521 unique attack groups further reveals pervasive naming fragmentation (up to 29 surface forms for a single attack) and heavy concentration in Safety \& Alignment Bypass, structural properties invisible at smaller scale. The taxonomy, attack records, and coverage mappings are released as extensible artifacts; as new benchmarks emerge, they can be mapped onto the same matrix, enabling the community to track whether evaluation gaps are closing.

</details>


### [96] [MetaBackdoor: Exploiting Positional Encoding as a Backdoor Attack Surface in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15172)
*Rui Wen,Mark Russinovich,Andrew Paverd,Jun Sakuma,Ahmed Salem*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaBackdoor，一种利用位置信息（如序列长度）而非文本内容作为触发信号的新型后门攻击方法，可绕过基于内容检测的防御机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后门攻击依赖内容触发词，需显式修改输入文本，存在局限性且易被检测。作者希望突破这一假设，探索利用Transformer固有位置编码特性作为攻击面。

Method: 利用Transformer中位置编码的特性，将序列长度相关的结构信息作为后门触发信号。后门模型在检测到满足特定长度条件的输入时激活恶意行为，而不需要修改文本内容。

Result: 证明简单的基于长度的位置触发即可激活后门，使模型泄露内部敏感信息（如系统提示词），并可在多轮对话中通过正常交互自激活，诱导恶意工具调用。该方法与内容后门正交，可组合使用。

Conclusion: 位置编码是此前被忽视的攻击面，扩展了LLM后门的威胁模型，挑战了基于内容检测的防御策略，需发展针对位置触发的新型防御机制。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks pose a serious security threat to large language models (LLMs), which are increasingly deployed as general-purpose assistants in safety- and privacy-critical applications. Existing LLM backdoors rely primarily on content-based triggers, requiring explicit modification of the input text. In this work, we show that this assumption is unnecessary and limiting. We introduce MetaBackdoor, a new class of backdoor attacks that exploits positional information as the trigger, without modifying textual content. Our key insight is that Transformer-based LLMs necessarily encode token positions to process ordered sequences. As a result, length-correlated positional structure is reflected in the model's internal computation and can be used as an effective non-content trigger signal.
  We demonstrate that even a simple length-based positional trigger is sufficient to activate stealthy backdoors. Unlike prior attacks, MetaBackdoor operates on visibly and semantically clean inputs and enables qualitatively new capabilities. We show that a backdoored LLM can be induced to disclose sensitive internal information, including proprietary system prompts, once a length condition is satisfied. We further demonstrate a self-activation scenario, where normal multi-turn interaction can move the conversation context into the trigger region and induce malicious tool-call behavior without attacker-supplied trigger text. In addition, MetaBackdoor is orthogonal to content-based backdoors and can be composed with them to create more precise and harder-to-detect activation conditions.
  Our results expand the threat model of LLM backdoors by revealing positional encoding as a previously overlooked attack surface. This challenges defenses that focus on detecting suspicious text and highlights the need for new defense strategies that explicitly account for positional triggers in modern LLM architectures.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [Vision-Based Runtime Monitoring under Varying Specifications using Semantic Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13923)
*Bardh Hoxha,Oliver Schön,Hideki Okamoto,Lars Lindemann,Georgios Fainekos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在部分可观测条件下，基于视觉观测的过去时间信号时态逻辑(ptSTL)的认证运行时监控问题。提出了一种可复用的监控框架：通过语义基（原子鲁棒性得分向量）作为最小预测目标，结合单调1-Lipschitz可复用接口和单次共形校准，实现对目标片段内任意公式的认证，无需针对每个公式重新训练。同时引入了滚动预测监控器。实验在行人十字路口和Waymo驾驶数据上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时监控方法在部分可观测条件下，从图像推理安全相关量并给出有限样本保证时，通常需要针对每个时态逻辑公式分别训练和校准，缺乏可复用性，效率低下。

Method: (1) 提出语义基（semantic basis）概念，即有限字典中时序原子的鲁棒性得分向量，证明其为单调1-Lipschitz可复用接口类中的最小预测目标；(2) 设计基于解析树的确定性解码器，从语义基评估任意公式；(3) 采用单次共形校准（single conformal calibration）认证整个公式片段，无需联合界；(4) 引入滚动预测监控器（rolling prediction monitor），仅预测当前谓词值并在线重构时序历史。

Result: 在行人十字路口基准上，滚动监控器在短时域上获得更紧的认证界，语义基监控器在长时域上紧致度高出4倍。在Waymo真实驾驶数据上，两种监控器均满足共形覆盖保证。

Conclusion: 提出的可复用监控框架有效解决了部分可观测条件下ptSTL公式的认证运行时监控问题，语义基方法在长时域上优势明显，滚动方法在短时域上更优，两者在实际数据上均验证了共形覆盖保证的有效性。

Abstract: We study certified runtime monitoring of past-time signal temporal logic (ptSTL) from visual observations under partial observability. The monitor must infer safety-relevant quantities from images and provide finite-sample guarantees, while being \emph{reusable}: once trained and calibrated, it should certify any formula in a target fragment without per-formula retraining. For fragments induced by a finite dictionary of temporal atoms, we prove that the \emph{semantic basis}, the vector of atom robustness scores, is the minimum prediction target within the class of monotone, 1-Lipschitz reusable interfaces: any formula is evaluated by a deterministic decoder derived from the parse tree, and a single conformal calibration pass certifies the entire fragment with no union bound. We also introduce a \emph{rolling prediction monitor} that predicts only current predicate values and reconstructs temporal history online; this is easier to learn but grows conservative at long horizons. On a pedestrian-crossroad benchmark, rolling achieves tighter certified bounds at short horizons while the semantic-basis monitor is up to 4-times tighter at long horizons. We validate the presented monitors on real-world Waymo driving data, where both monitors satisfy the conformal coverage guarantee empirically.

</details>


### [98] [Forgetting That Sticks: Quantization-Permanent Unlearning via Circuit Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15138)
*Saisab Sadhu,Pratinav Seth,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现标准机器学习评估忽略量化部署现实，揭示梯度类去学习存在双重失效：量化后遗忘能力逆转或模型几乎不变。根源是参数更新远小于量化bin宽度。提出MANSU方法，通过因果电路归因、零空间投影和对角Fisher约束，首次同时实现有意义遗忘、保留保持、非正量化间隙和结构性擦除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去学习评估仅在全精度下衡量行为抑制，但实际部署的语言模型都经过量化。近期工作发现4-bit量化可逆转去学习效果，本文旨在探究这一现象的普遍性、根本原因并提出解决方案。

Method: 提出MANSU方法，包含三步：(1)因果电路归因识别最小遗忘子图；(2)电路限制的零空间投影配合对角Fisher保留约束；(3)逐参数幅度下限确保量化后参数不变。同时引入电路归因散度(CAD)作为结构性擦除的验证指标。

Result: MANSU是首个在所有四个维度（有意义遗忘、保留保持、非正PTQ间隙、结构性擦除）上均有裕度的方法。梯度类基线在量化后恢复高达+0.05准确率，而MANSU不受影响。

Conclusion: 标准去学习评估存在系统性缺陷，必须考虑量化部署现实。参数更新量远小于量化bin宽度（47-828倍）是根本原因，形式化为稀疏性-持久性权衡。MANSU通过机制对齐的设计从根本上解决双重失效模式。

Abstract: Standard unlearning evaluations measure behavioral suppression in full precision, immediately after training, despite every deployed language model being quantized first. Recent work has shown that 4-bit post-training quantization can reverse machine unlearning; we show this is not a tuning artefact but a systematic dual failure: gradient-based methods that achieve meaningful forgetting lose it under compression, while methods that survive quantization barely change the model. Both failures trace to the same root cause: across all baselines, per-parameter updates lie 47-828x below the NF4 quantization bin width; updates diffused across billions of parameters cannot clear quantization bin boundaries, a consequence we formalize as a sparsity-permanence tradeoff. We present MANSU (Mechanistic-Aligned Null-Space Unlearning), which resolves both modes by combining causal circuit attribution to isolate the minimal forget-set subgraph, circuit-restricted null-space projection with a diagonal-Fisher retain bound, and a per-parameter magnitude floor guaranteeing quantization survival by construction. We additionally introduce Circuit Attribution Divergence (CAD), a mechanistic verification metric distinguishing structural erasure from behavioral suppression, a distinction existing metrics cannot make. Across multiple model families and hazard benchmarks, MANSU is the first method to jointly satisfy all four properties with margin on each (meaningful forgetting, retain preservation, non-positive PTQ gap, and structural erasure), while gradient-based baselines recover up to +0.05 accuracy under compression.

</details>


### [99] [Mechanistic Interpretability of EEG Foundation Models via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13930)
*William Lehn-Schiøler,Magnus Ruud Kjær,Rahul Thapa,Magnus Guldberg Pedersen,Anton Storgaard Mosquera,Nick Williams,Radu Gatej,Tue Lehn-Schiøler,Sándor Beniczky,Sadasivan Puthusserypady,James Zou,Lars Kai Hansen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用TopK稀疏自编码器从三个EEG Transformer（SleepFM、REVE、LaBraM）中提取稀疏特征字典，通过临床概念引导分析单语义性和纠缠性，揭示了选择性可控、编码但纠缠和未编码三种操作机制，并发现了"破坏球"干预和临床纠缠（如年龄-病理混杂）等关键表征缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型在临床任务上表现出色，但其内部计算过程不透明，阻碍了临床信任的建立。

Method: 1) 在三个架构不同的EEG Transformer（SleepFM、REVE、LaBraM）的嵌入层上应用TopK稀疏自编码器提取稀疏特征字典；2) 将特征基于临床分类体系（异常、年龄、性别、药物）进行基准测试，量化单语义性和纠缠性；3) 通过单一超参数流程（基于字典健康审计）实现跨架构迁移；4) 利用概念引导引入"目标vs.非目标"探针区域指标量化引导选择性；5) 使用频谱解码器将干预映射回幅度谱。

Result: 揭示了三种操作机制：选择性可控、编码但纠缠、未编码。发现"破坏球"干预（导致全局模型性能崩溃）和临床纠缠（如年龄-病理混杂，无法在不破坏一个概念的情况下抑制另一个概念）。光谱解码器可将干预转化为生理可解释的频率特征（如病理慢波抑制和α波段恢复）。

Conclusion: 该框架揭示了EEG基础模型中的关键表征缺陷，提供了将潜在操作转化为可解释生理信号的方法，有助于提升模型在临床中的可信度和可解释性。

Abstract: EEG foundation models achieve state-of-the-art clinical performance, yet the internal computations driving their predictions remain opaque: a barrier to clinical trust. We apply TopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) across three architecturally distinct EEG transformers: SleepFM, REVE, and LaBraM to extract sparse feature dictionaries from their embeddings. By grounding these features in a clinical taxonomy (abnormality, age, sex, and medication), we benchmark monosemanticity and entanglement across architectures. A single hyperparameter procedure, driven by an intrinsic dictionary health audit, transfers robustly across all three architectures. Via concept steering, we introduce a "target vs. off-target" probe area metric to quantify steering selectivity and reveal three operational regimes: selectively steerable, encoded but entangled, and non-encoded. This framework exposes critical representational failures: "wrecking-ball" interventions that collapse global model performance, and clinical entanglements, such as age-pathology confounding, where it is impossible to suppress one concept without corrupting the other. Finally, a spectral decoder maps these interventions back to the amplitude spectrum, translating latent manipulations into physiologically interpretable frequency signatures, such as pathological slow-wave suppression and $α$-band restoration.

</details>


### [100] [PreFT: Prefill-only finetuning for efficient inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14217)
*Andrew Lanpouthakoun,Aryaman Arora,Zhengxuan Wu,Dhruv Pai,Ben Keigwin,Dan Jurafsky,Christopher Potts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Prefill-only Finetuning (PreFT)，仅在预填充阶段应用适配器，相比传统PEFT在多适配器服务场景下实现1.9倍吞吐提升，且通过增加秩可补偿性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PEFT在多适配器服务时，prefill与decode阶段存在吞吐量不匹配问题，decode阶段服务多个适配器时吞吐严重下降，现有专用内核和内存管理技术无法根本解决。

Method: 提出PreFT方法，仅在prefill阶段对输入token应用适配器（LoRA/ReFT），decode阶段丢弃适配器。基于vLLM引擎实现了高效的prefill-only PEFT版本。

Result: 服务512个适配器时Llama 3.1 70B吞吐量提升1.9倍；SFT任务中PreFT评估损失略高但可通过增加秩补偿且几乎不降低吞吐；RL任务中PreFT与标准PEFT性能接近。

Conclusion: PreFT在准确率-吞吐量权衡上优于传统PEFT，验证了仅对prefill阶段进行适配的有效性，为个性化服务提供了更高效的方案。

Abstract: Large language models can now be personalised efficiently at scale using parameter efficient finetuning methods (PEFTs), but serving user-specific PEFTs harms throughput, even with specialised kernels and memory management techniques. This is because, theoretically and empirically, a mismatch exists between prefill (processing a large number of tokens at once) and decode (generating a single token autoregressively): the latter has far lower throughput when serving multiple adapters. Rather than optimising performance relative to parameter count, for efficient multi-adapter serving, we instead ought to optimise performance relative to serving throughput. We therefore propose PreFT (Prefill-only Finetuning), wherein we only apply the adapter to prefill tokens and discard it afterwards. PreFT significantly increases throughput with minimal effect on performance. We develop and release an efficient implementation of two prefill-only PEFTs, LoRA and ReFT, on the vLLM inference engine. We first show that serving multi-user PreFTs is more efficient than traditional PEFTs ($1.9\times$ the throughput when serving $512$ adapters on Llama 3.1 70B). Then, we compare the performance of prefill-only vs. all-token adapters on a variety of supervised finetuning and reinforcement learning tasks with LMs at varying scales. On SFT, we observe that the evaluation loss of PreFTs is higher than PEFTs, but can be compensated by increasing rank with nearly no reduction in throughput. On RL, we consistently find that PreFTs approach parity with standard PEFTs. Together, this work validates prefill-only adaptation of LLMs as a more favourable accuracy-throughput tradeoff than existing PEFTs for personalised serving.

</details>


### [101] [Unsupervised learning of acquisition variability in structural connectomes via hybrid latent space modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13933)
*Gaurav Rudravaram,Lianrui Zuo,Karthik Ramadass,Elyssa McMaster,Jongyeon Yoon,Aravind R. Krishnan,Adam M. Saunders,Chenyu Gao,Nancy R. Newlin,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Lori L. Beason Held,Murat Bilgel,Laura A. Barquero,Micah DArchangel,Tin Q. Nguyen,Laurie B. Cutting,Derek Archer,Timothy J. Hohman,Daniel C. Moyer,Bennett A. Landman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需手动调参的无监督框架（Joint-VAE），通过架构退火（architectural annealing）在编码器输出与解码器之间平衡离散和连续潜在变量，以分离dMRI结构连接组中的采集效应与生物变异。在N=7,416样本的大规模数据集上，该方法比标准VAE、PCA+k-means及基于损失退火的混合模型更优地捕获采集变异性（ARI=0.53, p<0.05）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dMRI数据因站点、扫描仪和协议差异引入变异性，干扰结构连接组分析。现有降维方法将所有方差建模为连续变量，导致采集效应被吸收；混合潜在空间模型需手动调参来平衡离散与连续成分，缺乏自动化机制。

Method: 提出Joint-VAE：在编码器输出后进行架构退火（architectural annealing），自适应平衡离散与连续潜在变量，无需手动调节容量。数据集包含N=7,416个结构连接组（年龄2-102岁，13项研究，25种采集参数组合），对比标准VAE、PCA+k-means及仅通过损失函数退火的混合模型。

Result: 架构退火方法在站点学习任务上ARI=0.53（p<0.05），优于所有基线。Joint-VAE的混合连续-离散潜在空间能有效恢复与扫描仪和协议差异一致的聚类，而无需人工干预。

Conclusion: 架构退火提供了一种有效的无监督机制，用于分离dMRI中的采集变异性与生物变异。联合建模光滑和类别结构为大规模多站点连接组分析提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Acquisition differences across sites, scanners, and protocols in dMRI introduce variability that complicates structural connectome analysis. This motivates deep learning models that can represent high-dimensional connectomes in a low-dimensional space while explicitly separating acquisition-related effects from biological variation. Conventional dimensionality reduction methods model all variance as continuous, so acquisition effects often get absorbed into a continuous latent space. Recent hybrid latent-space models combine discrete and continuous components to address this, but typically require manual capacity tuning to ensure the discrete component captures the intended variability. We introduce an unsupervised framework that removes this manual tuning by architecturally annealing encoder outputs before decoding, allowing the model to adaptively balance discrete and continuous latent variables during training. To evaluate it, we curated a dataset of N=7,416 structural connectomes derived from dMRI, spanning ages 2 to 102 and 13 studies with 25 unique acquisition-parameter combinations. Of these, 5,900 are cognitively unimpaired, 877 have mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and 639 have Alzheimer's disease (AD). We compare against a standard VAE, PCA with k-means clustering, and hybrid models that anneal only through the loss function. Our architectural annealing produces stronger site learning (ARI=0.53, p<0.05) than these baselines. Results show that a hybrid continuous-discrete latent space, with architectural rather than loss-based annealing, provides a useful unsupervised mechanism for capturing acquisition variability in dMRI: by jointly modeling smooth and categorical structure, the Joint-VAE recovers clusters aligned with scanner and protocol differences.

</details>


### [102] [Towards the Next Frontier of LLMs, Training on Private Data: A Cross-Domain Benchmark for Federated Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13936)
*Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Enrique Zuazua,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于联邦学习的框架，能够在隐私保护前提下，联合多个分散机构对大型语言模型进行参数高效微调，在医疗和金融领域的四个数据集上验证了该方法的表现接近集中式训练，并优于单机构独立学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的进一步发展需要利用分散在多个机构中的私有数据，但这些数据因隐私、监管和组织壁垒而无法共享，且呈非独立同分布（non-IID）特性。

Method: 基于Sherpa.ai联邦学习平台构建框架，采用LoRA、QLoRA和IA3三种参数高效微调策略，在非IID场景下对预训练大语言模型进行联邦联合微调，无需交换原始数据。

Result: 联邦微调表现接近集中式训练，优于单机构独立学习；QLoRA和IA3在效率提升的同时仅带来有限的精度损失。

Conclusion: 联邦参数高效微调是在数据无法共享条件下适配大语言模型的可行方法，兼顾隐私保护与模型性能。

Abstract: The recent success of large language models (LLMs) has been largely driven by vast public datasets. However, the next frontier for LLM development lies beyond public data. Much of the world's most valuable information is private, especially in highly regulated sectors such as healthcare and finance, where data include patient histories or customer communications. Unlocking this data could represent a major leap forward, enabling LLMs with deeper domain expertise and stronger real-world utility. Yet, these data cannot be shared because they are distributed across institutions and constrained by privacy, regulatory, and organizational barriers. Moreover, institutional datasets are typically non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID), differing across sites in population characteristics, data modalities, documentation patterns, and task-specific label distributions.
  In this paper, we demonstrate a practical approach to unlocking private and distributed institutional data for LLM adaptation through federated collaboration across data silos. Built on the Sherpa.ai Federated Learning platform, our framework enables nodes to jointly fine-tune a shared LLM without exchanging private data. We evaluate this approach through a cross-domain benchmark in healthcare and finance, using four closed-ended question answering and classification datasets: MedQA, MedMCQA, FPB, and FiQA-SA. We compare three parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) strategies-LoRA, QLoRA, and IA3-across pretrained backbones under non-IID settings reflecting institutional data heterogeneity. Our results show that federated fine-tuning performs close to centralized training and outperforms isolated single-institution learning. From a Green AI perspective, QLoRA and IA3 improve efficiency with limited accuracy degradation, supporting federated PEFT as a viable approach for adapting LLMs where data cannot be shared.

</details>


### [103] [EvolveMem:Self-Evolving Memory Architecture via AutoResearch for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13941)
*Jiaqi Liu,Xinyu Ye,Peng Xia,Zeyu Zheng,Cihang Xie,Mingyu Ding,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvolveMem提出了一种自进化记忆架构，不仅让存储内容进化，还让检索机制本身（评分函数、融合策略、答案生成策略）也在部署后持续优化。通过LLM驱动的诊断模块分析失败日志、识别根因、调整配置，并配备回滚和探索机制，实现全自动的架构调优。在LoCoMo和MemBench上分别超越最强基线25.7%和18.9%，且进化策略可跨基准迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM agent记忆系统将检索基础设施视为固定不变——存储内容可以演化，但评分函数、融合策略和答案生成策略在部署后保持冻结。要实现真正自适应的记忆，存储知识和检索机制需要共同进化。

Method: EvolveMem将完整检索配置暴露为结构化动作空间，由LLM驱动的诊断模块读取每个问题的失败日志、识别根因并提出配置调整建议；配备带自动回滚和探索机制的 guarded meta-analyzer 执行调整，形成闭环自进化过程（AutoResearch），系统自主进行迭代研究。

Result: 在LoCoMo上超越最强基线25.7%，比最弱基线提升78.0%；在MemBench上超越最强基线18.9%。进化配置可跨基准正迁移，说明学到的是通用检索原理而非数据特定启发式。

Conclusion: EvolveMem验证了记忆系统应实现存储内容与检索机制的双层共同进化，自进化过程能自主发现全新的配置维度，且学到的策略具有跨基准泛化能力。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM agents that operate across multiple sessions, yet existing memory systems treat retrieval infrastructure as fixed: stored content evolves while scoring functions, fusion strategies, and answer-generation policies remain frozen at deployment. We argue that truly adaptive memory requires co-evolution at two levels: the stored knowledge and the retrieval mechanism that queries it. We present EvolveMem, a self-evolving memory architecture that exposes its full retrieval configuration as a structured action space optimized by an LLM-powered diagnosis module. In each evolution round, the module reads per-question failure logs, identifies root causes, and proposes targeted configuration adjustments; a guarded meta-analyzer applies them with automatic revert-on-regression and explore-on-stagnation safeguards. This closed-loop self-evolution realizes an AutoResearch process: the system autonomously conducts iterative research cycles on its own architecture, replacing manual configuration tuning. Starting from a minimal baseline, the process converges autonomously, discovering effective retrieval strategies including entirely new configuration dimensions not present in the original action space. On LoCoMo, EvolveMem outperforms the strongest baseline by 25.7% relative and achieves a 78.0% relative improvement over the minimal baseline. On MemBench, EvolveMem exceeds the strongest baseline by 18.9% relative. Evolved configurations transfer across benchmarks with positive rather than catastrophic transfer, indicating that the self-evolution process captures universal retrieval principles rather than benchmark-specific heuristics. Code is available at https://github.com/aiming-lab/SimpleMem.

</details>


### [104] [Data-Augmented Game Starts for Accelerating Self-Play Exploration in Imperfect Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14379)
*JB Lanier,Nathan Monette,Pierre Baldi,Roy Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出数据增强游戏起始状态采样策略（DAGS），通过从离线人类演示中采样中间状态来初始化强化学习数据收集，以加速大规模不完美信息双人零和博弈中正则化策略梯度方法的在线探索，并解决因稀疏奖励和长视界探索带来的计算难题。实验表明，在固定计算预算下，DAGS能在更具挑战性的探索环境中显著降低可剥削性，同时作者提供了对可能产生的均衡偏差的缓解方法，并发布了新的基准环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模不完美信息竞争游戏（如星际争霸、Dota、反恐精英）中，由于奖励稀疏且探索视界过长，寻找近似均衡在计算上仍然不可行。现有方法难以在合理计算预算下有效探索战略相关的子博弈。

Method: 提出DAGS方法：基于离线人类演示数据覆盖高层策略的假设，从离线数据采样的中间状态初始化强化学习数据收集，引导探索转向战略相关的子博弈。同时提出多任务观测标志来缓解均衡偏差问题。

Result: 在合成数据集和可解析处理的长期控制变体（双人Kuhn扑克、Goofspiel及一个针对隐藏信息偏见的反例游戏）上，固定计算预算下DAGS使正则化策略梯度方法在挑战性探索场景中达到更低的可剥削性。

Conclusion: 增强起始状态分布可以加速不完美信息博弈求解，但可能导致均衡偏差，多任务观测标志是一种简单有效的缓解方案。论文还发布了新的基准环境用于推动该领域研究。

Abstract: Finding approximate equilibria for large-scale imperfect-information competitive games such as StarCraft, Dota, and CounterStrike remains computationally infeasible due to sparse rewards and challenging exploration over long horizons. In this paper, we propose a multi-agent starting-state sampling strategy designed to substantially accelerate online exploration in regularized policy-gradient game methods for two-player zero-sum (2p0s) games. Motivated by an assumption that offline demonstrations from skilled humans can provide good coverage of high-level strategies relevant to equilibrium play, we propose the initialization of reinforcement learning data collection at intermediate states sampled from offline data to facilitate exploration of strategically relevant subgames. Referring to this method as Data-Augmented Game Starts (DAGS), we perform experiments using synthetic datasets and analytically tractable, long-horizon control variants of two-player Kuhn Poker, Goofspiel, and a counterexample game designed to penalize biased beliefs over hidden information. Under fixed computational budgets, DAGS enables regularized policy gradient methods to achieve lower exploitability in games with significantly more challenging exploration. We show that augmenting starting state distributions when solving imperfect information games can lead to biased equilibria, and we provide a straightforward mitigation to this in the form of multi-task observation flags. Finally, we release a new set of benchmark environments that drastically increase exploration challenges and state counts in existing OpenSpiel games while keeping exploitability measurements analytically tractable.

</details>


### [105] [A Unified Geometric Framework for Weighted Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13943)
*Raphael Vock,Edouard Duchesnay,Benoit Dufumier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从距离几何问题（DGP）的视角解释加权InfoNCE对比学习目标函数，揭示了不同加权方案如何决定嵌入空间的几何结构。研究发现：监督对比学习（SupCon）在类别平衡时会恢复经典的正则单纯形几何，但类别不平衡会破坏这种对称性；而Soft SupCon则能保持正则单纯形几何。在连续标签场景中，y-Aware CL存在几何不一致性，而欧几里得-欧几里得加权或X-CLR则能产生唯一的最优嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习旨在通过保留样本间的相似性关系来学习表征，但所得嵌入的几何结构仍未被充分理解。本文旨在通过距离几何问题的视角，系统性地理解不同加权方案下对比学习目标函数的几何特性。

Method: 将加权InfoNCE目标函数重新解释为距离几何问题，通过理论分析推导出监督、弱监督及连续标签场景下最优嵌入的精确表征，并比较不同加权方案（SupCon、Soft SupCon、y-Aware CL、X-CLR等）的几何一致性。

Result: 1）监督分类中，SupCon和Soft SupCon将同类样本坍缩至单一原型；2）类别平衡时SupCon恢复正则单纯形几何，但类别不平衡会破坏对称性，而Soft SupCon在任何类别分布下均保持正则单纯形；3）y-Aware CL的欧几里得标签权重与球形潜在相似性不匹配，无法达到其熵最优；4）欧几里得-欧几里得加权和X-CLR能产生唯一最优嵌入。

Conclusion: 对比学习中加权方案的选择决定了嵌入几何是否可实现、退化或不一致，为设计对比学习目标函数提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Contrastive learning (CL) aims to preserve relational structure between samples by learning representations that reflect a similarity graph. Yet, the geometry of the resulting embeddings remains poorly understood. Here we show that weighted InfoNCE objectives can be interpreted as Distance Geometry Problems, where the weighting scheme specifies the target geometry to be realized by the representation. This viewpoint yields exact characterizations of the optimal embeddings for several supervised and weakly supervised objectives. In supervised classification, both SupCon and Soft SupCon (a dense relaxation of it where pairs from distinct classes have small non-zero similarity) collapse samples within each class to a single prototype. However, while balanced SupCon recovers the classical regular simplex geometry, class imbalance breaks this symmetry: SupCon induces non-uniform inter-class similarities depending on class sizes, whereas Soft SupCon preserves a regular simplex geometry regardless of class imbalance. In continuous-label settings, our framework reveals a different failure mode: y-Aware CL generally cannot attain its entropic optimum unless the labels lie on a hypersphere, exposing a mismatch between Euclidean label weights and spherical latent similarity. By contrast, geometrically consistent choices such as Euclidean-Euclidean weighting or X-CLR admit unique optimal embeddings. Our results show that the choice of weighting scheme determines whether contrastive learning is geometrically realizable, degenerate, or inconsistent, providing a principled framework for designing contrastive objectives.

</details>


### [106] [WarmPrior: Straightening Flow-Matching Policies with Temporal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13959)
*Sinjae Kang,Chanyoung Kim,Kaixin Wang,Li Zhao,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WarmPrior，一种基于近期动作历史构建的时间接地先验分布，用于替代生成式机器人控制策略中的标准高斯源分布。实验证明该方法能显著提升操作任务的成功率，主要归因于更直的路径概率，同时改善强化学习中的探索效率与最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于扩散和流匹配的生成式策略在机器人视觉运动控制中占据主导地位，但标准高斯源分布可能并非最优选择。作者旨在探索源分布这一被忽视的设计维度，通过引入包含时间结构信息的先验分布来提升生成控制策略的性能。

Method: WarmPrior：从近期可用动作历史构建简单的时间接地先验分布，替代标准高斯分布作为生成模型的源分布。该方法可直接应用于扩散/流匹配的行为克隆，以及先验空间中的强化学习探索分布重塑。

Result: WarmPrior在机器人操作任务上持续提高了成功率和样本效率；产生更直的概率路径（类似于Rectified Flow中最优传输耦合的效果）；在强化学习中同时改善了样本效率和最终性能。

Conclusion: 源分布是生成式机器人控制中重要且被低估的设计维度；基于动作历史的WarmPrior提供了一种简单有效且即插即用的改进方案。

Abstract: Generative policies based on diffusion and flow matching have become a dominant paradigm for visuomotor robotic control. We show that replacing the standard Gaussian source distribution with WarmPrior, a simple temporally grounded prior constructed from readily available recent action history, consistently improves success rates on robotic manipulation tasks. We trace this gain to markedly straighter probability paths, echoing the effect of optimal-transport couplings in Rectified Flow. Beyond standard behavior cloning, WarmPrior also reshapes the exploration distribution in prior-space reinforcement learning, improving both sample efficiency and final performance. Collectively, these results identify the source distribution as an important and underexplored design axis in generative robot control.

</details>


### [107] [An Amortized Efficiency Threshold for Comparing Neural and Heuristic Solvers in Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14624)
*Sohaib Afifi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出“神经组合优化求解器训练能耗高因此整体效率低”这一常见批评在逻辑上存在缺陷，提出了摊销效率阈值（AET）框架来量化部署规模对能耗比较的影响，并在MTVRP环境上进行了实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有批评错误地将神经求解器的固定训练成本与元启发式算法的每实例运行成本直接比较，忽略了部署规模这一关键变量。本文旨在纠正这一推理谬误，提供合理的效率比较框架。

Method: 定义摊销效率阈值（AET）作为在给定解质量约束下，神经求解器在总能耗或碳排放上与启发式基线达到平衡所需的最小部署实例数。推导出当神经求解器每实例能耗更低时，累计能耗比趋于小于1的常数，且该极限与训练成本测量方式无关。在MTVRP环境（n=20, 19个变体, 5个随机种子）上以HGS/PyVRP为基线进行实测。

Result: 实测交叉点约为1.58×10^5个部署实例；每实例能耗比为0.41（神经求解器更低）。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于提出了AET框架、开源工具和测量协议；更大问题规模下的结构收敛性留待未来实证研究。

Abstract: A common critique of neural combinatorial-optimization solvers is that they are less energy-efficient than CPU metaheuristics, given the operational energy cost of training them on GPUs. This paper examines the inferential step from "training is expensive" to "neural solvers are net-inefficient", which is where the critique actually goes wrong. Training the network costs a large fixed amount of GPU energy; running the metaheuristic costs a small amount of CPU energy on every instance, repeated as long as the solver is deployed. The two are not commensurable until a deployment volume is fixed. We define the Amortized Efficiency Threshold (AET) as the deployment volume above which a neural solver breaks even with a heuristic baseline in total energy or carbon, under an explicit constraint on solution quality. We show that the cumulative-energy ratio between the two solvers tends to a constant strictly below one whenever the network wins per-instance, and that this limit does not depend on how the training cost was measured. An embodied-carbon term amortizes hardware fabrication symmetrically on both sides. We instantiate the framework on the Multi-Task VRP (MTVRP) environment at n=20 customers across 19 problem variants and five training seeds, with HGS via PyVRP as the heuristic baseline. The measured crossover sits near $1.58 \times 10^5$ deployed instances; the per-instance ratio is 0.41, reflecting the moderate size of the instances tested. The contribution is the framework, the open instrumentation, and the measurement protocol; structural convergence of the ratio at larger problem sizes is left to future empirical work.

</details>


### [108] [Towards Resource-Efficient LLMs: End-to-End Energy Accounting of Distillation Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13981)
*Katherine Lambert,Sasha Luccioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全面衡量蒸馏流程端到端能耗与资源成本的核算框架，通过逐阶段追踪GPU功耗，揭示了传统知识蒸馏和合成数据微调中常被忽视的教师侧能耗（如数据生成、logit缓存、评估等），并构建了能耗-质量帕累托前沿，为在能耗与预算约束下选择蒸馏方法和超参数提供了实用设计准则。同时，作者开源了标准化测量工具和核算协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在推广蒸馏技术时，往往只关注模型压缩带来的收益，却忽略了教师侧数据生成、logit缓存和评估等完整端到端流程中的能耗与资源成本，导致对蒸馏实际能源效率的评估不全面。

Method: 构建分阶段追踪GPU设备功耗的能源核算框架，实验中对两种蒸馏方法（经典logit知识蒸馏和合成数据监督微调）按不同阶段分离并记录实测能耗，建立能耗-质量帕累托前沿。

Result: 获得了两种蒸馏方法在不同阶段的实际能耗与排放数据，构建了能源-质量帕累托前沿，揭示了教师侧先前被忽略的成本，并得出了在能耗和预算约束下选择蒸馏方法与超参数的设计规则。

Conclusion: 蒸馏的能源效率评估必须纳入教师侧完整流程的能耗；所提出的开源测量框架和核算协议可为可重复、可比较的蒸馏研究提供标准化基础。

Abstract: The rise in deployment of large language models has driven a surge in GPU demand and datacenter scaling, raising concerns about electricity use, grid stress, and the impacts of modern AI workloads. Distillation is often promoted as one of the most effective paths to obtain cheaper, more efficient models, yet these claims rarely account for the full end-to-end energy and resource costs, including crucial teacher-side workloads such as data generation, logit caching, and evaluation. We present a comprehensive energy accounting framework that measures the complete computational cost of distillation pipelines via detailed stage-wise tracking of GPU device power consumption. In our experiments, we separate and log empirical energy use across distinct phases and systematically measure the energy and emissions of two common distillation methods: the classic logit-based knowledge distillation and synthetic-data supervised fine-tuning, constructing energy-quality Pareto frontiers that expose the previously ignored costs. From these measurements and analyses, we derive practical design rules for selecting distillation methods and hyperparameters under energy and budget constraints, and release an open-source measurement harness and accounting protocol to provide a standardized foundation for comparable, reproducible distillation research, explicitly accountable for complete pipeline energy impact.

</details>


### [109] [Controlling Logical Collapse in LLMs via Algebraic Ontology Projection over F2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12968)
*Hisashi Miyashita,Mgnite Inc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出代数本体投影（AOP），将LLM隐藏状态投影到伽罗瓦域F2中，仅用42个关系对作为代数密钥，在零样本条件下对未见概念对实现最高93.33%的包含关系准确率。同时提出语义结晶化（SC）指标量化F2约束满足度，并揭示系统提示作为代数边界条件、指令微调防止"晚层坍塌"等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型内部是否以形式化可验证的代数结构编码本体论关系，即LLM的推理过程是否具有可形式化的代数基础，而不仅仅是统计近似。

Method: 提出代数本体投影（AOP），将LLM隐藏状态投影到F2域（基于里斯科夫替换原则约束），仅用42个关系对作为代数密钥进行投影；提出语义结晶化（SC）指标，量化F2约束满足度并与随机基线对比，用于预测零样本准确率。

Result: AOP在Gemma-2 Instruct上实现93.33%零样本包含准确率，多个模型族上稳定达到86.67%；发现系统提示与指令微调的组合是防止晚层坍塌的关键，7/10条件下观察到晚层坍塌现象。

Conclusion: LLM的前向计算可被重新理解为迭代的代数组织过程，其逻辑结构不仅是近似得到的，而是形式上可访问的，为构建逻辑结构可验证的LLM开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Do large language models internally encode ontological relations in a formally verifiable algebraic structure? We introduce Algebraic Ontology Projection (AOP), which projects LLM hidden states into the Galois Field F2 under Liskov Substitution Principle constraints, using only 42 relational pairs as algebraic keys. AOP achieves up to 93.33% zero-shot inclusion accuracy on unseen concept pairs (Gemma-2 Instruct with optimized prompt), with consistent 86.67% accuracy observed across multiple model families -- with no model tuning, but through prompt alone.
  This algebraic structure is strongly layer-dependent. We introduce Semantic Crystallisation (SC), a metric that quantifies F2 constraint satisfaction relative to a random baseline and predicts zero-shot accuracy without held-out data. System prompts act as algebraic boundary conditions: only their combination with instruction tuning prevents Late-layer Collapse -- a systematic degradation of logical consistency in the final layers, observed in 7 of 10 conditions. These findings reframe forward computation as an iterative process of algebraic organisation, and open a path toward LLMs whose logical structure is not merely approximated, but formally accessible.

</details>


### [110] [TabPFN-3: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13986)
*Léo Grinsztajn,Klemens Flöge,Oscar Key,Felix Birkel,Philipp Jund,Brendan Roof,Mihir Manium,Shi Bin,Hoo,Magnus Bühler,Anurag Garg,Dominik Safaric,Jake Robertson,Benjamin Jäger,Simone Alessi,Adrian Hayler,Vladyslav Moroshan,Lennart Purucker,Philipp Singer,Alan Arazi,Julien Siems,Jan Hendrik Metzen,Georg Grab,Nick Erickson,Siyuan Guo,Eliott Kalfon,Simon Bing,David Salinas,Clara Cornu,Lilly Charlotte Wehrhahn,Diana Kriuchkova,Kursat Kaya,Lydia Sidhoum,Marie Salmon,Jerry Chen,Madelon Hulsebos,Yann LeCun,Samuel Müller,Bernhard Schölkopf,Sauraj Gambhir,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-3是一个可扩展至100万训练样本的表格数据基础模型，在TabArena基准上显著超越所有现有模型（包括调参和集成基线），且推理速度比TabPFN-2.5快20倍。其API版本TabPFN-3-Plus(Thinking)引入测试时计算缩放，在TabArena上超过非TabPFN模型200+ Elo，比AutoGluon 1.5 extreme快10倍。模型还实现了对时间序列、关系数据和表格-文本数据的最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据支撑着科学和工业中大多数高价值预测问题，TabPFN已推动了该领域的基础模型革命。现有模型在规模、速度和性能上仍有提升空间，且缺乏对多样化数据类型（时间序列、关系数据、表格-文本）的统一处理能力。基于用户反馈，作者旨在将TabPFN扩展至100万训练样本、显著降低训练和推理时间，并拓展到更多数据类型。

Method: TabPFN-3完全在来自先验分布的合成数据上预训练，引入了测试时计算缩放（test-time compute scaling）到表格基础模型。通过缩减KV缓存和行分块（row-chunking）技术，实现在单张H100上扩展到100万行数据。此外提供了针对时间序列的专用检查点TabPFN-TS-3。

Result: 在TabArena上，前向传播显著优于所有其他模型（包括调参和集成基线），在速度/性能前沿上占据帕累托优势。在更多类别数据集上排名第一，在多达100万训练行和200特征的数集上击败8小时调参的梯度提升树基线。TabPFN-3-Plus(Thinking)在TabArena上超过非TabPFN模型200+ Elo（最大数据子集上420 Elo），比AutoGluon 1.5 extreme快10倍。在RelBenchV1上取得新SOTA，在TabSTAR上取得SOTA，TabPFN-TS-3在fev-bench上排名第二，SHAP值计算快120倍，整体速度比TabPFN-2.5快20倍。

Conclusion: TabPFN-3大幅推进了表格预测的前沿，在保持速度优势的同时实现了规模化和多数据类型扩展，证明了纯合成数据预训练和测试时计算缩放的有效性，为表格基础模型确立了新的性能标准。

Abstract: Tabular data underpins most high-value prediction problems in science and industry, and TabPFN has driven the foundation model revolution for this modality. Designed with feedback from our users, TabPFN-3 builds on this foundation to scale state-of-the-art performance to datasets with 1M training rows and substantially reduce training and inference time. Pretrained exclusively on synthetic data from our prior, TabPFN-3 dramatically pushes the frontier of tabular prediction and brings substantial gains on time series, relational, and tabular-text data. On the standard tabular benchmark TabArena, a forward pass of TabPFN-3 outperforms all other models, including tuned and ensembled baselines, by a significant margin, and pareto-dominates the speed/performance frontier. On more diverse datasets, TabPFN-3 ranks first on datasets with many classes, and beats 8-hour-tuned gradient-boosted-tree baselines on datasets up to 1M training rows and 200 features. TabPFN-3 introduces test-time compute scaling to tabular foundation models. Our API offering TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking) exploits this to beat all non-TabPFN models by over 200 Elo on TabArena, rising to 420 Elo on the largest data subset, and outperforms AutoGluon 1.5 extreme while being 10x faster, without using LLMs, real data, internet search or any other model besides TabPFN. TabPFN-3 extends the capabilities of our models, enabling SOTA prediction on relational data (new SOTA foundation model on RelBenchV1) and tabular-text data (SOTA on TabSTAR via TabPFN-3-Plus); and improves existing integrations: a specialized checkpoint, TabPFN-TS-3, ranks 2nd on the time-series benchmark fev-bench, and SHAP-value computation is up to 120x faster. TabPFN-3 achieves this performance while being up to 20x faster than TabPFN-2.5. In addition, a reduced KV cache and row-chunking scale to 1M rows on one H100 with fast inference speed.

</details>


### [111] [Neural Fields for NV-Center Inverse Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13988)
*Zhixuan Zhao,Tao Zhong,Yixun Hu,Nathalie P. de Leon,Christine Allen-Blanchette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对NV色心量子传感中的非线性逆问题，提出NeTMY方法——一种免摊销的坐标神经场耦合可微正向模型，通过退火位置编码、多尺度优化、稀疏性门控和频谱保真度损失，解决了传统方法在稀疏自旋源磁噪声重构中的中心塌缩失败模式，实现了最优定位与分布指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学传感中的逆问题常依赖手工正则化器或监督学习，但当正向模型具有非线性、光谱耦合且物理敏感时，这些方法均会失效。NV色心量子传感中，传统标量/相干正向近似导致逆问题景观失真，自由密度优化出现中心塌缩失败模式，亟需一种物理保真的神经逆问题求解方案。

Method: 提出NeTMY：①将传统标量/相干正向近似替换为张量功率求和偶极算子，修正逆问题景观；②构建免摊销的坐标神经场，耦合可微NV正向模型；③采用退火位置编码与多尺度优化策略；④引入稀疏性门控机制和频谱保真度损失函数。

Result: 在修正算子生成的稀疏合成重建基准测试中，NeTMY在定位和分布指标上均取得最佳性能。机制实验表明NeTMY不直接执行原始密度空间梯度，其参数化平滑并重新分布更新，有效缓解了中心塌缩病理。

Conclusion: NeTMY方法证明了物理保真的神经逆问题求解器在NV量子传感中的有效性，并将NV量子传感定位为物理保真神经逆问题的理想测试平台。

Abstract: Inverse problems in scientific sensing are often solved with either hand-designed regularizers or supervised networks trained on simulated labels, yet both can fail when the forward model is nonlinear, spectrally coupled, and physically delicate. We study this issue for noise sensing based on nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond, where a quantum sensor measures magnetic-noise spectra generated by sparse spin sources. We show that replacing a common scalar/coherent forward approximation with a tensor power-summed dipolar operator changes the inverse landscape and exposes a center-collapse failure mode in free-density optimization. We propose NeTMY, an amortization-free coordinate neural field coupled to the differentiable NV forward model, with annealed positional encoding, multiscale optimization, sparsity/gating, and spectrum-fidelity losses. Across sparse synthetic reconstructions generated by the corrected operator, NeTMY achieves the best localization and distributional metrics in the tested benchmark. Mechanism experiments show that NeTMY does not directly execute the raw density-space gradient; its parameterization smooths and redistributes updates, mitigating the center-collapse pathology. These results position NV quantum sensing as a useful testbed for physics-faithful neural inverse problems.

</details>


### [112] [HodgeCover: Higher-Order Topological Coverage Drives Compression of Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13997)
*Tao Zhong,Dongzhe Zheng,Christine Allen-Blanchette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示稀疏MoE层中专家合并存在不可约三元环障碍（谐和核），并提出HodgeCover方法，通过2-复形上的Hodge分解识别并覆盖这些关键边/三角形，在激进压缩下达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练压缩方法仅基于两两专家间的兼容性进行评分和合并，但存在一个根本性缺陷：三个专家可能两两兼容却形成不可约的合并环路，导致任何仅依赖成对信号的评分方法在结构上无法识别哪些三元组是可合并的。需要一种能暴露并处理这种高阶障碍的压缩框架。

Method: 将专家视为顶点，KL散度屏障作为边权重，三元组屏障作为面权重，构建2-复形（simplicial 2-complex）。对边屏障信号进行Hodge分解，分离出谐和核（harmonic kernel）——即不可约三元环对应的精确数学对象。提出HodgeCover算法：贪婪覆盖谐和核关键边及三角形关键面；混合变体进一步结合现成的权重剪枝。

Result: 在三个开源稀疏MoE模型上，在激进专家缩减设置下：1）HodgeCover在纯专家缩减轴上匹配SOTA无监督基线；2）在混合轴（专家缩减+权重剪枝）的激进压缩前沿上领先；3）在所有四个Hodge分量上保持均衡的质量保留。

Conclusion: 暴露MoE结构的谐和核改变了压缩器的性能格局，使更优的压缩器在最关键的激进压缩场景下胜出。HodgeCover方法为无训练MoE压缩提供了新的理论视角和实用工具。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers route tokens through a handful of experts, and learning-free compression of these layers reduces inference cost without retraining. A subtle obstruction blocks every existing compressor in this family: three experts can each be pairwise compatible yet form an irreducible cycle when merged together, so any score that ranks experts on pairwise signals is structurally blind to which triples are jointly mergeable. We show the obstruction is a precise mathematical object, the harmonic kernel of the simplicial Laplacian on a 2-complex whose vertices are experts, whose edges carry KL merge barriers, and whose faces carry triplet barriers; Hodge-decomposing the edge-barrier signal isolates the kernel exactly. We turn the diagnostic into a selection objective: HodgeCover greedily covers the harmonic-critical edges and triplet-critical triangles, and a hybrid variant of HodgeCover pairs it with off-the-shelf weight pruning on survivors. On three open-weight Sparse MoE backbones under aggressive expert reduction, HodgeCover matches state-of-the-art learning-free baselines on the expert-reduction axis, leads on the aggressive-compression frontier of the hybrid axis, and uniquely balances retained mass across all four Hodge components. These results show that exposing the harmonic kernel of a learned MoE structure changes which compressor wins at the regime that matters most.

</details>


### [113] [Beyond Binary: Reframing GUI Critique as Continuous Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14311)
*Yuchen Sun,Pei Fu,Shaojie Zhang,Anan Du,Xiuwen Xi,Ruoceng Zhang,Zhenbo Luo,Jian Luan,Chongyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BBCritic，将GUI批判从二元分类范式转变为基于功能等价假设的度量学习问题，通过两阶段对比学习在3B参数下超越7B SOTA模型，并构建了首个细粒度等级评估基准BBBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI批判模型采用二元分类，导致有效动作与看似合理但无效的干扰项得分严重纠缠，原因在于两个结构缺陷：Affordance Collapse（层级功能空间被压缩为0/1标签）和Noise Sensitivity（二元目标对噪声决策边界过拟合）。

Method: 基于Functional Equivalence Hypothesis，通过两阶段对比学习将指令和动作对齐到共享的Affordance Space中，恢复被二元监督压平的层级结构。同时构建BBBench基准，包含密集动作空间和四级层级分类体系。

Result: BBCritic-3B无需额外标注，在多个指标上超越7B参数的SOTA二元模型，并展现出跨平台跨任务的强零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: GUI批判本质上是度量学习问题而非分类问题，超越二元的范式转变能更有效地恢复动作空间的层级结构，提升细粒度排序能力。

Abstract: Test-Time Scaling (TTS), which samples multiple candidate actions and ranks them via a Critic Model, has emerged as a promising paradigm for generalist GUI agents. Its efficacy thus hinges on the critic's fine-grained ranking ability. However, existing GUI critic models uniformly adopt binary classification. Our motivational analysis of these models exposes a severe entanglement: scores for valid actions and plausible-but-invalid distractors become indistinguishable. We attribute this failure to two structural defects: Affordance Collapse--the hierarchical affordance space is compressed into 0/1 labels; and Noise Sensitivity--binary objectives overfit to noisy decision boundaries. To resolve this, we introduce BBCritic (Beyond-Binary Critic), a paradigm shift grounded in the Functional Equivalence Hypothesis. Through two-stage contrastive learning, BBCritic aligns instructions and actions in a shared Affordance Space, recovering the hierarchical structure that binary supervision flattens. We also present BBBench (Beyond-Binary Bench), the first GUI critic benchmark that pairs a dense action space with a hierarchical four-level taxonomy, enabling fine-grained ranking evaluation. Experimental results show that BBCritic-3B, trained without any extra annotation, outperforms 7B-parameter SOTA binary models. It demonstrates strong zero-shot transferability across platforms and tasks, supporting our methodological view: GUI critique is fundamentally a metric-learning problem, not a classification one.

</details>


### [114] [Support Before Frequency in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13999)
*Adrian Müller,Antoine Gonon,Zebang Shen,Ya-Ping Hsieh,Niao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 离散扩散模型在语言建模中表现日益突出，但去噪目标如何组织学习尚不清楚。本文证明，在均匀扩散和吸收（掩码）扩散中，小噪声区域（最终去噪步骤）的逆向过程会形成层级结构：粗粒度的支撑信息（如语法有效性）和细粒度的频率信息。恢复有效性结构只需学习逆向概率的正确数量级，而恢复数据频率则需要系数级估计。实验支持这一预测：支撑定位先于频率排序出现，均匀扩散与吸收扩散的对比符合预测的速率分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示离散扩散模型中去噪目标如何组织学习，特别是模型如何从数据中学习支撑信息（如语法有效性）与频率信息之间的层级关系。

Method: 理论分析证明在均匀扩散和吸收扩散的小噪声区域中，每个单token逆向编辑分解为主导尺度（判断是否向数据支撑移动）和精细系数（同一尺度内的相对概率）；通过实验在掩码语言扩散模型和合成正则语言任务上验证预测。

Result: 支撑定位（学习数据支撑结构）先于支撑内频率排序出现；均匀扩散与吸收扩散的对比符合理论预测的速率分离。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型先学习数据支撑（如语法有效性）再学习数据频率；恢复有效性只需数量级估计，恢复频率需要更精细的系数级估计；去噪目标存在内在的层级学习机制。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are increasingly competitive for language modeling, yet it remains unclear how their denoising objectives organize learning. Although these objectives target the full data distribution, we show that the exact reverse process induces a hierarchy between coarse support information and finer frequency information. For uniform and absorbing (a.k.a. masking) diffusion, we prove that, in the small-noise regime of the final denoising steps, each single-token reverse edit decomposes into a leading scale, determined by whether it moves toward the data support (e.g., grammatically valid sentences), and a finer coefficient, determining relative probabilities within the same scale. Thus, recovering validity structure only requires learning the correct order of magnitude of reverse probabilities, whereas recovering data frequencies requires coefficient-level estimation. The separation is mechanism-dependent: uniform diffusion exhibits a trichotomy into validity-improving, validity-preserving, and validity-worsening edits, while absorbing diffusion places its leading-order mass on validity-improving moves. Experiments on a masked language diffusion model and synthetic regular-language tasks support these predictions: support-localization emerges earlier than within-support frequency ranking, and the contrast between uniform and absorbing diffusion matches the predicted rate separation. Together, our results suggest that discrete diffusion models learn data support before data frequencies.

</details>


### [115] [Dywave: Event-Aligned Dynamic Tokenization for Heterogeneous IoT Sensing Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14014)
*Tomoyoshi Kimura,Denizhan Kara,Jinyang Li,Hongjue Zhao,Yigong Hu,Yizhuo Chen,Xiaomin Ouyang,Shengzhong Liu,Tarek Abdelzaher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dywave，一种基于小波层次分解的动态令牌化框架，用于物联网传感信号，通过识别语义事件边界并自适应压缩冗余区间，在5个真实数据集上实现高达12%的准确率提升和75%的输入令牌长度缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网系统持续采集的异构传感信号具有非平稳、多尺度特性，标准令牌化技术难以有效处理这些信号。

Method: Dywave利用基于小波的层次化分解，识别与潜在语义事件对应的有意义时间边界，自适应压缩冗余区间同时保持时间连贯性，构建与内在时间结构和物理事件对齐的紧凑输入表示。

Result: 在人类活动识别、压力评估、附近物体检测等5个真实物联网传感数据集上，Dywave相比现有最优方法准确率提升达12%，主流序列模型输入令牌长度减少达75%，且对领域偏移和可变序列长度具有更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Dywave是一种有效的动态令牌化框架，能够显著提升物联网传感信号处理的计算效率和模型性能。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) systems continuously collect heterogeneous sensing signals from ubiquitous sensors to support intelligent applications such as human activity analysis, emotion monitoring, and environmental perception. These signals are inherently non-stationary and multi-scale, posing unique challenges for standard tokenization techniques. This paper proposes Dywave, a dynamic tokenization framework for IoT sensing signals that constructs compact input representations aligned with intrinsic temporal structures and underlying physical events. Dywave leverages wavelet-based hierarchical decomposition, identifies meaningful temporal boundaries corresponding to underlying semantic events, and adaptively compresses redundant intervals while preserving temporal coherence. Extensive evaluations on five real-world IoT sensing datasets across activity recognition, stress assessment, and nearby object detection demonstrate that Dywave outperforms state-of-the-art methods by up to 12% in accuracy, while improving computational efficiency by reducing input token lengths by up to 75% across mainstream sequence models. Moreover, Dywave exhibits improved robustness to domain shifts and varying sequence lengths.

</details>


### [116] [R2R2: Robust Representation for Intensive Experience Reuse via Redundancy Reduction in Self-Predictive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14026)
*Sanghyeob Song,Donghyeok Lee,Jinsik Kim,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对高数据重用（高UTD）下自预测学习（SPL）中的表征过拟合问题，提出了一种基于冗余缩减的鲁棒表征正则化方法R2R2。理论分析发现SPL的谱性质与标准零中心化冲突，设计了非中心化目标。在TD7和SimbaV2-SPL上验证，在11个连续控制任务中，UTD=20时提升TD7约22%，并刷新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据稀缺场景（如真实机器人）下的强化学习需要高频率数据重用，但会导致过拟合。现有工作主要关注评论员偏差问题，而自预测学习在高更新-数据比（UTD）下表征层面的不稳定性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出R2R2方法，作为SPL中的正则化技术。理论分析了标准零中心化与SPL谱性质的冲突，据此设计了非中心化目标。在原生SPL算法TD7上验证，并将SPL模块融入原本缺乏SPL的SimbaV2，构建SimbaV2-SPL展示正交性。

Result: 在11个连续控制任务上，UTD=20时，R2R2使TD7提升约22%；在SimbaV2-SPL基础上进一步带来增益，SimbaV2-SPL本身即达到新的SOTA。

Conclusion: R2R2有效缓解高UTD下的表征过拟合问题，与现有方法正交，可进一步提升SPL类算法的性能。

Abstract: For reinforcement learning in data-scarce domains like real-world robotics, intensive data reuse enhances efficiency but induces overfitting. While prior works focus on critic bias, representation-level instability in Self-Predictive Learning (SPL) under high Update-to-Data (UTD) regimes remains underexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose Robust Representation via Redundancy Reduction (R2R2), a regularization method within SPL. We theoretically identify that standard zero-centering conflicts with SPL's spectral properties and design a non-centered objective accordingly. We verify R2R2 on SPL-native algorithms like TD7. Furthermore, to demonstrate its orthogonality to prior advancements, we extend the state-of-the-art SimbaV2, which originally lacks SPL, by integrating a tailored SPL module, termed SimbaV2-SPL. Experiments across 11 continuous control tasks confirm that R2R2 effectively mitigates overfitting; specifically, at a UTD ratio of 20, it improves TD7 by ~22% and provides additional gains on top of SimbaV2-SPL, which itself establishes a new state-of-the-art. The code can be found at: https://github.com/songsang7/R2R2

</details>


### [117] [Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention: Learning When to Write by Predicting Future Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14037)
*Gergely Szilvasy,Manuel Faysse,Maria Lomeli,Matthijs Douze,Pierre-Emmanuel Mazaré,Loïc Cabannes,Wen-tau Yih,Hervé Jégou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention (SP-KV)，通过轻量级效用预测器对KV对进行评分，动态剪枝低效用KV对，实现3-10倍KV缓存压缩，显著提升内存效率和解码速度，且不损害模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列生成场景下，Transformer的KV缓存内存占用和带宽成为效率瓶颈，亟需一种高效且自适应的KV缓存缩减机制。

Method: SP-KV包含一个轻量级效用预测器，对每个KV对评分；通过局部窗口保留近期KV，全局注意力中仅保留预测效用高于阈值的旧KV对；与LLM端到端联合训练，仅使用下一个token预测损失。

Result: 动态稀疏化实现3-10倍KV缓存压缩，长序列压缩率更高；极大提升内存使用和生成速度，验证损失和下游任务性能几乎无损；揭示了层和头特定的稀疏模式。

Conclusion: SP-KV是一种有效的KV缓存缩减机制，可动态适应输入，同时其发现的稀疏模式可为混合局部-全局注意力架构设计提供指导。

Abstract: Under modern test-time compute and agentic paradigms, language models process ever-longer sequences. Efficient text generation with transformer architectures is increasingly constrained by the Key-Value cache memory footprint and bandwidth. To address this limitation, we introduce Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention (SP-KV), a mechanism designed to predict future KV utility in order to reduce the size of the long-term KV cache. This strategy operates at a fine granularity: a lightweight utility predictor scores each key-value pair, and while recent KVs are always available via a local window, older pairs are written in the cache and used in global attention only if their predicted utility surpasses a given threshold. The LLM and the utility predictor are trained jointly end-to-end exclusively through next-token prediction loss, and are adapted from pretrained LLM checkpoints.
  Rather than enforcing a fixed compression ratio, SP-KV performs dynamic sparsification: the mechanism adapts to the input and typically reduces the KV cache size by a factor of $3$ to $10\times$, longer sequences often being more compressible. This leads to vast improvements in memory usage and decoding speed, with little to no degradation of validation loss nor performance on a broad set of downstream tasks. Beyond serving as an effective KV-cache reduction mechanism, our method reveals structured layer- and head-specific sparsity patterns that we can use to guide the design of hybrid local-global attention architectures.

</details>


### [118] [Reliability-Gated Source Anchoring for Continual Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14063)
*Vikash Singh,Debargha Ganguly,Weicong Chen,Sabyasachi Sahoo,Sreehari Sankar,Biyao Zhang,Mohsen Harir,Shouren Wang,Osama Zafar,Christian Gagné,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有持续测试时自适应（CTTA）方法在源模型严重退化时仍盲目使用源锚点，导致性能崩溃。本文提出RMemSafe，通过基于预测熵的可靠性门控机制动态减弱源耦合项，在源模型失效时退化为无源回退方案，在CCC-Hard基准上显著提升性能，且对源退化具有更平缓的损伤斜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有源锚点CTTA方法（如ROID）假设源模型始终可靠，但在真实场景中源模型可能严重退化（如CCC-Hard上ResNet-50仅1.3% top-1准确率），此时固定强度的源锚点反而有害，导致"盲目锚定"失败模式。

Method: 提出RMemSafe：利用冻结源模型的归一化预测熵作为可靠性门控信号。当源后验趋近均匀分布（高熵）时，门控关闭，消除源锚点和一致性过滤项，目标函数退化为仅含ROID基础损失与边缘校准的无源回退方案；否则正常使用源锚点。

Result: 在9个匹配划分的持续损坏单元格中，RMemSafe+ASR在8个上取得最低错误率，在全部9个上是最优的基于重置的方法；相比ROID+ASR，ResNet-50提升1.05个百分点，ViT-B/16提升0.48个百分点；源退化扫描实验中损伤斜率仅为ROID+ASR的1/1.13。

Conclusion: RMemSafe通过可微分门控机制实现了CTTA中对源模型可靠性的自适应，验证了门控仅响应高熵源崩溃（而非低熵但错误的情况），实现了源退化时的优雅降级。

Abstract: Continual test-time adaptation (CTTA) updates a pretrained model online on an unlabeled, non-stationary stream while anchoring it to a frozen source checkpoint. This anchor is useful only when the source remains reliable. On CCC-Hard, however, a ResNet-50 source falls to approximately $1.3\%$ top-$1$ accuracy, while existing source-anchored CTTA methods continue applying the same anchor strength. We call this failure mode blind anchoring and propose RMemSafe, a reliability-gated extension of ROID that uses the frozen source's normalized predictive entropy to attenuate all explicit source-coupled uses in the objective. When the source posterior approaches uniformity, the gate closes: the source anchor and agreement filter vanish, and the objective reduces to a source-agnostic fallback comprising ROID's base losses plus marginal calibration. Combined with ASR, RMemSafe achieves the lowest error on $8$ of $9$ matched-split continual-corruption cells and is the best reset-based method on all $9$, improving ROID+ASR by $1.05$~pp on ResNet-50 and $0.48$~pp on ViT-B/16. A controlled source-degradation sweep shows a $1.13{\times}$ shallower harm slope than ROID+ASR, consistent with the graceful-decay prediction. The entropy gate detects high-entropy source collapse, not confidently wrong low-entropy sources; this scope is explicitly evaluated and discussed.

</details>


### [119] [SurF: A Generative Model for Multivariate Irregular Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14069)
*Mohammad R. Rezaei,Tejas Balaji,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SurF是一种基于时间重标定理（TRT）的生成模型，将事件序列与i.i.d.单位速率指数噪声之间建立可学习双射，实现了跨异构事件流数据集的统一训练。该模型采用三种高效累积强度参数化和Transformer编码器，在六个真实世界基准上取得最优或接近最优的性能，朝着异步事件流基础模型迈出了第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不规则采样的多元事件流生成建模面临两大瓶颈：(1) 基于分词的方法无法处理变化幅度达数量级的间隔时间；(2) 神经时序点过程受限于窗口级数值积分。现有方法缺乏跨异构数据集的统一建模能力。

Method: (i) 利用时间重标定理（TRT）作为可学习双射，将事件序列映射为i.i.d.单位速率指数噪声；(ii) 提出三种高效累积强度函数参数化方案以扩展至长序列；(iii) 采用基于Transformer的编码器实现多数据集预训练。

Result: 在六个真实世界基准上，SurF在Earthquake、Retweet和Taobao数据集上取得了最佳时间RMSE；在其余三个数据集上与专用最优模型相比仅相差试验级噪声。在严格的留一法协议下，保留的检查点在5/6数据集上击败了所有经典和神经自回归基线，在Amazon和Earthquake上击败了所有基线。

Conclusion: SurF是朝着异步事件流基础模型迈出的初始步骤，证明了跨异构事件流数据集进行统一预训练的可行性。

Abstract: Irregularly sampled multivariate event streams remain a stubbornly difficult modality for generative modeling: tokenization-based approaches break down when inter-event intervals vary by orders of magnitude, and neural temporal point processes are bottlenecked by window-level numerical quadrature. We (i) propose SurF, a generative model that uses the Time Rescaling Theorem (TRT) as a learnable bijection between event sequences and i.i.d.\ unit-rate exponential noise, enabling a single model to be trained across heterogeneous event-stream datasets; (ii) three efficient parameterizations of the cumulative intensity that scale to long sequences; and (iii) a Transformer-based encoder for multi-dataset pretraining. On six real-world benchmarks, SurF achieves the best reported time RMSE on Earthquake, Retweet, and Taobao, and is within trial-level noise of the strongest specialist on the remaining three. Under a strict leave-one-out protocol, the held-out checkpoint beats every classical and neural-autoregressive baseline on 5/6 datasets and beats every baseline on Amazon and Earthquake, an initial step toward foundation models over asynchronous event streams.

</details>


### [120] [Fair and Calibrated Toxicity Detection with Robust Training and Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14074)
*Mokshit Surana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 毒性分类中的公平性涉及排序、校准和弃权三个维度。训练时干预与事后安全机制不能独立评估。本文比较了ERM、实例重加权和Group DRO在这三个维度上的表现，发现：(1)校准差异是隐藏的公平性问题：ERM全局校准良好但各子群严重失准；(2)训练干预只是转移而非消除差异：重加权改善排序但恶化校准差距，DRO消除校准差异但代价是全局失准；(3)事后方法继承训练失败模式：温度缩放因非均匀失准而失败，置信度弃权在DRO下失效；(4)弃权本身不公平：置信度弃权对背景内容比对涉及身份的内容更有利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性研究多关注单一维度（如排序或校准），忽视了毒性分类中公平性涉及排序、校准和弃权三个相互交织的维度，且训练时干预与事后安全机制存在依赖关系。本文旨在综合评估不同方法在这三个维度上的表现，揭示隐藏的公平性问题。

Method: 比较ERM、实例级重加权和Group DRO三种训练方法，结合温度缩放、基于置信度的弃权和每身份阈值优化等事后机制。使用子群AUC、BPSN/BNSP AUC、误差差距和每子群预期校准误差（ECE）进行评测，采用bootstrap置信区间（n=1000）。

Result: (1)ERM全局校准误差0.013但各身份子群ECE偏差+0.029至+0.134；(2)重加权ERM提升BPSN AUC 0.06-0.12但校准-公平差距恶化至+0.232，DRO全局ECE达0.118；(3)温度缩放因非均匀失准失效，置信度弃权在DRO下风险-覆盖曲线随延迟上升；(4)置信度弃权对背景内容的帮助远大于对涉及身份的内容。

Conclusion: SRAI公平性需要多维度框架：仅在某一维度（如排序）上表现相同的方法可能在决定实际危害的失败模式上存在显著差异。校准差异是隐藏的公平性违规，训练干预仅转移而非消除差异，事后方法继承训练失败模式，且弃权本身不公平。

Abstract: Fairness in toxicity classification involves three integrated axes: ranking, calibration, and abstention. Training-time interventions and post-hoc safety mechanisms cannot be evaluated independently because the former determines the efficacy of the latter. We compare Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM), instance-level reweighting, and Group DRO across these axes, combined with temperature scaling, confidence-based abstention, and per-identity threshold optimization. Evaluation uses subgroup AUC, BPSN/BNSP AUC, error gaps, and per-subgroup Expected Calibration Error (ECE) with bootstrap CIs ($n = 1000$).
  We report four findings. (1) Calibration disparity is a hidden fairness violation. ERM has near-perfect aggregate calibration ($0.013$) but is significantly miscalibrated across all identity subgroups ($+0.029$ to $+0.134$). (2) Training interventions reshape rather than eliminate disparity. Reweighted ERM improves ranking (BPSN AUC $+0.06$ to $+0.12$) but worsens the calibration-fairness gap by up to $+0.232$. Group DRO eliminates calibration disparity but only by becoming uniformly miscalibrated globally (ECE $0.118$). (3) Post-hoc methods inherit training failure modes. Temperature scaling fails because miscalibration is non-uniform. Confidence-based abstention works under ERM but breaks under DRO, where the risk-coverage curve rises with deferral. (4) Abstention itself is unfair. Confidence-based deferral helps background content far more than identity-mentioning content. We argue that SRAI fairness requires a multi-axis framework: methods that differ only in aggregate ranking can differ sharply in failure modes that determine real-world harm.

</details>


### [121] [Rethinking Layer Relevance in Large Language Models Beyond Cosine Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14075)
*Cristian Hinostroza,Rodrigo Toro Icarte,Christ Devia,Andres Carvallo De Ferari,Eugenio Herrera-Berg,Denis Parra,Jorge F Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明余弦相似度是评估Transformer各层重要性的不良指标，并提出以实际移除层后的准确率下降作为更鲁棒的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型内部机制的理解对于开发更可解释和优化的架构至关重要，现有方法广泛使用余弦相似度评估层相关性，但该指标的有效性尚未得到充分验证。

Method: 首先通过理论分析证明余弦相似度可能任意低而层仍然至关重要；其次在多种LLM上进行实证实验，验证余弦相似度与实际性能下降之间的相关性通常较弱或中等；最后提出以移除某一层后模型准确率的实际下降值作为更准确的层重要性评估指标。

Result: 理论分析表明余弦相似度与层重要性的真实表现存在极大偏差；经验证据显示两者相关性弱或中等，余弦相似度会导致对Transformer内部机制的误导性解释；基于实际性能下降的指标虽计算成本高但更准确。

Conclusion: 研究对开发可解释LLM具有重要意义，强调需要超越余弦相似度来评估层相关性，所提指标可支持更明智的剪枝策略和轻量化模型设计。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have revolutionized natural language processing. Understanding their internal mechanisms is crucial for developing more interpretable and optimized architectures. Mechanistic interpretability has led to the development of various methods for assessing layer relevance, with cosine similarity being a widely used tool in the field. On this work, we demonstrate that cosine similarity is a poor proxy for the actual performance degradation caused by layer removal. Our theoretical analysis shows that a layer can exhibit an arbitrarily low cosine similarity score while still being crucial to the model's performance. On the other hand, empirical evidence from a range of LLMs confirms that the correlation between cosine similarity and actual performance degradation is often weak or moderate, leading to misleading interpretations of a transformer's internal mechanisms. We propose a more robust metric for assessing layer relevance: the actual drop in model accuracy resulting from the removal of a layer. Even though it is a computationally costly metric, this approach offers a more accurate picture of layer importance, allowing for more informed pruning strategies and lightweight models. Our findings have significant implications for the development of interpretable LLMs and highlight the need to move beyond cosine similarity in assessing layer relevance.

</details>


### [122] [Reinforcement Learning for Tool-Calling Agents in Fast Healthcare Interoperability Resources (FHIR)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14126)
*Marius S. Knorr,Robert Müller,Jan P. Bremer,Nils Schweingruber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用强化学习后训练多轮CodeAct智能体，在FHIR医疗数据图上进行结构化问答，将准确率从50%（o4-mini）提升至77%（Qwen3-8B）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FHIR是医疗数据互操作的主流标准，但现有LLM智能体在FHIR图上进行多步推理、过滤和聚合时常选错资源或违反遍历约束，亟需一种能有效提升结构化图上多轮推理能力的方法。

Method: 将FHIR推理建模为可查询结构化图上的序列决策问题，实现多轮CodeAct智能体，并利用自定义环境、工具和LLM Judge提供的执行奖励，通过强化学习进行后训练。

Result: 在FHIR-AgentBench基准上，使用更小更便宜的Qwen3-8B模型，答案正确率从50%（o4-mini）提升至77%。

Conclusion: 端到端后训练流程（环境构建、工具封装、模型训练和自定义评估）能够可靠地提升LLM智能体在结构化临床图上的多轮推理能力，且更小模型通过RL后训练可超越更大闭源模型。

Abstract: Fast Healthcare Interoperability Resources (FHIR) is the dominant standard for interoperable exchange of healthcare data. In FHIR, electronic health records form a directed graph of resources. Answering clinically meaningful questions over FHIR requires agents to perform multi-step reasoning, filtering, and aggregation across multiple resource types. Prior work shows that even tool-augmented LLM agents (retrieval, code execution, multi-turn planning) often select the wrong resources or violate traversal constraints. We study this problem in the context of FHIR-AgentBench, a benchmark for realistic question answering over real-world hospital data, and frame reasoning on FHIR as a sequential decision-making problem over a queryable structured graph. We implement a multi-turn CodeAct agent and post-train it with reinforcement learning using a custom harness and tools. A LLM Judge provides execution-grounded rewards. Compared to prompt-based, closed-model baselines, RL post-training improves performance while enforcing data-integrity constraints. Empirically, our approach improves answer correctness from 50% (o4-mini) to 77% on FHIR-AgentBench using a smaller and cheaper Qwen3-8B model. We present an end-to-end post-training pipeline (environment building, harness construction, model training and custom evaluation) that reliably improves multi-turn reasoning over structured clinical graphs.

</details>


### [123] [bde: A Python Package for Bayesian Deep Ensembles via MILE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14146)
*Vyron Arvanitis,Angelos Aslanidis,Emanuel Sommer,David Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: bde是一个基于JAX实现的Python包，专注于表格数据的贝叶斯深度集成，提供与scikit-learn兼容的估计器，用于快速训练、高效MCMC采样和不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为表格数据的贝叶斯深度集成提供一个用户友好、高效且与scikit-learn兼容的Python工具包，简化采样推断和不确定性量化的实现流程。

Method: 基于JAX实现Microcanonical Langevin Ensembles (MILE)采样推断方法，提供scikit-learn兼容估计器，支持回归和分类任务。

Result: 实现了高效的MCMC采样和不确定性量化，在回归和分类任务中均有应用。

Conclusion: bde为表格数据上的贝叶斯深度集成提供了一种易用且高效的实现方案。

Abstract: bde is a user-friendly Python package for Bayesian Deep Ensembles with a particular focus on tabular data. Built on an efficient JAX implementation of the sampling-based inference method Microcanonical Langevin Ensembles (MILE), it provides scikit-learn compatible estimators for fast training, efficient Markov Chain Monte Carlo sampling, and uncertainty quantification in both regression and classification tasks.

</details>


### [124] [Uncovering Trajectory and Topological Signatures in Multimodal Pediatric Sleep Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14156)
*Scott Ye,Harlin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究儿科睡眠分析中多模态自编码器嵌入的潜在结构，通过引入PHATE几何特征、持续同调拓扑特征和EHR临床特征，使用简单线性/MLP模型进行可解释性分析。结果表明几何、拓扑和临床特征提供互补增益，融合模型在四项二分类任务中AUPRC提升显著（如去饱和0.26→0.34，EEG觉醒0.31→0.48），且校准性能最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成模型在儿科睡眠分析中显示出潜力，但其多模态嵌入的潜在结构仍未被充分理解，本研究旨在揭示其中包含的会话级诊断信息。

Method: 使用多模态掩码自编码器处理30秒儿科PSG时段嵌入，分别提取PHATE几何坐标、持续同调拓扑摘要（全夜运动描述符）和EHR特征，通过简单线性模型和MLP进行可解释性分类，采用晚期融合策略整合多模态特征。

Result: 四项二分类任务上，晚期融合模型AUPRC分别提升：去饱和0.26→0.34，EEG觉醒0.31→0.48，低通气0.09→0.22，呼吸暂停0.05→0.14；全融合模型在所有任务上取得最佳校准（Brier评分和期望校准误差）。

Conclusion: 潜在几何/拓扑特征与EHR提供互补的可解释信号，在极端类别不平衡下改善校准性能和鲁棒性，优于单纯依赖嵌入的方法。

Abstract: While generative models have shown promise in pediatric sleep analysis, the latent structure of their multimodal embeddings remains poorly understood. This work investigates session-wide diagnostic information contained in the sequences of 30-second pediatric PSG epochs embedded by a multimodal masked autoencoder. We test whether augmenting embeddings with PHATE-derived per-epoch coordinates and whole-night movement descriptors, persistent homology summaries of the embedding cloud, and EHR yields task-relevant signals. Simple linear and MLP models, chosen for interpretability rather than state-of-the-art performance, show that geometric, topological, and clinical features each provide complementary gains. For binary predictions, feature importance is task-dependent, and more expressive late-fusion models generally perform better, with AUPRC improving from 0.26 to 0.34 for desaturation, 0.31 to 0.48 for EEG arousal, 0.09 to 0.22 for hypopnea, and 0.05 to 0.14 for apnea. We also report Brier score and Expected Calibration Error, where the full fusion model yields the best calibration across all four binary tasks. Our study reveals that latent geometry/topology and EHR offer complementary, interpretable signals beyond embeddings, improving calibration and robustness under extreme imbalance.

</details>


### [125] [Slot-MPC: Goal-Conditioned Model Predictive Control with Object-Centric Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14937)
*Jonathan Spieler,Angel Villar-Corrales,Sven Behnke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Slot-MPC提出了一种基于对象中心的世界模型框架，结合模型预测控制（MPC）进行行动规划。它利用视觉编码器学习基于槽（slot）的表示来编码场景中的个体对象，并学习以行动为条件的对象中心动力学模型。在推理时，通过可微分的梯度-Based MPC直接优化行动，提升了规划效率。在离线设置下的模拟机器人操作任务中，相比非对象中心的世界模型基线，任务性能和规划效率均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大多数对象中心世界模型和强化学习方法学习的是固定推理策略，限制了在未见情况下的泛化能力。作者希望构建一个能够实现推理时规划的对象中心世界模型框架，使智能体能够适应新场景。

Method: 提出Slot-MPC框架：1) 使用视觉编码器学习基于槽的对象中心表示；2) 基于这些结构化表示学习以行动为条件的对象中心动力学模型；3) 利用可微分世界模型，采用基于梯度的MPC直接优化行动，替代传统无梯度、基于采样的MPC方法。

Result: 在模拟机器人操作任务中，Slot-MPC在任务性能和规划效率上均优于非对象中心的世界模型基线。在离线设置且状态-行动覆盖有限的条件下，基于梯度的MPC相比无梯度、基于采样的MPC表现更好。

Conclusion: 显式结构化的对象中心表示为可控且可泛化的决策提供了强归纳偏置，验证了对象中心表示在规划中的有效性。

Abstract: Predictive world models enable agents to model scene dynamics and reason about the consequences of their actions. Inspired by human perception, object-centric world models capture scene dynamics using object-level representations, which can be used for downstream applications such as action planning. However, most object-centric world models and reinforcement learning (RL) approaches learn reactive policies that are fixed at inference time, limiting generalization to novel situations. We propose Slot-MPC, an object-centric world modeling framework that enables planning through Model Predictive Control (MPC). Slot-MPC leverages vision encoders to learn slot-based representations, which encode individual objects in the scene, and uses these structured representations to learn an action-conditioned object-centric dynamics model. At inference time, the learned dynamics model enables action planning via MPC, allowing agents to adapt to previously unseen situations. Since the learned world model is differentiable, we can use gradient-based MPC to directly optimize actions, which is computationally more efficient than relying on gradient-free, sampling-based MPC methods. Experiments on simulated robotic manipulation tasks show that Slot-MPC improves both task performance and planning efficiency compared to non-object-centric world model baselines. In the considered offline setting with limited state-action coverage, we find that gradient-based MPC performs better than gradient-free, sampling-based MPC. Our results demonstrate that explicitly structured, object-centric representations provide a strong inductive bias for controllable and generalizable decision-making. Code and additional results are available at https://slot-mpc.github.io.

</details>


### [126] [Finite Sample Bounds for Learning with Score Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14168)
*Devin Smedira,Abhijith Jayakumar,Sidhant Misra,Marc Vuffray,Andrey Y. Lokhov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为非多项式指数族分布的得分匹配学习提供了非渐进的样本复杂度分析，证明了样本复杂度与模型维度呈多项式依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 得分匹配在高维连续指数族分布学习中计算效率高，但其统计特性的理论基础尚不完善，现有研究仅给出渐进样本复杂度界。

Method: 对多项式指数族分布的得分匹配方法进行非渐近样本复杂度分析，推导出与模型维度呈多项式关系的样本界。

Result: 推导出的样本界与模型维度呈多项式依赖关系，这是首个非渐近的样本复杂度结果。

Conclusion: 填补了得分匹配在指数族分布学习中的理论空白，为非渐近分析提供了首个有效结果。

Abstract: Learning of continuous exponential family distributions with unbounded support remains an important area of research for both theory and applications in high-dimensional statistics. In recent years, score matching has become a widely used method for learning exponential families with continuous variables due to its computational ease when compared against maximum likelihood estimation. However, theoretical understanding of the statistical properties of score matching is still lacking. In this work, we provide a non-asymptotic sample complexity analysis for learning the structure of exponential families of polynomials with score matching. The derived sample bounds show a polynomial dependence on the model dimension. These bounds are the first of its kind, as all prior work has shown only asymptotic bounds on the sample complexity.

</details>


### [127] [LLMs Know When They Know, but Do Not Act on It: A Metacognitive Harness for Test-time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14186)
*Qi Cao,Yufan Wang,Peijia Qin,Shuhao Zhang,Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受认知心理学启发的元认知控制框架，将LLM的自我监测信号（求解前的感知知晓FOK和求解后的判断学习JOL）转化为显式的推理控制接口，在不更新参数的情况下显著提升模型在文本、代码和多模态推理基准上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽然具备自我监测能力（如预估成功率、判断答案正确性），但这些信号通常仅在孤立场景下被测量或激发，未被有效用于控制推理过程。作者希望探索能否将这些潜在的元认知能力转化为有效的测试时控制机制，从而提升模型推理表现。

Method: 基于Nelson-Narens认知心理学理论，提出元认知控制框架：1）求解前收集感知知晓（FOK）信号；2）求解后收集判断学习（JOL）信号；3）将这些信号转化为显式控制接口，决定何时信任当前解、何时以紧凑元认知反馈重试、何时将多次尝试传递给最终聚合器。

Result: 在文本、代码和多模态推理基准上，该方法在不更新参数或基准特定微调的情况下，将固定Claude Sonnet-4.6基模型的池化准确率从48.3提升至56.9，并在HLE-Verified、LiveCodeBench v6和R-Bench-V三个主要评估设置上超过最强排行榜条目。

Conclusion: 强LLM可能已经具备有用的元认知能力，但需要显式的控制框架才能在推理过程中发挥这种能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often expose useful signals of self-monitoring: before solving a problem, they can estimate whether they are likely to succeed, and after solving it, they can judge whether their answer is likely to be correct. However, these signals are typically measured or elicited in isolation, rather than used to control inference. In this work, we ask whether LLMs possess latent metacognitive ability that can be turned into effective test-time control. Inspired by the Nelson--Narens theory from cognitive psychology, we propose a metacognitive harness that separates monitoring from reasoning.
