Method: 提出SeesawNet统一架构，核心是自适应平稳-非平稳注意力(ASNA)，从归一化序列捕获通用依赖，从原始序列捕获特定依赖，并根据实例级非平稳性自适应融合两者。在此基础上，SeesawNet交替建模时间和通道维度的依赖关系，联合捕获长程依赖和跨变量依赖。

Result: 在多个真实世界基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SeesawNet一致性地优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: SeesawNet通过自适应平衡通用与实例特定依赖建模，有效解决了实例归一化在非平稳时间序列预测中的过度平滑问题，是一种更优的预测架构。

Abstract: Instance normalization (IN) is widely used in non-stationary multivariate time series forecasting to reduce distribution shifts and highlight common patterns across samples. However, IN can over-smooth instance-specific structural information that is essential for modeling temporal and cross-channel heterogeneity. While prior methods further suppress distribution discrepancies or attempt to recover temporal specific dependencies, they often ignore a central tension: how to adaptively model common and instance-specific dependency based on each instance's non-stationary structures. To address this dilemma, we propose SeesawNet, a unified architecture that dynamically balances common and instance-specific dependency modeling in both temporal and channel dimensions. At its core is Adaptive Stationary-Nonstationary Attention (ASNA), which captures common dependencies from normalized sequences and specific dependencies from raw sequences, and adaptively fuses them according to instance-level non-stationarity. Built upon ASNA, SeesawNet alternates dedicated temporal and channel relationship modeling to jointly capture long-range and cross-variable dependencies. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world benchmarks demonstrate that SeesawNet consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [167] [Efficient Multi-objective Prompt Optimization via Pure-exploration Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14553)
*Donghao Li,Chengshuai Shi,Weijuan Ou,Cong Shen,Jing Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将提示选择问题建模为多目标纯探索博弈，提出了帕累托提示集恢复和最佳可行提示识别两种设置，并采用多目标博弈算法及新设计的结构化博弈最佳可行臂识别算法进行求解，实验证明该方法优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示选择方法大多使用单一指标评估提示性能，忽略了提示性能内在的多面性（多目标特性），作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将问题建模为多目标纯探索博弈框架，针对两种实际场景（帕累托提示集恢复和最佳可行提示识别）分别适配了多目标博弈中已被证明高效的算法，并为结构化博弈场景设计了新的最佳可行臂识别算法，在线性情况下给出了识别误差的理论保证。

Result: 在多个大型语言模型上的广泛实验表明，基于博弈的方法显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 建立了一个原则性且高效的多目标提示优化框架，为提示选择问题提供了理论保证和实用算法。

Abstract: Prompt engineering has become central to eliciting the capabilities of large language models (LLMs). At its core lies prompt selection -- efficiently identifying the most effective prompts. However, most prior investigations overlook a key challenge: the inherently multi-faceted nature of prompt performance, which cannot be captured by a single metric. To fill this gap, we study the multi-objective prompt selection problem under two practical settings: Pareto prompt set recovery and best feasible prompt identification. Casting the problem into the pure-exploration bandits framework, we adapt provably efficient algorithms from multi-objective bandits and further introduce a novel design for best feasible arm identification in structured bandits, with theoretical guarantees on the identification error in the linear case. Extensive experiments across multiple LLMs show that the bandit-based approaches yield significant improvements over baselines, establishing a principled and efficient framework for multi-objective prompt optimization.

</details>


### [168] [Resolving Action Bottleneck: Agentic Reinforcement Learning Informed by Token-Level Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14558)
*Langzhou He,Junyou Zhu,Yue Zhou,Zhengyao Gu,Junhua Liu,Wei-Chieh Huang,Henry Peng Zou,David Wipf,Philip S. Yu,Qitian Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现强化学习训练大语言模型时，对所有token进行均匀信用分配会导致训练信号错配——动作token（仅占轨迹一小部分）承载了绝大部分与奖励方差相关的信号（即Action Bottleneck现象）。为此提出ActFocus方法，通过降低推理token梯度权重并增加高不确定性动作token权重，在不增加运行时和内存开销的情况下，在四个环境中相比PPO和GRPO获得最高65.2和63.7个百分点的最终收益提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略梯度方法（如PPO、GRPO）在训练大语言模型时对轨迹中每个token分配相同的训练信号，但作者发现这种均匀信用分配会导致训练信号严重错配，大量信号集中在占轨迹极小比例的动作token上。因此需要解决这种token层面训练信号分配不均的问题。

Method: 从基于能量的建模视角出发，量化不同token的训练信号（与不同rollout间奖励方差的关联度），发现信号集中在动作token上（Action Bottleneck）。据此提出ActFocus方法：1）一种简单的token重加权方法，降低推理token的梯度权重；2）基于能量的再分配机制，对不确定性更高的动作token进一步增加权重。

Result: 在四个环境下，ActFocus一致性地优于PPO和GRPO基线，最终收益提升分别高达65.2和63.7个百分点，且不增加任何运行时或内存开销。

Conclusion: 识别出Action Bottleneck现象并提出ActFocus方法可有效解决训练信号分配不均问题，显著提升智能体强化学习中大语言模型的训练效果。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning trains large language models using multi-turn trajectories that interleave long reasoning traces with short environment-facing actions. Common policy-gradient methods, such as PPO and GRPO, treat each token in a trajectory equally, leading to uniform credit assignment. In this paper, we critically demonstrate that such uniform credit assignment largely misallocates token-level training signals. From an energy-based modeling perspective, we show that token-level training signals, quantified by their correlations with reward variance of different rollouts sampled from a given prompt, concentrate sharply on action tokens rather than reasoning tokens, even though action tokens account for only a small fraction of the trajectory. We refer to this phenomenon as the Action Bottleneck. Motivated by this observation, we propose an embarrassingly simple token reweighting approach, ActFocus, that downweights gradients on reasoning tokens, along with an additional energy-based redistribution mechanism that further increases the weights on action tokens with higher uncertainty. Across four environments and different model sizes, ActFocus consistently outperforms PPO and GRPO, yielding final-step gains of up to 65.2 and 63.7 percentage points, respectively, without any additional runtime or memory cost.

</details>


### [169] [Woodelf++: A Fast and Unified Partial Dependence Plot Algorithm for Decision Tree Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14578)
*Ron Wettenstein,Alexander Nadel,Udi Boker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Woodelf++，一个统一高效的决策树集成可解释性工具计算框架，能够计算PDP、Joint-PDP、Any-Order-PDIVs及Full PDPs，在速度上相比现有技术实现指数级提升，Any-Order-PDIVs计算从百万年量级降至5分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PDP、Joint-PDP和PDIVs等可解释性工具在决策树集成上计算效率低下，尤其是Any-Order-PDIVs任务的计算复杂度极高，亟需一种统一且高效的计算方法。

Method: 基于Woodelf的SHAP高效计算框架，通过推导伪布尔函数上的适当度量，统一实现了PDP（精确与近似）、Joint-PDP、Any-Order-PDIVs的计算；同时引入Full PDPs，利用模型的分裂阈值捕获其在所有可能特征值上的行为。

Result: 在40万行数据集上，PDP和Joint-PDP比现有技术快6倍，比scikit-learn快五个数量级；Any-Order-PDIVs可在5分钟内计算完成，而现有技术估计需要超过100万年。

Conclusion: Woodelf++在纯Python实现且支持GPU加速的条件下，为决策树集成的多种可解释性工具提供了统一、高效的计算方案，显著降低了计算复杂度，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Partial Dependence Plots (PDPs) visualize how changes in a single feature affect the average model prediction. They are widely used in practice to interpret decision tree ensembles and other machine learning models. Joint-PDPs extend this idea to pairs of features, revealing their combined effect. Partial Dependence Interaction Values (PDIVs) measure feature interactions. The Any-Order-PDIVs task computes these interactions for every feature subset across all rows of the dataset.
  We introduce Woodelf++, a unified and efficient approach for computing all these useful explainability tools on decision tree ensembles, building on Woodelf, an algorithm for efficient SHAP computation. By deriving suitable metrics over pseudo-Boolean functions, Woodelf++ can compute PDPs (exact and approximate), Joint-PDPs, and Any-Order-PDIVs in a unified framework. Our method delivers substantial complexity improvements over the state of the art, including an exponential gain for Any-Order-PDIVs. Additionally, we introduce and efficiently compute Full PDPs, which leverage the model's split thresholds to faithfully capture its behavior across all possible feature values.
  Woodelf++ is implemented in pure Python and supports GPU acceleration. On a dataset with 400,000 rows, Woodelf++ computes PDP and Joint-PDP up to 6x faster than the state of the art and up to five orders of magnitude faster than scikit-learn. For Any-Order-PDIVs, the gap is even larger: Woodelf++ computes all interaction values in 5 minutes, while the state of the art is estimated to require over 1,000,000 years.

</details>


### [170] [Silent Collapse in Recursive Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14588)
*Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现递归学习（模型用自身先前版本生成的数据训练）中存在一种称为"静默崩溃"的现象——模型内部分布（预测熵、表征多样性、尾部覆盖）逐渐收缩，而传统性能指标（损失、困惑度、准确率）仍看似稳定或改善。该现象有三个可检测的前兆信号：锚点熵收缩、表征漂移冻结、尾部覆盖侵蚀。据此提出MTR框架（监测-信任-调节器），通过轻量级元认知循环实现早期预警并主动预防静默崩溃，无需访问原始数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有性能指标（损失、困惑度、准确率）在递归学习场景下无法及时检测模型内部退化，往往在退化不可逆后才被发现。需要能够早期预警模型内部分布收缩的机制。

Method: 识别静默崩溃的三个轨迹级前兆信号（锚点熵收缩、表征漂移冻结、尾部覆盖侵蚀），并提出MTR框架：监测轨迹统计量→估计慢时间尺度信任变量→自适应调节有效学习强度。

Result: 静默崩溃并非突然发生，其前兆信号在标准验证指标退化之前的多个世代就已经显现，可实现早期预警。MTR无需原始真实数据即可主动预防静默崩溃。

Conclusion: MTR框架能在原始数据不可用、被污染或涉及隐私的情况下，有效监测和预防递归学习中的静默崩溃现象。

Abstract: Recursive learning -- where models are trained on data generated by previous versions of themselves -- is increasingly common in large language models, autonomous agents, and self-supervised systems. However, standard performance metrics (loss, perplexity, accuracy) often fail to detect internal degradation before it becomes irreversible. Here we identify a phenomenon we call silent collapse: under broad recursive conditions, model internal distributions -- predictive entropy, representational diversity, and tail coverage -- progressively contract even as conventional metrics appear stable or improving.
  We discover that silent collapse is not abrupt. Its onset is reliably preceded by three trajectory-level precursors: (1) contraction of anchor entropy, (2) freezing of representation drift, and (3) erosion of tail coverage. These signals manifest multiple generations before any degradation in standard validation metrics, enabling early warning.
  Based on these precursors, we propose the MTR (Monitor--Trust--Regulator) framework, a lightweight metacognitive loop that monitors trajectory statistics, estimates a slow-timescale trust variable, and adaptively modulates the effective learning intensity. MTR provides early warning and actively prevents silent collapse without requiring access to pristine real data -- a critical advantage when original data is unavailable, contaminated, or private.

</details>


### [171] [Fast Rates for Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14599)
*Andreas Schlaginhaufen,Maryam Kamgarpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了熵正则化最小-最大逆强化学习（Min-Max-IRL）在线性奖励函数类、有限时域、Borel状态-动作空间的MDP中的结构性和统计性结果。结构上，证明了MLE与Min-Max-IRL在总体水平等价，且在确定性动力学下经验水平等价。统计上，利用伪自洽性，证明了轨迹级KL散度和Hessian范数下的参数误差以快速率O(n^{-1})衰减，适用于模型误设定且无需探索假设。此外，将奖励可识别性结果推广到一般Borel空间，并推导了软最优值函数对奖励参数的导数新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆强化学习方法在理论保证上存在不足，尤其是在奖励函数为线性类、状态-动作空间为一般Borel空间的情况下，缺乏对熵正则化Min-Max-IRL的结构等价性和统计收敛速率的深入理解。

Method: 通过理论分析建立MLE与Min-Max-IRL的等价性（总体和确定性动力学下经验水平）；利用伪自洽性（pseudo-self-concordance）推导Min-Max-IRL损失的统计收敛速率；在一般Borel空间下扩展奖励可识别性结果；分析软最优值函数对奖励参数的导数。

Result: 轨迹级KL散度和Hessian范数下的参数误差以O(n^{-1})速率衰减；奖励可识别性结果适用于一般Borel空间；导数分析为后续优化提供基础。

Conclusion: 熵正则化Min-Max-IRL在理论层面具有稳健的统计性质，快收敛速率无需探索假设且允许模型误设定，为大规模连续空间中的逆强化学习提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We establish novel structural and statistical results for entropy-regularized min-max inverse reinforcement learning (Min-Max-IRL) with linear reward classes in finite-horizon MDPs with Borel state and action spaces. On the structural side, we show that maximum likelihood estimation (MLE) and Min-Max-IRL are equivalent at the population level, and at the empirical level under deterministic dynamics. On the statistical side, exploiting pseudo-self-concordance of the Min-Max-IRL loss, we prove that both the trajectory-level KL divergence and the squared parameter error in the Hessian norm decay at the fast rate $\mathcal{O}(n^{-1})$, where $n$ is the number of expert trajectories. Our guarantees apply under misspecification and require no exploration assumptions. We further extend reward-identifiability results to general Borel spaces and derive novel results on the derivatives of the soft-optimal value function with respect to reward parameters.

</details>


### [172] [Unbiased and Second-Order-Free Training for High-Dimensional PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14643)
*Jaemin Seo,Surin Lee,Jae Yong Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了基于欧拉-丸山时间离散化的BSDE方法在求解高维PDE时存在的偏差问题，并提出了一种无偏、免二阶导数的训练框架，在保留计算优势的同时消除偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的BSDE方法使用Euler-Maruyama时间离散化会导致训练损失存在固有偏差，而高阶格式（如Heun）虽能消除偏差但会重新引入二阶空间导数并带来巨大计算开销。因此，需要一种既能消除偏差又保持计算效率的方法。

Method: 对EM时间离散化导致的损失偏差进行原理性分析，在此基础上提出一种无偏且免二阶导数的训练框架，该框架保留BSDE方法的计算优势。

Result: 提出了Un-EM-BSDE无偏训练框架，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 成功解决了BSDE方法中EM离散化带来的偏差问题，在不引入二阶导数计算的前提下实现了无偏训练，保持了BSDE方法在求解高维PDE时的计算优势。

Abstract: Deep learning methods based on backward stochastic differential equations (BSDEs) have emerged as competitive alternatives to physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) for solving high-dimensional partial differential equations (PDEs). By leveraging probabilistic representations, BSDE approaches can avoid the curse of dimensionality and often admit second-order-free training objectives that do not require explicit Hessian evaluations. It has recently been established that the commonly used Euler-Maruyama (EM) time discretization induces an intrinsic bias in BSDE training losses. While high-order schemes such as Heun can fully eliminate this bias, such schemes re-introduce second-order spatial derivatives and incur substantial computational overhead. In this work, we provide a principled analysis of EM-induced loss bias and propose an unbiased, second-order-free training framework that preserves the computational advantages of BSDE methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/seojaemin22/Un-EM-BSDE.

</details>


### [173] [Slower Generalization, Faster Memorization: A Sweet Spot in Algorithmic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14659)
*Shin So,Kyelim Lee,Albert No*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在结构化输出任务（Needleman-Wunsch矩阵生成）中，小Transformer的验证准确率收敛速度在中等数据集规模时最快，而非最大数据集；存在一个数据集大小的"甜蜜点"，超出后泛化虽可实现但需更多梯度更新。这分离了泛化起始所需的关键数据量与优化收敛速度的数据量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验"临界数据量理论"的自然直觉——即训练数据一旦足够识别潜在规则，更多数据应加速验证收敛——是否在结构化输出任务中成立。

Method: 在Needleman-Wunsch矩阵生成这一受控结构化输出任务上训练小型Transformer，比较不同数据集大小下验证准确率收敛所需梯度更新次数，并以乘法任务作为基线对照。

Result: 中等数据集规模下验证准确率收敛最快；更大数据集反而需要更多梯度更新才能达到高验证准确率；在部分验证能力首次出现的阶段，更大数据集可加速训练准确率提升；乘法基线未呈现类似的后阈值减速现象。

Conclusion: 泛化起始所需的关键数据量与优化收敛速度的最优数据量是分离的；在结构化输出任务中，学习规则与完成精确拟合可能发散。

Abstract: Critical-data-size accounts of grokking suggest a natural post-threshold intuition: once training data is sufficient to identify the underlying rule, additional data should accelerate validation convergence. We show that this intuition can fail in a controlled structured-output task. In Needleman--Wunsch (NW) matrix generation, small Transformers reach high validation exact-match accuracy fastest at an intermediate dataset size, not at the largest one. Past this dataset-size sweet spot, generalization remains achievable but requires more gradient updates. Conversely, in the regime where partial validation competence first appears, larger datasets can require fewer updates to reach high training accuracy, suggesting that emerging rule structure can accelerate fitting beyond example-wise memorization. A multiplication baseline does not show the same post-threshold slowdown. These results separate the critical data size for the onset of generalization from the dataset size that optimizes update-based convergence, and identify structured-output tasks where learning the rule and completing exact-fitting can diverge.

</details>


### [174] [AQKA: Active Quantum Kernel Acquisition Under a Shot Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14672)
*Jian Xu,Chao Li,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对量子核学习中测量预算分配问题，提出了自适应量子核分配(AQKA)方法，通过非均匀分配测量次数(根据核条目对分类器的边际贡献)显著提升性能；建立了完整的预算区间分解理论，推导了闭式配对级采集公式，并在IBM量子硬件上实现了首个多种子在线自适应分配实验，最高提升32个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子核学习方法在有限测量预算下对所有核条目均匀分配测量次数，忽略了不同核条目对下游分类器的差异化重要性，导致预算利用率低下，成为部署于近端硬件的瓶颈。

Method: 1) 提出AQKA方法，基于闭式公式 $s_{ij}^{\star} \propto |g_{ij}|\sqrt{K_{ij}(1-K_{ij})}$ 非均匀分配测量次数，其中梯度 $g_{ij}$ 对KRR为 $|β_iα_j+β_jα_i|$，对SVM为 $|η_i^*η_j^*|$；2) 建立完整的预算区间分解理论，明确各方法(AQKA、Nyström-QKE、ShoFaR)的最优区域；3) 推导了修正的稀疏感知Cauchy-Schwarz率 $\rho \le 2m/N$ 和显式常数遗憾界(定理2)；4) 在IBM量子硬件上执行多种子在线自适应分配实验。

Result: AQKA在预算受限区间($B \lesssim 16 n_{\mathrm{pairs}}$)内主导稀疏敏感KRR，随N从225增至1000，领先均匀分配从+8增至+25个百分点；在156-qubit Heron硬件核上达到+26~32个百分点领先。在ibm_aachen上N=20时达+17.0±4.8 pts(3.5σ)，ibm_berlin上N=30时达+14.0±8.5 pts。

Conclusion: 非均匀测量分配在量子核学习中具有显著优势，不同预算区间存在明确的最优策略；所提出的AQKA方法及理论框架为有限预算下的量子核学习提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Estimating an $N \times N$ quantum kernel from circuit fidelities requires $Θ(N^2 S)$ measurement shots, the dominant bottleneck for deployment on near-term hardware. Existing budget-saving methods (Nyström-QKE, ShoFaR, kernel-target alignment) sub-sample \emph{which} entries to measure but allocate shots \emph{uniformly} within their chosen subset, ignoring how much each entry drives the downstream classifier. We close this gap with two contributions. \textbf{First, a complete regime decomposition} for shot-budgeted quantum kernel learning: a principled menu of when each allocator wins. Our method, \emph{AQKA}, dominates the budget-limited regime ($B \lesssim 16 n_{\mathrm{pairs}}$) on sparse-sensitivity KRR, with the gap \emph{growing} from $+8$ to $+25$ pts over uniform as $N$ scales $225{\to}1000$ and reaching $+26$--$32$ pts on an \texttt{ibm\_pittsburgh} (156-qubit Heron) hardware kernel; Nyström-QKE wins at saturating budgets on planted-sparse via low-rank reconstruction; ShoFaR is competitive only at extreme low budgets. \textbf{Second, a closed-form pair-level acquisition theory}: $s_{ij}^{\star} \propto |g_{ij}|\sqrt{K_{ij}(1-K_{ij})}$ with explicit gradient $g_{ij}$ for KRR (Lemma~1, $|β_iα_j+β_jα_i|\sqrt{K_{ij}(1-K_{ij})}$) and SVM via the envelope theorem ($|η_i^*η_j^*|\sqrt{K_{ij}(1-K_{ij})}$); a \emph{corrected} sparsity-aware Cauchy--Schwarz rate $ρ\le 2m/N$ matching empirics (vs.\ the naive $m^2/N^2$); an explicit-constant plug-in regret bound (Theorem~2); and a tighter SVM ceiling $ρ^{\mathrm{SVM}} \le m_{\mathrm{sv}}^2/N^2$. We close with the first multi-seed live online adaptive shot allocation on quantum hardware: $+17.0 \pm 4.8$ pts at $N{=}20$ on \texttt{ibm\_aachen} ($3.5σ$, 5 seeds), with the advantage holding at $N{=}30$ at higher budget on \texttt{ibm\_berlin} ($+14.0 \pm 8.5$ pts, 5 seeds).

</details>


### [175] [NeuroAtlas: Benchmarking Foundation Models for Clinical EEG and Brain-Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14698)
*Konstantinos Kontras,Trui Osselaer,Stylianos G. Mouslech,Angeliki-Ilektra Karaiskou,Guido Gagliardi,Thomas Strypsteen,Mohammad Hossein Badiei,Anku Rani,Maarten Vanmarcke,Miguel Bhagubai,Chanakya Ekbote,Jaedong Hwang,Christos Chatzichristos,Paul Pu Liang,Maarten De Vos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroAtlas是迄今最大的EEG基准测试（42个数据集，26万小时），系统评估了EEG基础模型。主要发现：EEG专用FM并不优于通用时间序列FM；标准ML指标不足以评估临床效用；模型排名和性能在领域内差异显著。结论是目前尚无统一的EEG基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型的效果尚不明确，已有评估在数据集、预处理和指标上不一致，常掩盖临床相关性。需要统一的基准测试来系统评估EEG-FM的真实表现。

Method: 构建NeuroAtlas基准，包含42个数据集和26万小时数据，覆盖临床EEG（癫痫、睡眠医学、脑龄估计）和脑机接口。对比EEG专用FM、通用时间序列FM和监督基线，并引入临床相关评估指标。

Result: 1) EEG专用FM并不一致优于通用时间序列FM；2) 标准ML指标不足以评估临床效用，需使用事件级决策质量、睡眠图特征、脑龄差等指标；3) 模型排名和性能在领域内差异显著。

Conclusion: 预训练模型表现大致相当，仅少数有微弱优势，当前模型尚未实现统一的EEG基础模型。NeuroAtlas揭示了这一差距，并为下一代统一EEG-FM提供了数据集和指标。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) promise to extract unified representations that generalize across downstream tasks. They have emerged across fields, including electroencephalography (EEG), but it is less clear how effective they are in this particular field. Published evaluations differ in datasets, in the EEG-specific preprocessing that might influence reported results, and in the reported metrics, frequently obscuring the clinical relevance in EEG. We introduce NeuroAtlas, the largest EEG benchmark to date: 42 datasets and 260k hours covering clinical EEG (epilepsy, sleep medicine, brain age estimation) and brain-computer interfaces, and include multiple datasets per task along with bespoke clinical evaluation metrics. Besides evaluating EEG-FMs with respect to supervised baselines, we present results from generic time-series FMs. We report three findings. First, EEG-specific FMs do not consistently outperform time-series FMs, which have neither EEG-focused architectures nor been pretrained on EEG. Second, standard machine learning metrics are insufficient to assess clinical utility: thus, we thoroughly evaluate more appropriate measures such as the quality of event-level decision-making, hypnogram-derived features, and the brain-age gap in the domains of epilepsy, sleep, and brain age, respectively. Third, model rankings and performance can vary substantially within domains. We conclude that pretrained models perform largely on par, with only narrow advantages for a few, and that current models do not yet deliver on the promise of an out-of-the-box unified EEG model. NeuroAtlas exposes this gap and provides the datasets and metrics for the next generation of unified EEG FMs.

</details>


### [176] [TAPIOCA: Why Task- Aware Pruning Improves OOD model Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14738)
*Krish Sharma,Omar Naim,Soumadeep Saha,Nicholas Asher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了任务感知层剪枝（如TALE）的效果，发现该方法对分布内（ID）数据无益，但能持续提升分布外（OOD）数据的准确性。通过实验揭示了OOD输入会扭曲模型的任务适应几何结构，而剪枝通过移除产生或放大这种扭曲的层，将OOD表示重新对齐到任务适应几何，从而改善性能。作者通过控制分布偏移和残差缩放干预提供了因果证据，并在不同规模模型上验证了一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究任务感知层剪枝（如TALE方法）在何种条件下能提升模型性能，以及其背后的机理。先前工作已表明该方法有效，但对其有效条件和原因缺乏系统理解。

Method: 在控制的多项式回归任务和大语言模型上进行实验，对比分布内（ID）与分布外（OOD）数据下的剪枝效果；分析OOD输入引起的层范数和成对距离分布偏差；提出任务适应几何的几何解释框架，并通过分布偏移控制和残差缩放干预实验提供因果证据。

Result: 任务感知层剪枝对分布内数据无提升，但一致性地提高了分布外数据的准确性。OOD数据会产生与ID数据不同的逐层范数和成对距离分布，剪枝通过移除扭曲层的表示，使OOD表示向任务适应几何对齐，从而改善性能。

Conclusion: 任务感知层剪枝的益处源于将OOD表示重新对齐到模型的任务适应几何结构，而非提升分布内性能；该机制在不同模型规模上表现一致。

Abstract: Recent work has promoted task-aware layer pruning as a way to improve model performance on particular tasks, as shown by TALE. In this paper, we investigate when such improvements occur and why. We show first that, across controlled polynomial regression tasks and large language models, such pruning yields no benefit on in-distribution (ID) data but consistently improves out-of-distribution (OOD) accuracy. We further show empirically that OOD inputs induce layerwise norm and pairwise-distance profiles that deviate from the corresponding ID profiles. This leads to a geometric explanation of task-aware pruning: each task induces a task-adapted geometry, characterized empirically by the representation profiles observed on ID inputs. OOD inputs can introduce a distorted version of the task-adapted geometry. Task-aware pruning identifies layers that create or amplify this distortion; by removing them, it shifts OOD representational norms and pairwise distances toward those observed on the adapted distribution. This realigns OOD inputs with the model's task-adapted geometry and improves performance. We provide causal evidence through controlled distribution shifts and residual-scaling interventions, and demonstrate consistent behavior across model scales.

</details>


### [177] [Selective Safety Steering via Value-Filtered Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14746)
*Bat-Sheva Einbinder,Hen Davidov,Yee Whye Teh,Yarin Gal,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于价值过滤的解码方法（Value-Filtered Decoding），在测试时通过安全奖励引导生成，并显式控制不必要的干预概率，从而在提升安全性的同时保持基础模型的有用性和流畅性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的解码时安全引导方法（如安全奖励调整）会不必要地干预本已安全的生成，损害模型的有用性、流畅性和一致性。作者希望减少这些不必要的干预，同时提升对不安全响应的安全性。

Method: 提出一种测试时安全引导方法：使用基于值的（value-based）安全标准对每个token进行过滤，并给出错误干预概率的显式上界。通过一个阈值超参数控制该界，实现安全性与不必要干预之间的权衡。

Result: 在多个数据集和实验中，该方法在安全性、有用性和与基础模型相似性之间的权衡上优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 价值过滤解码是一种更高效的测试时安全对齐方法，能在不牺牲基础模型核心能力的前提下显著提升安全性，并提供了可调节的干预控制机制。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) are trained to align with human values, their generations may still violate safety constraints. A growing line of work addresses this problem by modifying the model's sampling policy at decoding time using a safety reward. However, existing decoding-time steering methods often intervene unnecessarily, modifying generations that would have been safe under the base model. Such unnecessary interventions are undesirable, as they can distort key properties of the base model such as helpfulness, fluency, style, and coherence. We propose a new test-time steering method designed to reduce such unnecessary interventions while improving the safety of unsafe responses. Our approach filters tokens using a value-based safety criterion and provides an explicit bound on the probability of false interventions. A single threshold hyperparameter controls this bound, allowing practitioners to trade off higher rates of unnecessary intervention for better output safety. Across multiple datasets and experiments, we show that our value-filtered decoding method outperforms existing baselines, achieving better trade-offs between safety, helpfulness, and similarity to the base model.

</details>


### [178] [Cognitive-Uncertainty Guided Knowledge Distillation for Accurate Classification of Student Misconceptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14752)
*Qirui Liu,Hao Chen,Weijie Shi,Jiajie Xu,Jia Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段知识蒸馏框架，通过认知不确定性驱动的双层边际选择机制，仅用10.30%的筛选数据即可在误概念识别任务上显著超越大模型和标准微调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生误概念识别面临数据稀缺（长尾分布）、标注噪声高（类别边界模糊）以及部署困境（大模型有预训练偏见且无法边缘部署，小模型过拟合噪声）三大挑战。

Method: 两阶段知识蒸馏框架：第一阶段进行标准蒸馏迁移任务能力；第二阶段引入基于认知不确定性的双层边际选择机制，根据教师模型不确定性和置信度差异识别四类关键样本，并设计难度自适应机制平衡不同数据子集的硬/软标签贡献。

Result: 仅用10.30%筛选样本在MAP-Charting数据集上MAP@3达0.9585（+17.8%）；4B模型在中学代数误概念基准跨主题测试中达84.38%准确率，显著优于SOTA LLM（67.73%）和标准微调72B模型（81.25%）。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过挖掘现有数据中的高价值样本而非大规模数据合成，有效解决了误概念识别的数据稀缺和部署矛盾，验证了认知不确定性指导样本筛选的优越性。

Abstract: Accurately identifying student misconceptions is crucial for personalized education but faces three challenges: (1) data scarcity with long-tail distribution, where authentic student reasoning is difficult to synthesize; (2) fuzzy boundaries between error categories with high annotation noise; (3) deployment parado-large models overlook unconventional approaches due to pretraining bias and cannot be deployed on edge, while small models overfit to noise. Unlike traditional methods that increase diversity through large-scale data synthesis, we propose a two-stage knowledge distillation framework that mines high-value samples from existing data. The first stage performs standard distillation to transfer task capabilities. The second stage introduces a dual-layer marginal selection mechanism based on cognitive uncertainty, identifying four types of critical samples based on teacher model uncertainty and confidence differences. For different data subsets, we design difficulty-adaptive mechanism to balance hard/soft label contributions, enabling student models to inherit inter-class relationships from teacher soft labels while distinguishing ambiguous error types. Experiments show that with augmented training on only 10.30% of filtered samples, we achieve MAP@3 of 0.9585 (+17.8%) on the MAP-Charting dataset, and using only a 4B parameter model, we attain 84.38% accuracy on cross-topic tests of middle school algebra misconception benchmarks, significantly outperforming sota LLM (67.73%) and standard fine-tuned 72B models (81.25%). Our code is available at https://github.com/RoschildRui/acl2026_map.

</details>


### [179] [Crys-JEPA: Accelerating Crystal Discovery via Embedding Screening and Generative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14759)
*Nian Liu,Nikita Kazeev,Stephen Gregory Dale,Artem Maevskiy,Yuwei Zeng,Ryoji Kubo,Pengru Huang,Thomas Laurent,Yann LeCun,Kostya S. Novoselov,Xavier Bresson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现现有晶体生成模型存在稳定性-新颖性权衡问题，提出Crys-JEPA架构（联合嵌入预测架构）通过能量感知潜在空间保留形成能差异，并构建筛选-精炼流程，在MP-20和Alex-MP-20数据集上V.S.U.N指标分别提升81.4%和82.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有晶体生成模型以最大化观测晶体似然为目标，导致生成的晶体在稳定性和新颖性之间存在严重权衡：靠近已知分布则新颖性不足，远离则稳定性迅速下降，难以同时满足材料发现所需的稳定且新颖的要求。

Method: 提出Crys-JEPA（联合嵌入预测架构），学习能量感知潜在空间以保留形成能差异，将稳定性评估转化为基于嵌入的比较；在此基础上构建筛选-精炼流程，识别有潜力的生成晶体并重新引入以精炼生成模型。

Result: 在MP-20和Alex-MP-20数据集上，V.S.U.N指标相比基线分别提升81.4%和82.6%。

Conclusion: Crys-JEPA通过能量感知的嵌入空间和筛选-精炼机制有效缓解了稳定性-新颖性权衡，显著提升了稳定且新颖晶体的生成质量。

Abstract: De novo crystal generation seeks to discover materials that are not merely realistic, but also stable and novel. However, most existing generative models are trained to maximize the likelihood of observed crystals, which encourages samples to stay close to known materials yet not necessarily align with the criteria that matter in discovery. Through an empirical investigation, we show that current crystal generative models are caught in a pronounced stability--novelty trade-off: moving toward the observed distribution preserves stability but limits novelty, whereas moving away from it quickly destroys stability. This suggests that the useful region for discovering crystals that are both stable and novel is extremely narrow. To escape the trade-off, we introduce Crys-JEPA, a joint embedding predictive architecture for crystals that learns an energy-aware latent space preserving formation-energy differences. In this space, stability assessment can be reformulated as an embedding-based comparison against accessible training crystals, reducing the reliance on expensive energy evaluation and task-specific external references. Building on Crys-JEPA, we further develop a screening-and-refinement pipeline that identifies promising generated crystals and reintroduces them to refine the generative model. On MP-20 and Alex-MP-20 datasets, we achieve improvements over baselines up to 81.4% and 82.6% on V.S.U.N metric, respectively.

</details>


### [180] [Composable Crystals: Controllable Materials Discovery via Concept Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14769)
*Nian Liu,Yuwei Zeng,Ryoji Kubo,Nikita Kazeev,Stephen Gregory Dale,Artem Maevskiy,Pengru Huang,Thomas Laurent,Kostya S. Novoselov,Xavier Bresson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于概念组合的晶体生成框架，通过向量量化变分自编码器自动发现可重用的晶体概念，实现可控且新颖的晶体生成，在MP-20和Alex-MP-20数据集上V.S.U.N指标提升最高达53.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有晶体生成方法主要依赖黑箱随机采样，对生成结构如何超越观测分布缺乏控制能力。

Method: 1) 训练VQ-VAE自动发现可重用的晶体概念（具有局部原子环境和全局对称模式的可解释性）；2) 引入组合生成器，利用模型自身产生的高质量样本迭代优化；3) 概念组合作为条件指导下游晶体生成。

Result: 在MP-20和Alex-MP-20数据集上，单独组合概念使基础模型的V.S.U.N指标分别提升53.2%和51.7%，尤其在新颖性方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 概念组合框架能有效实现晶体生成的可控探索，生成超越训练分布的新颖晶体，优于传统随机采样方法。

Abstract: De novo crystal generation, a central task in materials discovery, aims to generate crystals that are simultaneously valid, stable, unique, and novel. Existing methods mainly rely on black-box stochastic sampling, providing limited control over how generated structures move beyond the observed distribution. In this paper, we introduce a concept-based compositional framework for crystal generation. We train a vector-quantized variational autoencoder to automatically discover a shared set of reusable crystal concepts, which serve as building blocks for guided generation. These learned concepts naturally exhibit interpretability from both local atomic environments and global symmetry patterns, and generalize to crystals from different distributions. By recombining such concepts, our framework enables controllable exploration of novel crystals beyond the training distribution, rather than relying solely on unconstrained random sampling. To further improve composition efficiency, we introduce a composition generator and iteratively refine it using high-quality samples generated by the model itself. The resulting concept compositions are then used to condition downstream crystal generation. Numerical experiments on MP-20 and Alex-MP-20 show that compositing concepts separately increase base model up to 53.2% and 51.7% on V.S.U.N metric, with particular gains in novelty.

</details>


### [181] [Beyond What to Select: A Plug-and-play Oscillatory Data-Volume Scheduling for Efficient Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14773)
*Suorong Yang,Hanqi Zhu,Hai Gan,Fangjian Su,Guang Li,Furao Shen,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PODS框架，通过振荡式数据量调度策略来替代传统固定选择比例的数据选择方法，在低比例正则化阶段和高比例恢复阶段之间交替，从而在保持优化稳定性的同时利用选择诱导的正则化效应，显著提升训练效率与泛化性能的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据选择方法主要关注设计样本重要性标准（选什么），但通常在整个训练过程中固定选择数据量（目标比例），即在样本身份上是动态的，但在数据量上是静态的。作者从优化视角重新审视数据选择，发现选择比例会引入隐式正则化效应，低比例放大正则化但可能损失覆盖面和优化保真度，而高比例则相反，因此需要动态调度数据量来权衡这一矛盾。

Method: 提出PODS（Plug-and-play Oscillatory Data-volume Scheduling）框架，作为一个轻量级模块，在训练过程中动态调度选择多少数据。在目标选择比例下，PODS交替进行低比例正则化阶段（利用选择诱导的正则化）和高比例恢复阶段（保持数据覆盖和优化稳定性），无需引入新的样本评分指标，兼容现有静态和动态选择方法。

Result: 实验涵盖多种数据集、架构和任务：在ImageNet-1k上减少50%训练成本并提升准确率；在LLM指令微调中加速超过2倍且无性能下降。

Conclusion: PODS通过轻量级、比例级、任务无关的设计，有效改善了效率-泛化权衡，可广泛应用于不同训练范式，为数据选择领域提供了新的优化视角和实践工具。

Abstract: Data selection accelerates training by identifying representative training data while preserving model performance. However, existing methods mainly focus on designing sample-importance criteria, i.e., deciding what to select, while typically fixing the selected data volume as the target ratio throughout training. Thus, they are often dynamic in sample identity but static in data volume. In this work, we revisit data selection from an optimization perspective and show that selected-data training induces an implicit regularization effect modulated by the instantaneous selection ratio. This reveals a key trade-off: lower ratios amplify selection-induced regularization, whereas higher ratios preserve data coverage and optimization fidelity. Motivated by this insight, we propose PODS, a Plug-and-play Oscillatory Data-volume Scheduling framework. Rather than introducing another sample-scoring metric, PODS serves as a lightweight module that dynamically schedules how much data to select over training. Under the target selection ratio, PODS alternates between low-ratio regularization phases and high-ratio recovery phases to exploit selection-induced regularization without sacrificing optimization stability. With its lightweight, ratio-level, and task-agnostic design, PODS is compatible with existing static and dynamic selection methods and broadly applicable across training paradigms. Experiments across various datasets, architectures, and tasks show that PODS consistently improves the efficiency-generalization trade-off, e.g., reducing ImageNet-1k training cost by 50% with improved accuracy and accelerating LLM instruction tuning by over 2x without performance degradation.

</details>


### [182] [Peng's Q($λ$) for Conservative Value Estimation in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14779)
*Byeongchan Kim,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CPQL，一种模型无关的离线多步强化学习算法，通过自适应Peng's Q(λ)算子进行保守值估计，在离线RL中首次从理论和实验上证明了多步算子的有效性，并在D4RL基准测试中显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线RL方法通常使用单步Bellman算子进行保守值估计，存在过度悲观的问题，且在离线到在线微调阶段会出现性能下降。作者希望提出一种多步算子来实现更有效的保守值估计。

Method: CPQL算法采用Peng's Q(λ)算子替代Bellman算子进行保守值估计。PQL算子在离线RL中的不动点更接近行为策略的价值函数，从而自然引入隐式行为正则化。算法同时缓解了过度悲观的价值估计问题。

Result: 在D4RL基准测试中，CPQL一致且显著优于现有离线单步基线方法。预训练的Q函数可使在线PQL智能体避免微调初期的性能下降，并实现稳健的性能提升。

Conclusion: CPQL实现了不低于行为策略的性能，并提供了接近最优性能的保证，这是之前保守方法未能达到的里程碑。该方法还适用于离线到在线学习框架。

Abstract: We propose a model-free offline multi-step reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm, Conservative Peng's Q($λ$) (CPQL). Our algorithm adapts the Peng's Q($λ$) (PQL) operator for conservative value estimation as an alternative to the Bellman operator. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work in offline RL to theoretically and empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of conservative value estimation with a \textit{multi-step} operator by fully leveraging offline trajectories. The fixed point of the PQL operator in offline RL lies closer to the value function of the behavior policy, thereby naturally inducing implicit behavior regularization. CPQL simultaneously mitigates over-pessimistic value estimation, achieves performance greater than (or equal to) that of the behavior policy, and provides near-optimal performance guarantees -- a milestone that previous conservative approaches could not achieve. Extensive numerical experiments on the D4RL benchmark demonstrate that CPQL consistently and significantly outperforms existing offline single-step baselines. In addition to the contributions of CPQL in offline RL, our proposed method also contributes to the offline-to-online learning framework. Using the Q-function pre-trained by CPQL in offline settings enables the online PQL agent to avoid the performance drop typically observed at the start of fine-tuning and to attain robust performance improvements. Our code is available at https://github.com/oh-lab/CPQL.

</details>


### [183] [Understanding Imbalanced Forgetting in Rehearsal-Based Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14785)
*Alberto Tamajo,Srinandan Dasmahapatra,Rahman Attar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于回放的类增量学习中存在系统性且严重的非平衡遗忘现象，通过构建三个最后一层系数（捕捉梯度层面的干扰源）来预测不同类别的遗忘程度，其中自诱导干扰系数是最强预测因子，为缓解非平衡遗忘提供了新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管回放策略在类增量学习中能缓解灾难性遗忘，但近期研究表明，即使采用平衡回放分配，某些类别的遗忘程度仍显著高于其他类别。这种非平衡遗忘现象尚未被充分探索，理解其成因对设计更公平的增量学习算法至关重要。

Method: 从理论分析出发，构建了三个最后一层系数（自诱导干扰系数、新类干扰系数等），用于捕捉增量步骤中影响每个旧类别的梯度级干扰源。通过实验验证这些系数联合预测各类别遗忘排名的有效性，并通过控制实验检验系数间的因果关系。

Result: 1. 非平衡遗忘在基于回放的类增量学习中系统性地出现且程度严重。2. 构建的三个最后一层系数能可靠预测各旧类别在增量步骤结束时的遗忘排名。3. 自诱导干扰系数是最强的预测因子，且受新类干扰系数的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解回放策略下的非平衡遗忘提供了机制性解释（梯度级干扰），并指出减少类别间干扰源差异是缓解非平衡遗忘的可行方向。

Abstract: Neural networks suffer from catastrophic forgetting in class-incremental learning (CIL) settings. Rehearsal$\unicode{x2013}$replaying a subset of past samples$\unicode{x2013}$is a well-established mitigation strategy. However, recent results suggest that, despite balanced rehearsal allocation, some classes are forgotten substantially more than others. Despite its relevance, this imbalanced forgetting phenomenon remains underexplored. This work shows that imbalanced forgetting arises systematically and severely in rehearsal-based CIL and investigates it extensively. Specifically, we construct, from a principled analysis, three last-layer coefficients that capture different gradient-level sources of interference affecting each past class during an incremental step. We then demonstrate that, together, they reliably predict how past classes will rank in terms of forgetting at the end of that step. While predictive performance alone does not establish causality, these results support the interpretation of the coefficients as a plausible mechanistic account linking last-layer gradient-level interactions during training to class-level forgetting outcomes. Notably, one coefficient$\unicode{x2013}$capturing self-induced interference$\unicode{x2013}$emerges as the strongest predictor, with controlled experiments providing evidence consistent with this coefficient being influenced by the new-class interference coefficient. Overall, our findings provide valuable insights and suggest promising directions for mitigating imbalanced forgetting by reducing class-wise disparities in the identified sources of interference.

</details>


### [184] [GFMate: Empowering Graph Foundation Models with Test-time Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14809)
*Yan Jiang,Ruihong Qiu,Zi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GFMate，一种预训练无关的测试时图提示微调方法，通过引入质心提示和层提示，结合测试时互补学习目标，利用有标签和无标签目标域数据，在12个基准数据集上实现最高30.63%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图提示方法将源域信息嵌入提示中，导致提示与特定源域和模型预训练策略纠缠，泛化性受限；此外，现有方法仅依赖少样本微调，忽略了目标域测试数据中丰富的无标签信息。

Method: 提出GFMate，包含：（1）质心提示和层提示，在预训练后应用于目标域，避免与源域和模型预训练纠缠；（2）测试时互补学习目标，利用有标签和无标签目标域数据进行有效的测试时提示微调。

Result: 在12个基准数据集上验证了GFMate的优越性能和效率，性能提升最高达30.63%。

Conclusion: GFMate通过预训练无关的测试时图提示微调，有效提升了图基础模型在目标域的泛化能力，实现了显著的性能改进。

Abstract: Graph prompt tuning has shown great potential in graph learning by introducing trainable prompts to enhance the model performance in conventional single-domain scenarios. Recent research has extended graph prompts to improve Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) by few-shot tuning auxiliary prompts. Despite their progress, most existing methods embed source-domain information into prompts, which serve either as input to GFMs or encoded during model pre-training. Such prompt entanglement with specific source domains and GFM pre-training strategy restricts their generalisability to other domains and different GFMs. Furthermore, existing GFM prompts merely rely on few-shot tuning for adaptation, neglecting the rich information in unlabelled target domain test data. Motivated by these insights, this paper aims to empower GFMs with pre-training-agnostic test-time graph prompt tuning, named GFMate. GFMate introduces centroid and layer prompts applied after pre-training on target domains, avoiding entanglement with specific source domains and model pre-training. In addition, a test-time complementary learning objective is devised to exploit both labelled and unlabelled target domain data for effective test-time prompt tuning. Extensive experiments on 12 benchmark datasets demonstrate the superior performance and efficiency of GFMate, achieving improvements of up to 30.63%. Code is available at https://github.com/YanJiangJerry/GFMate.

</details>


### [185] [ToMAToMP: Robust and Multi-Parameter Topological Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14824)
*Ludo Andrianirina,Mathieu Carrière*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ToMAToMP，这是首个能够同时处理多个函数且具有理论保证的拓扑聚类方法。通过利用多参数持久同调中的MMA分解，该方法解决了传统ToMATo算法对图超参数敏感、易受异常值影响以及只能处理单一函数三大局限，并证明了其鲁棒性。实验表明，ToMAToMP在多个数据集上显著优于非拓扑和拓扑基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ToMATo算法虽有广泛应用，但存在三个关键局限：(1)需要用户提供数据点图作为超参数，调优困难；(2)对函数值域中的异常值高度敏感；(3)只能处理单一函数，而许多应用场景需要同时利用多个函数。因此，需要一种能同时处理多函数且具有理论保证的拓扑聚类方法。

Method: 利用多参数持久同调中的MMA分解（Morse-Milnor Approximation decomposition）来设计聚类算法，将多个函数的信息融合到统一的拓扑聚类框架中，从而实现对多个函数的同时处理，并保持理论上的鲁棒性保证。

Result: (1)证明了ToMAToMP具有鲁棒性性质；(2)作为推论，证明了该方法能使ToMATo摆脱对图调优的依赖并对异常值具有鲁棒性；(3)数值实验表明，ToMAToMP在多个数据集上的聚类质量相较于非拓扑和拓扑基线方法均有显著提升。

Conclusion: ToMAToMP是首个能同时处理多个函数且具有理论保证的拓扑聚类方法，有效克服了传统ToMATo的三大局限，在聚类质量和鲁棒性方面均表现优异。

Abstract: Topological clustering, and its main algorithm ToMATo, is a clustering method from Topological Data Analysis (TDA) which has been applied successfully in several applications during the last few years. This is due to its high versatility, as clusters are detected from the persistent components in the sublevel sets of any user-defined function (gene expression, pixel values, etc), and efficiency, as topological clustering enjoys robustness guarantees. However, ToMATo is also limited in several ways. First, a graph on the data points needs to be provided as a hyper-parameter of the method (whose fine-tuning is left to the user). Second, ToMATo is known to be very sensitive to outlier values in the function range. Finally, and most importantly, ToMATo can only handle one function at a time, whereas it is critical to use several functions in various applications. In this article, we introduce ToMAToMP: the first topological clustering method able to handle several functions at the same time with theoretical guarantees. More specifically, we leverage a recent tool from multi-parameter persistent homology, called MMA decomposition, to design our clustering algorithm, and prove that it enjoys robustness properties. As corollaries, we show that it can be used to make ToMATo independent of graph tuning, and robust to outliers. Finally, we provide a set of numerical experiments showcasing the efficiency and quality of the clusterings produced by ToMAToMP, by showing strong improvement over non-topological and topological baselines for various datasets.

</details>


### [186] [GenAI for Energy-Efficient and Interference-Aware Compressed Sensing of GNSS Signals on a Google Edge TPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14839)
*Thorben Wegner,Lucas Heublein,Tobias Feigl,Felix Ott,Christopher Mutschler,Alexander Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于生成式人工智能（变分自编码器VAE）的GNSS干扰信号压缩与分类方法，部署在Google Edge TPU上，通过8位量化实现硬件端实时压缩（>42倍）和干扰分类（F2分数0.915），在保持高分类精度的同时显著降低传输成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GNSS干扰分类方法依赖云端后处理原始或频谱数据流，数据传输复杂且成本高昂，无法满足功耗受限场景下的实时需求。亟需一种能在接收机硬件端直接压缩数据并实时分类干扰/欺骗攻击的高效方案。

Method: 利用变分自编码器（VAE）在Google Edge TPU上对GNSS信号进行压缩与重建；采用8位量化适配边缘设备；对比不同AE架构（含条件VAE和FactorVAE）；处理原始IQ数据、FFT数据及手工特征；通过消融实验研究潜在特征解耦。

Result: 系统实现>42倍压缩，对约72种干扰类型的分类F2分数达0.915（原始信号为0.923），显著降低干扰信号传输成本。

Conclusion: 硬件中心化的GenAI方法为GNSS干扰缓解提供了实用解决方案，潜在特征解耦增强了模型可解释性和在敏感干扰应用中的可信度。

Abstract: Traditional methods for classifying global navigation satellite system (GNSS) jamming signals typically involve post-processing raw or spectral data streams, requiring complex and costly data transmission to cloud-based interference classification systems. In contrast, our proposed approach efficiently compresses GNSS data streams directly at the hardware receiver while simultaneously classifying jamming and spoofing attacks in real time. Given the growing prevalence of GNSS jamming, there is a critical need for real-time solutions suitable for power-constrained environments. This paper introduces a novel method for compressing and classifying GNSS jamming threats using generative artificial intelligence (GenAI), specifically variational autoencoders (VAEs), deployed on Google Edge tensor processing units (TPUs). The study evaluates various autoencoder (AE) architectures to compress and reconstruct GNSS signals, focusing on preserving interference characteristics while minimizing data size near the receiver hardware. The pipeline adapts large-scale AE models for Google Edge TPUs through 8-bit quantization to ensure energy-efficient deployment. Tests on raw in-phase and quadrature-phase (IQ) data, Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) data, and handcrafted features show the system achieves significant compression (>42x) and accurate classification of approximately 72 interference types on reconstructed signals (F2-score 0.915), closely matching the original signals (F2-score 0.923). The hardware-centric GenAI approach also substantially reduces jammer signal transmission costs, offering a practical solution for interference mitigation. Ablation studies on conditional and factorized VAEs (i.e., FactorVAE) explore latent feature disentanglement for data generation, enhancing model interpretability and fostering trust in machine learning (ML) solutions for sensitive interference applications.

</details>


### [187] [In-Context Learning for Data-Driven Censored Inventory Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14840)
*Sohom Mukherjee,Anh-Duy Pham,Richard Pibernik,Yunbei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ICGPS的在线学习框架，结合离线元训练的生成模型与在线上下文自回归生成，用于解决带有决策依赖删失的库存控制问题。理论上建立了贝叶斯遗憾界，实证上通过ChronosFlow实例化，在多种场景下超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统参数化汤普森采样在先验不匹配时表现脆弱，离线插补方法无法直接迁移到在线学习场景。为弥合这一差距，受决策的预测性视角启发，将离线学习与在线决策结合。

Method: 提出ICGPS框架：离线元训练生成模型，在线通过上下文自回归生成后验样本，取oracle行动作用于学习到的潜在需求补全。理论上通过完成核刻画偏差，将贝叶斯遗憾分解为TS基准遗憾加部署惩罚项；对重复报童问题，将删失反馈降级为bandit凸优化反馈。实例化使用ChronosFlow（冻结时间序列Transformer骨干+可训练条件归一化流头）。

Result: 理论上得到亚线性贝叶斯遗憾界，离线预测质量可迁移至在线表现。实验中ChronosFlow-ICGPS匹配正确设定的TS，优于短视策略和UCB类基线，对先验不匹配和分布偏移鲁棒，在真实SuperStore数据集尤其高删失场景下表现良好。

Conclusion: ICGPS提供了一个通用即插即用模板，可将任何已知TS遗憾界的运筹问题转化为生成式后验采样问题，且离线预测质量保障在线性能。ChronosFlow-ICGPS验证了该框架在删失新闻vendor问题中的有效性。

Abstract: We study inventory control with decision-dependent censoring, focusing on the censored or repeated newsvendor (R-NV), where each order quantity determines whether demand is fully observed or censored by sales. Existing approaches based on parametric Thompson sampling (TS) can be brittle under prior mismatch, while offline imputation methods need not transfer to online learning. Motivated by the predictive view of decision making, we combine these ideas by taking oracle actions on learned completions of latent demand. We propose in-context generative posterior sampling (ICGPS), which uses modern generative models that are meta-trained offline and deployed online by in-context autoregressive generation. Theoretically, we show that the Bayesian regret of ICGPS with a learned completion kernel is bounded by the Bayesian regret of a TS benchmark with the ideal completion kernel plus a deployment penalty scaling as $\sqrt{T}$ times the square root of the completion mismatch. This yields a plug-in template for operational problems with known TS regret bounds. For R-NV, we derive sublinear Bayesian regret by reducing censored feedback to bandit convex optimization feedback. We also show that, under reasonable coverage and stability assumptions, the online completion mismatch is controlled by the offline censored predictive mismatch, so offline predictive quality transfers to online performance. Practically, we instantiate ICGPS with ChronosFlow, which combines a frozen time-series transformer backbone with a trainable conditional normalizing-flow head for fast censoring-consistent sampling. In benchmark experiments, ChronosFlow-ICGPS matches correctly specified TS, outperforms myopic and UCB-style baselines, and is robust to prior mismatch and distribution shift. ChronosFlow-ICGPS also performs well for the real-world SuperStore dataset, especially under heavy censoring.

</details>


### [188] [GPart: End-to-End Isometric Fine-Tuning via Global Parameter Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14841)
*Paolo Mandica,Michał Brzozowski,Zuzanna Dubanowska,Neo Christopher Chung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GPart提出了一种无需低秩瓶颈的参数高效微调方法，通过单个等距分割矩阵将可训练向量直接映射到完整权重空间，实现了端到端等距、极简的超参数配置和存储成本，在多项任务上达到或超越现有PEFT方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA的双线性结构破坏了映射的距离保持性，扭曲了优化景观；Uni-LoRA等方法虽提升了参数效率，但LoRA映射仍然破坏了端到端等距性。核心问题在于低秩瓶颈本身。

Method: 使用单个等距分割矩阵将d维可训练向量直接映射到模型完整权重空间，去除低秩矩阵结构约束，实现端到端等距映射。仅需一个超参数d，存储成本为d+1个值（可训练向量+随机种子）。

Result: 在自然语言理解、计算机视觉和数学推理任务上，GPart达到或超越了现有PEFT方法（如LoRA）的性能，同时实现了更高的参数效率和存储效率。

Conclusion: GPart通过移除结构性约束，证明了有效的微调可以从完整权重空间的随机低维子空间中涌现，无需低秩矩阵结构，为PEFT提供了一种简洁优雅的解决方案。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has become the dominant paradigm for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) of large language models (LLMs). However, its bilinear structure introduces a critical limitation: the mapping from trainable parameters to weight updates is not distance-preserving, distorting the optimization landscape. Methods that project a low-dimensional vector into LoRA's parameter space, such as Uni-LoRA, improve parameter efficiency, but the subsequent bilinear LoRA map breaks end-to-end isometry, leaving the core distance-preservation problem unresolved. We propose GPart (Global Partition fine-tuning), a highly parameter-efficient fine-tuning method which removes the low-rank bottleneck entirely. Our method uses a single isometric partition matrix to map a $d$-dimensional trainable vector directly into the full weight space of the model. The result is an extremely minimal fine-tuning pipeline: one random projection, end-to-end isometric, with a single clean hyperparameter ($d$) and storage cost of $d+1$ values (the trainable vector plus a random seed). GPart builds on the theoretical premise that effective fine-tuning can emerge from random low-dimensional subspaces of the full weight space, without imposing low-rank matrix structure. We empirically demonstrate the superior or comparable performance of GPart to existing PEFT methods on natural language understanding, computer vision tasks, and mathematical reasoning. Overall, GPart achieves state-of-the-art efficiency and performance by removing structural constraints, offering a straightforward and elegant path to PEFT.

</details>


### [189] [Exploitation of Hidden Context in Dynamic Movement Forecasting: A Neural Network Journey from Recurrent to Graph Neural Networks and General Purpose Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14855)
*Lukas Schelenz,Shobha Rajanna,Denis Gosalci,Lucas Heublein,Jonas Pirkl,Jonathan Ott,Felix Ott,Christopher Mutschler,Tobias Feigl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了多种轨迹预测模型在NBA球员运动预测中的表现，提出了一种结合上下文信息的混合LSTM架构，在最终位移误差（FDE）上达到1.51m，优于TCNN、GAT和Transformer等模型，同时所需数据和训练时间更少。研究指出，在快节奏动态环境中，没有单一架构在所有指标上均最优，需根据具体任务选择模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信号处理流水线中的预测对于缓解延迟至关重要，尤其是在预测NBA球员等动态物体运动时。由于体育运动的交互性和不可预测性（速度和方向突变），传统模型（ARIMA、卡尔曼滤波、粒子滤波）难以建模非线性动态，而现有ML方法（LSTM、GNN、Transformer）虽更灵活，却未显式捕捉时间依赖与上下文交互之间的协同关系，这在混乱的体育环境中至关重要。

Method: 系统评估了多种模型：线性模型（ARIMA类、卡尔曼滤波、粒子滤波）与ML方法（LSTM、TCNN、GAT、Transformer），并提出了一种混合LSTM架构，通过引入上下文信息增强预测能力。实验在NBA球员轨迹数据上进行，对比不同输入历史长度、泛化能力和上下文信息利用程度下的性能。

Result: ML方法在长达2秒的预测范围内显著优于线性模型。混合LSTM（含上下文信息）取得最低FDE（1.51m），优于TCNN、GAT和Transformer，且所需数据和训练时间更少。但无单一架构在所有指标上全面占优。

Conclusion: 在快节奏动态环境（如NBA比赛）的轨迹预测中，需根据具体任务需求选择模型。混合LSTM在精度与效率之间取得了良好平衡，但不同架构各有优劣，强调任务特异性设计的重要性。

Abstract: Forecasting within signal processing pipelines is crucial for mitigating delays, particularly in predicting the dynamic movements of objects such as NBA players. This task poses significant challenges due to the inherently interactive and unpredictable nature of sports, where abrupt changes in velocity and direction are prevalent. Traditional approaches, including (S)ARIMA(X), Kalman filters (KF), and Particle filters (PF), often struggle to model the non-linear dynamics present in such scenarios. Machine learning (ML) methods, such as long short-term memory (LSTM) networks, graph neural networks (GNNs), and Transformers, offer greater flexibility and accuracy but frequently fail to explicitly capture the interplay between temporal dependencies and contextual interactions, which are critical in chaotic sports environments. In this paper, we evaluate these models and assess their strengths and weaknesses. Experimental results reveal key performance trade-offs across input history length, generalizability, and the ability to incorporate contextual information. ML-based methods demonstrated substantial improvements over linear models across forecast horizons of up to 2s. Among the tested architectures, our hybrid LSTM augmented with contextual information achieved the lowest final displacement error (FDE) of 1.51m, outperforming temporal convolutional neural network (TCNN), graph attention network (GAT), and Transformers, while also requiring less data and training time compared to GAT and Transformers. Our findings indicate that no single architecture excels across all metrics, emphasizing the need for task-specific considerations in trajectory prediction for fast-paced, dynamic environments such as NBA gameplay.

</details>


### [190] [REALM: Retrospective Encoder Alignment for LFP Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14867)
*Peicheng Wu,Zhenyu Bu,Runze Ma,Lin Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出REALM，一种回顾式蒸馏框架，通过将预训练的双向Mamba-2 LFP教师模型的知识蒸馏到因果学生模型中，实现了仅基于LFP信号的因果式行为解码。该方法在参数量减少2倍、训练时间减少10倍的情况下，优于现有因果和非因果LFP解码方法，为下一代无线植入式BCI提供了实用且可扩展的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尖峰信号虽具有高时空分辨率，但其高采样频率在高通道数和无线BCI场景下导致高功耗和带宽瓶颈。LFP信号虽具有长期稳定性好、能耗低和带宽要求低等优势，但其解码精度通常不如尖峰信号，且现有方法多依赖非因果架构，无法用于实时部署。因此，本文旨在弥合离线与实时神经解码之间的差距，实现高精度、因果性的LFP解码。

Method: 采用回顾式离线到在线蒸馏策略：首先使用掩码自编码目标预训练一个双向Mamba-2教师模型；然后通过表示对齐和任务监督的联合目标，将教师模型的知识蒸馏到一个紧凑的因果学生模型中，实现实时部署。

Result: REALM在行为解码任务上一致优于现有因果和非因果LFP-based SOTA方法；同时实现了2倍参数量减少和10倍训练时间缩减。

Conclusion: 回顾式蒸馏有效弥合了离线与实时神经解码之间的差距。仅基于LFP的模型可以在不依赖尖峰信号的情况下达到有竞争力的解码性能，为下一代无线植入式BCI提供了实用且可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: Spike activity has been the dominant neural signal for behavior decoding due to its high spatial and temporal resolution. However, as brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) move toward high channel counts and wireless operation, the high sampling frequency of spike signals becomes a bottleneck due to high power and bandwidth requirements. Local field potentials (LFPs) represent a different spatial-temporal scale of brain activity compared to spikes, offering key advantages including improved long-term stability, reduced energy consumption, and lower bandwidth requirement. Despite these benefits, LFP-based decoding models typically show reduced accuracy and often rely on non-causal architectures that are unsuitable for real-time deployment. To address these challenges, we propose REALM: a retrospective distillation framework that enables causal LFP decoding. Inspired by offline-to-online distillation strategies in speech recognition, REALM transfers representational knowledge from a pretrained multi-session bidirectional LFP model to a causal version for real-time deployment. We first pretrain a bidirectional Mamba-2 teacher model using a masked autoencoding objective. We then distill this teacher model into a compact student model via a combined objective of representation alignment and task supervision. REALM consistently outperforms both causal and non-causal LFP-based SOTA methods for behavior decoding. Notably, our REALM improves decoding performance while achieving a $2\times$ reduction in parameter count and a $10\times$ reduction in training time. These results demonstrate that retrospective distillation effectively bridges the gap between offline and real-time neural decoding. REALM shows that LFP-only models can achieve competitive decoding performance without reliance on spike signals, offering a practical and scalable alternative for next-generation wireless implantable BCIs.

</details>


### [191] [TILBench: A Systematic Benchmark for Tabular Imbalanced Learning Across Data Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14915)
*Ruizhe Liu,Jiaqi Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TILBench，一个大规模表格数据不平衡学习基准，评估了40多种算法在57个数据集上的表现，发现没有单一方法在所有场景中占优，方法的选择需依据数据特征和计算约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管不平衡学习已有数十年研究和大量算法，但对不同方法在不同数据特征下的预测性能、鲁棒性和计算可扩展性缺乏系统性的实证理解。

Method: 构建TILBench基准，包含57个多样化表格数据集，评估40多种代表性算法，在超过200,000次受控实验中系统比较不同方法族的表现。

Result: 没有任何单一方法在所有设置中一致占优；不平衡学习方法的有效性强烈依赖于数据集特征和计算约束。

Conclusion: 基于发现提供了实际应用中选择合适方法的实用建议。

Abstract: Imbalanced learning remains a fundamental challenge in tabular data applications. Despite decades of research and numerous proposed algorithms, a systematic empirical understanding of how different imbalanced learning methods behave across diverse data characteristics is still lacking. In particular, it remains unclear how different method families compare in predictive performance, robustness under varying data characteristics, and computational scalability. In this work, we present Tabular Imbalanced Learning Benchmark (TILBench), a large-scale empirical benchmark for tabular imbalanced learning. TILBench evaluates more than 40 representative algorithms across 57 diverse tabular datasets, resulting in over 200000 controlled experiments across a wide range of data characteristics. Our findings show that no single method consistently dominates across all settings; instead, the effectiveness of imbalanced learning methods depends strongly on dataset characteristics and computational constraints. Based on these findings, we provide practical recommendations for selecting appropriate methods in real-world applications.

</details>


### [192] [A Hardware-Aware, Per-Layer Methodology for Post-Training Quantization of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14929)
*Earl Killian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向大语言模型权重的后训练量化方法SOP，结合分层搜索的固定与动态码本、符号逐块缩放、激活加权余弦选择和多选择背包策略，在4.5-6比特每权重实现近无损保真度，并提出了硬件高效的查找表输出格式HIF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型部署中，权重量化是实现模型高效推理的关键技术。传统FP8格式在更低比特率下精度损失较大，亟需一种能在4.5-6比特范围内实现近无损保真度的量化方法，同时兼顾硬件部署的效率和成本。

Method: SOP方法包含以下关键技术：(1) 分层搜索固定码本(NF4, BOF4, Split87, SH4)与动态码本(DD4)，由逐块选择位决定；(2) 符号化逐块缩放；(3) 激活加权余弦选择；(4) 多选择背包策略提升敏感层的精度；(5) 离群值与稀疏残差校正；(6) 提出硬件高效的查找表输出格式HIF。

Result: 在6个开放模型家族上，推荐的FP6工作点(E2M3sUE4M4, 6.5 bpw)在1.5 bpw更低的存储成本下，权重重建误差低于传统逐层POT FP8基线(E4M3, 8.0 bpw)。

Conclusion: 实验表明，采用精心选择精度的逐块缩放小原子可以替代传统部署的FP8格式，在更低比特率下实现更优的权重重建质量。SOP方法在4.5-6 bpw范围内具有显著的精度-效率优势。

Abstract: Scaled Outer Product (SOP) is a post-training quantization methodology for large language model weights, designed to deliver near-lossless fidelity at 4.5--6 bits per weight on hardware with per-layer LUT decode. The methodology combines per-layer search of fixed and dynamic codebook pairs selected by a per-block selection bit, signed per-block scales, activation-weighted cosine selection, and multiple-choice knapsack promotion of sensitive layers with outlier and sparse-residual correction. Fixed codebooks include NF4, BOF4, Split87, and SH4; per-layer optimized codebooks (DD4) are hosted in LUT SRAM. A new hardware-efficient LUT output format (HIF) is proposed to improve performance, energy, and cost. Across six open model families, the recommended FP6 operating point (E2M3sUE4M4, 6.5 bpw) achieves lower weight reconstruction error than the conventional per-layer-POT FP8 baseline (E4M3, 8.0 bpw) at 1.5 bpw lower storage cost, demonstrating that block-scaled small atoms with carefully chosen scale precision can replace conventionally-deployed FP8. Full evaluation across the 4.5--6 bpw range, including layer promotion and sparse residual correction, is reported in a companion paper.

</details>


### [193] [Octopus: History-Free Gradient Orthogonalization for Continual Learning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14938)
*Yuehao Liu,Shanyan Guan,Weijia Zhang,Xuanming Shang,Yanhao Ge,Wei Li,Chao Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Octopus框架，基于无历史数据的梯度正交化（HiFGO）和两阶段微调策略，在多模态大语言模型的持续学习中实现SOTA性能，平均性能提升2.14%，最后任务性能提升6.82%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM持续学习方法存在局限性：架构类方法计算开销大且泛化差；回放类方法依赖历史数据，存在隐私和存储问题；传统正则化方法难以完全防止参数干扰。

Method: 提出Octopus两阶段持续学习框架：1）无历史数据梯度正交化（HiFGO），在梯度层面强制正交性；2）两阶段微调策略，将任务适应与正则化解耦，平衡可塑性与稳定性。

Result: 在UCIT基准上，Avg指标超越SOTA 2.14%，Last指标超越SOTA 6.82%。

Conclusion: HiFGO结合两阶段微调策略能有效解决持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，无需历史数据即可实现参数干扰的缓解，在性能上显著超越现有方法。

Abstract: Continual learning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) aims to sequentially acquire knowledge while mitigating catastrophic forgetting, yet existing methods face inherent limitations: architecture-based approaches incur additional computational overhead and often generalize poorly to new tasks, rehearsal-based methods rely on storing historical data, raising privacy and storage concerns, and conventional regularization-based strategies alone are insufficient to fully prevent parameter interference. We propose Octopus, a two-stage continual learning framework based on History-Free Gradient Orthogonalization (HiFGO), which enforces gradient-level orthogonality without historical task data. Our proposed two-stage finetuning strategy decouples task adaptation from regularization, achieving a principled balance between plasticity and stability. Experiments on UCIT show that Octopus establishes state-of-the-art performance, surpassing prior SOTA by 2.14% and 6.82% in terms of Avg and Last.

</details>


### [194] [Not All Symbols Are Equal: Importance-Aware Constellation Design for Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14940)
*Albert Shaju,Christo Kurisummoottil Thomas,Mayukh Roy Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种联合语义-物理层框架，通过向量量化变分自编码器提取离散潜在概念、语义关键性指标（SCI）评估任务相关性、深度强化学习动态选择传输子集，以及语义感知M-QAM星座映射，实现了接近100%的语义保护概率（对比传统星座的50%），并在MNIST等数据集上达到21:1压缩比且语义质量高于0.9。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义通信系统将星座设计与语义编码解耦，导致关键符号与无关符号暴露于相同速率的信道错误中，缺乏对任务相关信息在物理层的差异化保护。

Method: 提出联合语义-物理层框架，包含：1) 向量量化变分自编码器提取离散潜在概念；2) 语义关键性指标（SCI）评分；3) 深度强化学习动态选择传输子集；4) 语义感知M-QAM星座（基于联合共现统计与SCI分数分配符号位置）；5) 提出语义符号脆弱性（SSV）与语义保护概率（SPP）指标。

Result: 1) 所提星座在4-QAM到1024-QAM范围内实现接近100%的SPP，对比标准星座在高频谱效率下仅50%；2) 实现21:1压缩比，语义质量高于0.9；3) 在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、FSDD上无需修改即可泛化。

Conclusion: 证明任何Gray编码星座在SCI加权SSV下严格次优（当源具有非均匀语义重要性和共现统计时）；所提方法有效实现了基于任务关键性的差异化物理层保护。

Abstract: Semantic communication systems for goal-oriented transmission must protect task-relevant information not only through source compression but also via physical layer mapping. Existing approaches decouple constellation design and semantic encoding, exposing critical symbols to channel errors at the same rate as irrelevant ones. Contrary to this, in this paper, a joint semantic-physical layer framework is proposed, which is composed of a vector quantized-variational autoencoder that extracts discrete latent concepts, a semantic criticality indicator (SCI) that scores each concept by task relevance, and a deep reinforcement learning agent that dynamically selects the transmission subset based on instantaneous channel conditions. At the physical layer, a learned semantic-aware M -QAM constellation assigns symbol positions according to joint co-occurrence statistics and SCI scores, departing from the uniform spacing and Gray coding of standard M -QAM which minimizes average BER without regard for semantic content. We introduce a novel semantic symbol vulnerability (SSV) metric and a semantic protection probability (SPP) to quantify the exposure of task-critical symbols to decoding errors, and prove that any Gray-coded constellation is strictly suboptimal in SCI-Weighted SSV whenever the source exhibits non-uniform semantic importance and co-occurrence statistics. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed constellation achieves near 100% SPP across modulation orders from 4-QAM to 1024-QAM versus 50% for standard constellations at high spectral efficiency, a 21:1 compression ratio with semantic quality above 0.9, generalizing across MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and FSDD without modification.

</details>


### [195] [Efficient Online Conformal Selection with Limited Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14953)
*Sreenivas Gollapudi,Kostas Kollias,Kamesh Munagala,Ali Sinop*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带限反馈（bandit feedback）下的符合性选择问题，证明自适应符合性推断（ACI）更新规则在应用于适当控制参数时，既能保证对抗性有效性（平均满足成功目标），又能实现随机效率（对i.i.d.输入达到次线性效率遗憾），通过李雅普诺夫函数统一分析框架处理bandit和semi-bandit反馈，建立限反馈在线学习与无分布不确定性量化之间的理论桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线符合性预测主要关注观测序列的有效性维持，但在有限反馈下最小化资源成本（效率）仍然是一个重大挑战。本文旨在解决这一难题。

Method: 提出将自适应符合性推断（ACI）更新规则应用于适当的控制参数或对偶变量，通过李雅普诺夫函数的统一分析框架，处理bandit和semi-bandit反馈的规范模型。

Result: ACI规则在对抗性设定下保证平均有效性（对任意输入序列均满足成功目标，包括分布偏移情形），在随机设定下对i.i.d.输入实现次线性效率遗憾。相比以往工作，本文方法需要显著更少的反馈，并能处理更复杂的设定。

Conclusion: 该工作为限反馈下的高效在线学习与无分布不确定性量化之间建立了新的理论桥梁。

Abstract: We address the problem of conformal selection, where an agent must select a minimal subset of options to ensure that at least one ``success'' is identified with a pre-specified target probability $φ$. While traditional online conformal prediction focuses on maintaining validity for the observed sequence, minimizing the resource cost (efficiency) of such selections, especially under limited feedback, remains a significant challenge. In this work, we consider settings with the most limited ``bandit'' feedback, and demonstrate that the simple Adaptive Conformal Inference (ACI) update rule, when applied to the appropriate control parameter or dual variable, is both adversarially valid, ensuring the success target is met on average for any input sequence (and hence under distribution shifts), and stochastically efficient, achieving sublinear efficiency regret for $i.i.d.$ inputs against an appropriate stochastic benchmark. We show such guarantees under canonical models capturing bandit and semi-bandit feedback to the agent via a unifying algorithmic technique, and analytic framework involving Lyapunov functions. Our approach handles more complex settings than prior work, while requiring significantly less feedback, and our results provide a new theoretical bridge between efficient online learning with limited feedback and distribution-free uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [196] [InfoSFT: Learn More and Forget Less with Information-Aware Token Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14967)
*Mahdi Sabbaghi,George Pappas,Adel Javanmard,Hamed Hassani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: InfoSFT提出了一种基于信息论加权的监督微调方法，通过聚焦中等置信度token来替代传统SFT对所有样本均匀拟合的方式，从而在保持预训练能力的同时提升在数学、代码和思维链任务上的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SFT对所有样本（包括基模型似然低的样本）均匀拟合，导致训练更新过度偏向特定样本而非学习目标行为，并且引入显著策略漂移，损害已有能力。现有方法通过过滤、重生成或降权重低似然数据，却抑制了基模型尚未学习的新行为。

Method: 提出InfoSFT，对SFT目标函数进行原则性的加权方案，将学习信号集中在最大信息量的中等置信度token上（既非基模型过于熟悉，也非过于不可能导致不稳定），仅需对标准token级损失进行一行修改。

Result: 在数学、代码和思维链任务上，使用多种模型族，InfoSFT在泛化性能上显著优于标准SFT和基于似然加权的基线方法，同时更好地保留了预训练已有能力。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦信息量最大、置信度适中的token，InfoSFT能够在不牺牲已有能力的前提下更有效地学习新行为，为SFT提供了一种简单有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) provides the standard approach for teaching LLMs new behaviors from offline expert demonstrations. However, standard SFT uniformly fits all samples -- including those with low likelihood under the base model -- which can disproportionately drive training updates toward overfitting specific samples rather than learning the target behavior. Moreover, adapting to these unlikely samples induces substantial policy shifts that degrade prior capabilities. Existing methods mitigate this by filtering, regenerating, or down-weighting low-likelihood data. In doing so, they often suppress precisely the novel behaviors the base model has yet to learn.
  We propose InfoSFT, a principled weighting scheme for the SFT objective that concentrates learning signals on maximally informative, medium-confidence tokens -- those neither overly familiar to the base model nor too unlikely to cause instability. Requiring only a one-line modification to the standard token-wise loss, InfoSFT demonstrably improves generalization over vanilla SFT and likelihood-weighted baselines across math, code, and chain-of-thought tasks with diverse model families, while better preserving pre-existing capabilities.

</details>


### [197] [Second-Order Actor-Critic Methods for Discounted MDPs via Policy Hessian Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14982)
*Sanjeev Manivannan,Shuban V*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在折扣奖励强化学习中的二阶演员-评论家方法，通过双时间尺度框架证明了对动作价值函数局部常数的合理近似，并利用Hessian-向量积（HVP）计算实现了计算高效且稳定的二阶策略更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略梯度方法中的一阶更新收敛较慢，而二阶优化虽能加速收敛但Hessian估计的计算复杂度过高，限制了其在强化学习中的应用。本文旨在克服这一矛盾，设计计算可行的二阶演员更新方法。

Method: 提出双时间尺度演员-评论家框架，其中评论家在更快速时间尺度上演化，使得演员更新时可将其视为准平稳过程；在此框架下，动作价值函数相对于策略参数近似局部常数；利用Hessian-向量积（HVP）计算实现计算高效且稳定的二阶更新。

Result: 证明了所提出的二阶近似在双时间尺度框架下是合理且稳定的，并基于HVP实现了计算高效的第二阶演员-评论家方法。

Conclusion: 本文为折扣奖励RL提供了首个在双时间尺度下理论上合理的二阶演员-评论家方法，通过HVP技术克服了传统二阶方法的计算瓶颈，同时保持了曲率感知更新的加速收敛优势。

Abstract: We address the discounted reward setting in reinforcement learning (RL). To mitigate the value approximation challenges in policy gradient methods, actor-critic approaches have been developed and are known to converge to stationary points under suitable assumptions. However, these methods rely on first-order updates. In contrast, second-order optimization provides principled curvature-aware updates that are proven to accelerate convergence, but its application in RL is limited by the computational complexity of Hessian estimation. In this work, we analyze second-order approximations for the actor update that leverage the full curvature information of the objective as much as possible. A stable approximation requires treating the action-value function as locally constant with respect to policy parameters, which does not generally hold in policy gradient methods. We show that this approximation becomes well-justified under a two-timescale actor-critic framework, where the critic evolves on a faster timescale and can be treated as quasi-stationary during actor updates. Building on this insight, we formulate a second-order actor-critic method for the discounted reward setting that leverages Hessian-vector product (HVP) computations, resulting in a computationally efficient and stable second-order update.

</details>


### [198] [DeepTokenEEG Enhancing Mild Cognitive Impairment and Alzheimers Classification via Tokenized EEG Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15009)
*Thinh Nguyen-Quang,Minh Long Ngo,Ngoc-Son Nguyen,Nguyen Thanh Vinh,Huy-Dung Han,Bui Thanh Tung,Nguyen Quang Linh,Khuong Vo,Manoj Vishwanath,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级高性能模型DeepTokenEEG，利用空间和时间分词器从EEG信号中捕获AD生物标志物，仅需0.29M参数，在274名受试者数据集上达到100%的最高准确率，比现有最优方法提升1.41-15.35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD的早期检测对改善患者预后至关重要，基于EEG的诊断虽具有无创、低成本等优势，但面临数据可用性有限、深度学习方法准确性不足以及专家解读耗时等挑战。

Method: 设计了一种轻量级模型DeepTokenEEG，利用空间和时间分词器分别在时域和频域捕获AD相关生物标志物，模型参数量仅0.29M。

Result: 在包含180例AD和94例健康对照的274名受试者联合数据集上，在特定频段达到100%的最高准确率，比现有最优方法提升1.41-15.35%。

Conclusion: DeepTokenEEG在AD早期检测和筛查中具有潜力，且因其轻量特性具备良好的部署可行性。

Abstract: The detection of Alzheimers disease (AD) is considered crucial, as timely intervention can improve patient outcomes. Electroencephalogram (EEG)-based diagnosis has been recognized as a non-invasive, accessible, and cost-effective approach for AD detection; however, it faces challenges related to data availability, accuracy of modern deep learning methods, and the time-consuming nature of expert-based interpretation. In this study, a novel lightweight and high-performance model, DeepTokenEEG, was designed for the diagnosis of AD and the classification of EEG signals from AD patients, individuals with other neurological conditions, and healthy subjects. Unlike traditional heavy-weight models, DeepTokenEEG ultilizes spatial and temporal tokenizer that effectively captures AD-related biomarkers in both temporal and frequency domain with only 0.29 million paramaters. Trained in a combined dataset of 274 subjects, including 180 AD cases, and 94 healthy controls, the proposed method achieves a maximum recorded accuracy of 100% on specific frequency bands, representing an improvement of 1.41-15.35% over state-of-the-art methods on the same dataset. These results indicate the potential of DeepTokenEEG for early detection and screening of AD, with promising applicability for deployment due to its compact size.

</details>


### [199] [Boosting Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards via Randomly Selected Few-Shot Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15012)
*Kai Yan,Alexander G. Schwing,Yu-Xiong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FEST算法，仅需128条演示数据即可通过少量监督微调信号引导强化学习，有效提升RLVR的样本效率，在多个基准上达到甚至超越全量数据训练的基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR在困难问题上样本效率低，正确rollout难以生成；现有方案通过演示引导RLVR（即SFT+RL）但SFT需要大量数据，成本高昂。本文旨在用极少量的演示数据实现高效的RLVR。

Method: 提出FEST算法，核心包含三个关键组件：监督信号（few-shot SFT）、在轨策略信号（on-policy RL）、以及衰减权重机制（decaying weights）来防止多轮训练中的过拟合。

Result: 仅用128条随机选取的演示数据，FEST在多个基准上超越使用大量SFT数据的基线方法，甚至匹配使用完整数据集的性能。

Conclusion: 少量高质量的演示数据配合合理的训练信号设计（监督+在轨+衰减权重）可以显著提升RLVR的样本效率，为降低数据成本提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has achieved great success in developing Large Language Models (LLMs) with chain-of-thought rollouts for many tasks such as math and coding. Nevertheless, RLVR struggles with sample efficiency on difficult problems where correct rollouts are hard to generate. Prior works propose to address this issue via demonstration-guided RLVR, i.e., to conduct Supervised FineTuning (SFT) when RL fails; however, SFT often requires a lot of data, which can be expensive to acquire. In this paper, we propose FEST, a FEw-ShoT demonstration-guided RLVR algorithm. It attains compelling results with only 128 demonstrations randomly selected from an SFT dataset. We find that three components are vital for the success: supervised signal, on-policy signal, and decaying weights on the few-shot SFT dataset to prevent overfitting from multiple-epoch training. On several benchmarks, FEST outperforms baselines with magnitudes less SFT data, even matching their performance with full dataset.

</details>


### [200] [Generalized Priority-Aware Shapley Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15018)
*Kiljae Lee,Ziqi Liu,Weijing Tang,Yuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了广义优先级感知Shapley值（GPASV），一种定义在任意有向加权优先图上的随机序值，能够处理现有方法无法应对的循环优先关系，并通过公理化表征、计算方法和优先级扫描诊断工具进行全面构建，最后在Chatbot Arena循环偏好图上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Shapley值及其优先级感知扩展要求成对优先级必须是二元的且无环的，这一限制在实际数据（如聚合的人类偏好和多标准比较）中被严重违反，因此需要一种能够处理任意有向加权优先图（允许循环）的广义方法。

Method: 定义了在任意有向加权优先图上的随机序值GPASV，其中成对边通过惩罚而非禁止顺序违规来发挥作用；通过公理化表征建立GPASV，开发了相应的计算方法，并引入了扩展PASV优先级扫描诊断的工具。

Result: GPASV覆盖了一系列经典模型作为边界情形；在Chatbot Arena循环偏好图上对LLM集成估值进行了应用，结果表明优先级感知估值并非单一操作：成对图优先级与个体软优先级的不同平衡会对相同数据产生实质不同的估值。

Conclusion: 优先级感知估值不是一个一键式操作，需要在成对图优先级和个体软优先级之间进行权衡；GPASV为处理实际中普遍存在的循环优先关系提供了理论框架和计算工具。

Abstract: Shapley value and its priority-aware extensions are widely used for valuation in machine learning, but existing methods require pairwise priority to be binary and acyclic, a restriction spectacularly violated in real-data examples such as aggregated human preferences and multi-criterion comparisons. We introduce the generalized priority-aware Shapley value (GPASV), a random order value defined on arbitrary directed weighted priority graphs, in which pairwise edges penalize rather than forbid order violations. GPASV covers a range of classical models as boundary cases. We establish GPASV through an axiomatic characterization, develop the associated computational methods, and introduce a priority sweeping diagnostic extending PASV's. We apply GPASV to LLM ensemble valuation on the cyclic Chatbot Arena preference graph, illustrating that priority-aware valuation is not a one-button operation: different balances of pairwise graph priority versus individual soft priority produce substantively different valuations of the same data.

</details>


### [201] [Separating Intrinsic Ambiguity from Estimation Uncertainty in Deep Generative Models for Linear Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15050)
*Yuxin Guo,Dongrui Deng,Pulkit Grover*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将后验不确定性分解为内在模糊性和传播不确定性的结构性分解方法，通过级联公式实现校准分析，可揭示仅靠重建质量无法发现的模型失效模式，并在高斯示例、加速MRI和EEG源成像中验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成模型在逆问题后验推断中广泛应用（如医学成像、科学发现），但后验不确定性难以解释，因为它混合了前向算子固有的模糊性和通过推断传播的不确定性，现有方法仅靠重建质量选择模型会隐藏失效模式。

Method: 提出一种后验不确定性的结构性分解方法，将不确定性分解为内在模糊性（intrinsic ambiguity）和传播不确定性；采用级联公式（cascade formulation）使内在模糊性可用于校准分析，支持定性诊断和基于仿真的校准测试。

Result: 在具有解析后验结构的高斯示例上验证了该方法；在加速MRI上展示了分解效果；在EEG源成像上应用校准诊断，成功揭示了仅靠重建质量无法发现的失效模式。

Conclusion: 所提出的后验不确定性分解与校准诊断方法能够有效识别逆问题推断中的模型失效模式，为高可靠性应用场景下的模型评估提供了超越传统重建质量指标的补充工具。

Abstract: Recently, deep generative models have been used for posterior inference in inverse problems, including high-stakes applications in medical imaging and scientific discovery, where the uncertainty of a prediction can matter as much as the prediction itself. However, posterior uncertainty is difficult to interpret because it can mix ambiguity inherent to the forward operator with uncertainty propagated through inference. We introduce a structural decomposition of posterior uncertainty that isolates intrinsic ambiguity. A cascade formulation makes this ambiguity accessible for calibration analysis, enabling qualitative diagnostics and simulation-based calibration tests that reveal failure modes that remain hidden when models are selected by reconstruction quality alone. We first validate the approach on a Gaussian example with analytical posterior structure, then illustrate the decomposition on accelerated magnetic resonance imaging (MRI), and finally apply the calibration diagnostics to electroencephalography (EEG) source imaging.

</details>


### [202] [An Interpretable Latency Model for Speculative Decoding in LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15051)
*Linghao Kong,Megan Flynn,Michael Peng,Nir Shavit,Mark Kurtz,Alexandre Marques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个简单可解释的推测解码（SD）延迟模型，用于分析LLM服务系统中SD的行为。该模型利用Little定律从请求率推断有效批处理大小，将每个请求的延迟分解为负载无关和负载相关两部分，并在vLLM系统上进行了广泛验证。模型准确描述了延迟变化，解释了为什么SD加速比常随服务器负载增加而降低，并刻画了草稿长度、接受率、验证器-草稿模型大小在不同负载条件下对延迟的影响，还扩展到混合专家模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有工作证明了推测解码（SD）在隔离或固定批处理设置下的加速效果，但在实际生产服务系统中，请求负载随时间变化，有效的批处理大小由服务系统动态决定而非直接控制，因此SD在真实LLM服务系统中的行为尚不清晰。
